## §2. Existing architecture that must not change

- **Events** INSERT-only with triggers (F §4.1, D52); every command through 00's registry (`defineCommand`, `approvable`, fixed processing order, `processed_commands`, D32); one command = one transaction; commands over HTTP only.
- **Projections** are events-only read models registered with 00's registry (`register`, `reset`); a rebuild from seq 0 equals live (02a T19, 04 V16). Hub state that is not a projection (print jobs, worker columns) follows 03c's rule: plain statements, never events, never synced.
- **02b** owns order lines, `billed_minor`, comps (`LineComped`, `comp` kind), `OrdersBilled` (schema `{ order_ids, bill_id }`, stream `order`, id = first order id), `billed_at` and `ORDER_BILLED`; **02a** owns seatings, `seating_orders`, `RequestBill` / `BillRequested`, the NEEDS_CLEANING fold on `BillClosed { seating_id }` (stream `table`, `.strict()`); **04** owns voids, `pendingVoids`, the void day-close guard and the audit projection / `AUDIT_ACTIONS`; **03c** owns printers, `print_jobs`, the worker and ESC/POS; **03a** owns `stockEffects` before `BusinessDayClosed` and the day-close checkpoint; **01d** owns the request record, steps, answers, PIN, `approval_receipt`, read-only `apply`; **01b** owns permissions, grants, `default_key`, the branch settings endpoint; **01a** owns the business day commands and events, tax classes and the config snapshot.
- **Money** is integer minor units (`_minor`, D47); IDs are ULIDs; time is `branchNow` (F §4.7).
- **Day close** keeps 01a's command and events, the 04 void guard (`409 DAY_CLOSE_BLOCKED`) and 03a's `stockEffects` before `BusinessDayClosed` as the checkpoint event (03a To 05).
- **Single-slot hooks** (`registerDayCloseGuard`, `registerMergeGuard`, `registerLineGuard`) become ordered lists (rows A-3, T-1, Q-3); 04's registrations keep their place and behaviour.

---

## §3. Domain rules

### 3.1 Words

**Bill** = what one seating (dine-in) or one `bill_key` (takeaway root order with its additions; parking plate) owes ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-25). **Split** = one part of a bill paid on its own (`split_no` 1..n). **Line base** = a line's `billed_minor` (02b) minus its `pendingVoids` `refund_minor` (04); a `deal_part` and a return line have base 0; a pending-weight line has base 0 and blocks close. **Payment class** = `cash` | `digital`, from the payment mode (data). **Default class** = `cash` (the class a check bill and an unpaid split show). **Net** = Σ line bases − discount. **Invoice** = the immutable money record of one closed split (`series: 'sale'`) or of a credit (`series: 'credit'`). **Drawer** = a cashier device whose open shift was opened with `as_drawer: true`. **Fiscalized** = the bill's order type has `fiscalize: true` and the branch's adapter (its jurisdiction's `adapter_key`) is not `none`. ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.2 (P0.1b, R-8 / G-1): this is **one test, defined once** — an invoice's `fiscal` snapshot is fiscalized iff `required && adapter_key !== 'none'` (`isFiscalized`, §3.15); every later "fiscalized" means it, and the `InvoiceIssued` schema refuses `required: true` with adapter `none`.
⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-1, J-2, J-4, J-7): **Jurisdiction** = the tax and fiscal rule set a branch sells under (a province, state or country; `jurisdiction_id`, B31). **Authority** = the body a jurisdiction reports to through its adapter (`adapter_key`; `none` = no reporting). **Tax profile** = what an item is taxed as (01a's tax class; the line's `tax_profile_id`); the jurisdiction maps it (by its `fiscal_category`) to an **ordered list of taxes**. **Tax** = one levy in that list (`tax_code`, label, `compound: boolean` — compound = charged on the base plus the taxes before it in the list), with rate rows. **Buyer tax ID** = `{ type, value }`, `type` from the jurisdiction's accepted types. **Fiscal number** = the authority's id for an accepted invoice. **Fiscal device ID** (`pos_id`) = the authority's register / POS identifier. **Exponent** = the currency's ISO 4217 minor-unit digits; one major unit = `10^exponent` minor units.

### 3.2 Bill state

```
 (projected) ──first 05 command──► OPEN ──first split closes──► PARTLY_PAID ──last split closes──► CLOSED
                                     ▲                                                              │
                                     └──────────────────── ReopenBill (approved) ◄──────────────────┘
                                                            (credits every sale invoice; REOPENED = OPEN with history)
```

| State | Lines editable (02b / 04) | Splits re-plannable | Payments | Merge / split of the seating (02a) |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| projected / OPEN, no payment | yes | yes | add / remove | yes; an absorbed bill folds into the surviving one (§3.9) |
| OPEN with payments | yes | only splits without payments may change or go | add / remove | refused `409 BILL_HAS_PAYMENTS` |
| PARTLY_PAID | lines **not** in a closed split: yes; units of a closed split: **no** (`LINE_IN_CLOSED_SPLIT`, B15) ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-6) | open splits without payments only; closed splits never | open splits only | refused |
| CLOSED | no (`ORDER_BILLED`); a new order on the seating opens a **new** bill (B1) ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-6) | no | no | n/a (seating NEEDS_CLEANING) |
| reopened (OPEN, `reopen_count ≥ 1`) ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-10 + S-9, owner R2) | **full edit:** yes, through 02b / 04 (add, remove, void, comp) — takeaway / parking always, dine-in when `SeatingReopened`; **corrections only** (dine-in, table cleaned / re-seated or another open bill on the seating): remove / void / comp / comp removal of its own lines, no new lines (02a's `409 TABLE_SEATING_CHANGED`, ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 H2-14) | yes for splits without payments | carried payments stay; balance may be negative (pay-out, §3.7) | full edit: as OPEN; corrections only: n/a |

### 3.3 Close preconditions (B12), checked in this order, all in the close transaction

1. Bill exists, state OPEN / PARTLY_PAID (`404 BILL_NOT_FOUND`, `409 BILL_NOT_OPEN`); the split exists and is open.
2. No pending void on the bill's orders (`pendingVoids(db, order_ids)` non-empty) → `409 BILL_CLOSE_BLOCKED { reason: 'void_pending', order_item_ids }`.
3. No pending weight (`live_units > 0` and `line_total_minor IS NULL`, 04 R-4) on a line of the split → `{ reason: 'weight_pending', order_item_ids }`.
3a. ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-2): every taxed line's profile resolves to at least one tax with a rate row for the issue date in the branch's jurisdiction (§3.4) → else `{ reason: 'tax_unresolved', order_item_ids }` (a config gap is never billed as untaxed). ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.2 (P0.1b, R-1): the same holds for the service charge — while the split's charge > 0 and its tax profile (`service_charge_tax_profile_id`, not `null`) is unresolved, close is refused with `tax_unresolved` and `order_item_ids` holds the literal `'service_charge'`; the charge is never billed untaxed.
4. No open `discount` request on the bill → `409 DISCOUNT_PENDING { alert_id }`.
5. *(Removed in v0.2, OQ-6: unassigned lines no longer exist — a `SetSplits` item plan must cover every live unit (§3.8) and a later line joins the open remainder automatically.)* ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-6)
6. Buyer tax ID (B19): the jurisdiction has a threshold and the whole bill's net > threshold → payload `buyer` present (its `tax_id.type` accepted, its value matching that type's pattern, §6.12), or a `BuyerIdWaived` on the bill → else `409 BUYER_ID_REQUIRED { threshold_minor, net_minor, accepted_types }` (⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-4): `accepted_types` = the type codes).
7. `expected_total_minor` (the total the screen showed) equals the recomputed split total → else `409 BILL_TOTAL_CHANGED { expected_minor, total_minor }` (a void approved, a round added, a rate effective date passed while the cashier looked).
8. Balance ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-2), ⚠️ Changed in v0.8 (§25): **Σ in `amount_minor` − Σ out `amount_minor` = the rounded total** `T = G + R` (§3.5 (7)), i.e. balance 0; no class check (⚠️ Changed in v0.8.1 (§26): the `pro_rata` tolerance is gone with `pro_rata`); an `in` never exceeds **its due field** (§3.5 (7), ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.2 (H16-3)) (v0.7.3–v0.7.5's "its mode's due" and "beyond the balance by `R`" rules removed) → else `409 BILL_BALANCE_DUE { split_no, balance_minor }`. Change = Σ over the split's cash `in` payments of (`tendered_minor` − `amount_minor`), each part on its own payment's shift.
8a. ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-26): an amount plan whose re-based last open share falls below 1 minor unit (§3.8, ⚠️ v0.7.2 H9-12) → `422 BILL_SPLIT_INVALID { reason: 'shares_sum' }`.
9. ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-3): change needs no check at close — each cash payment's change was fixed at `AddPayment` on that payment's money shift (§3.7). A **new** cash payment needs the closer's money shift (§6.9).

⚠️ Changed in v1.0.6 (R-29, design brief Q-5): the bill view (§6.1) names each rule above with one pinned `blocks` `reason` and the `details` of the error the close would answer (table in §6.1).

Pending **comp** requests do not block: each split close closes `obsolete` the open `comp` requests on the units it invoiced ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-23: was "only at the last close"); the last close closes the rest (02b To 05).

### 3.4 Line grouping for tax ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-2, owner §17)

For each live line of the bill's orders (by `orderLines`), base as §3.1, profile = the line's `tax_profile_id` (row Q-1; the value of `ItemAdded.tax_class_id`): `item` → its own; `deal` → the deal's (on the whole billed deal amount); `deal_part` → none (base 0); `option_child` → its own. A line is taxed only when the branch has `tax_enabled` **and** the line's profile is non-null (01a S34). No line is ever in two profiles.

**Profile → taxes.** The branch's jurisdiction maps the profile's `fiscal_category` (01a tax class field, row A-1) to an **ordered list of 1–4 taxes** `[{ seq, tax_code, label, compound }]` (`jurisdiction_profile_taxes`), each with rate rows (B5). The jurisdiction **`none`** maps every profile to one tax `{ seq: 1, tax_code: 'TAX', label: <tax class name>, compound: false }` at the tax class's `rate_bp` for both payment classes (v0.6.2's adapter-`none` rule). A taxed line whose profile has no tax list, or a tax with no rate row in force at the issue date, is **`tax_unresolved`**: the bill view lists it in `blocks` and close is refused (§3.3 3a); the billing-settings `PUT` already refuses an active tax class without a mapping (row A-2), so this is a guard, not a flow. ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.2 (P0.1b, R-1): an unresolved **service-charge** tax profile is `tax_unresolved` too (§3.3 3a, `order_item_ids` = `'service_charge'`). Taxes stack **only** through such a list; Pakistan's seeded profiles have one tax each (B4).

### 3.5 Split arithmetic (pure, `@pos/domain/src/billing/compute.ts`)

Per split `s`, given its lines (item split: the assigned units of each line, base pro rata to units with `allocateDealMinor`; amount / equal split: every bill line, base allocated by the split's share with `allocateDealMinor`, remainder to the last split), the discount, the service charge state, the payment classes and the jurisdiction data (price mode, taxes per profile with their rate rows, rounding rule; ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-1–J-3)):

1. `S` = Σ line bases of the split.
2. `D` = the split's share of the **bill** discount (v0.2, OQ-4): the discount (amount, or percent → `floor(Σ bill bases × pct_bp / 10000)`) is spread over **every bill line** by base with **`allocateCappedMinor(D_bill, bases)`** ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-4): largest-remainder allocation, each share `floor(D × base / Σ base)`, the left-over minor units one each to the lines with the largest fractional part (ties: the line added first), never above the line's base; so `0 ≤ share ≤ base` always (property #10d). A split's `D` = Σ its lines' shares (item split: per assigned units; amount / equal: per allocated share; a line's share is split over its parts with **`allocateCappedMinor(share, base_parts)`**, the caps being the base parts just computed, ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-12); a line's **open base** = its base − Σ its `closed_split_units.share_minor`, shared by the open units, ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-2)); frozen at the first split close and capped for the open part per B7 (only over `bill_discount_lines`, Q-1) ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-5, owner R3); `D ≤ S` always. ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.2 (P0.1b, R-2): the frozen discount is re-spread over the open `bill_discount_lines` on every read, so an open split's `D` can move by ±1 minor unit after a sibling split closes; accepted in v1.0. (v0.6.2's per-class net `N_c` is `A_p` / `N_p` below.)
   ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-3): **bases and the discount are in the bill's price mode** (⚠️ Changed in v0.7.2 (H9-9): the mode in force when the bill opened, frozen in `bills.prices_include_tax` from `BillOpened`; a settings or dated-rule change applies to bills opened after it, never to an open or reopened bill; a projected bill uses the mode in force now; ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.2 (H16-2): **the rounding rule is frozen likewise** — `BillOpened` carries `rounding: { step_minor | null, mode, applies_to }` in force at open, folded into `bills.rounding_json`; a reopen and its refunds keep it; every `InvoiceIssued` (and so every credit) records it in `fiscal.rounding`) — tax-exclusive amounts when prices are exclusive, tax-inclusive (the menu price the customer saw) when inclusive; the discount is therefore always taken **before** tax in both modes. Per-profile discounted amount `A_p` = Σ over the profile's lines of (base − discount share); untaxed lines form their own group with no taxes.
3. ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-2, J-3) **Pre-tax net per profile** `N_p`, for the split's payment class (rule 5) — **exclusive:** `N_p = A_p`. **Inclusive (back-calculation):** with the profile's taxes `t_1 … t_n` at rates `r_k` (bp), the exact rational multiplier `M_p = 1 + Σ a_k`, `a_k = (r_k / 10000) × (1 if t_k is simple, else 1 + Σ_{j<k} a_j)`; the profile's **included tax** `TI_p = round_half_up(A_p − A_p / M_p)` (exact BigInt rational inside, **one rounding per profile**), and `N_p = A_p − TI_p`. With one tax this is v0.6.2's `round_half_up(A − A × 10000 / (10000 + r))` exactly. Bill pre-tax net `N = Σ N_p` (untaxed groups: `N_p = A_p`).
4. `SC` = `round_half_up(N × sc_bp / 10000)` when the bill's service charge is applied, else 0 — on the **pre-tax net in both price modes** (⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-3), ⚠️ Changed in v0.7.1 (OQ-10, developer call, owner delegated: final): in inclusive mode the back-calculated `N`, so the service charge never includes tax; v0.6.2's `S − D` is the same value in exclusive mode) and **never tax-inclusive itself** ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-14). SC is taxed under `service_charge_tax_profile_id` (setting, ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-2): was `service_charge_tax_class_id`; `null` = untaxed, Appendix P A9): `SC_p = SC` for that profile, else 0. SC is counted once.
5. Rates: for each profile `p`, tax `t` and payment class `k ∈ {cash, digital}`, `r(p, t, k)` from the jurisdiction's rate rows at the issue date (B5). Split payment class (⚠️ Changed in v0.7.5 (H12-5): decided by the split's live **`in`** payments only; an `out` never changes it): all digital → digital; all cash / none → cash; both → the jurisdiction's `mixed_class_rule`, which in v1.0 is always `higher_rate` = cash (⚠️ Changed in v0.8.1 (§26); `pro_rata` out of v1.0, §3.6).
6. **Taxes** ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-14), ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-2, J-3): per profile, in list order, each tax rounded half up **at its own point**: (a) the **exclusive part** — the whole `N_p` when prices are exclusive, plus `SC_p` in both modes — `T_k = round_half_up(B_k × r_k / 10000)` with `B_k` = that part for a simple tax and that part + Σ_{j<k} `T_j` (the already rounded earlier taxes on the same part) for a compound tax; (b) the **included part** (inclusive mode, menu amounts): `TI_p` is spread over the taxes by their exact shares `e_k = A_p × a_k / M_p` with `apportionMinor(TI_p, e)` (⚠️ Changed in v0.8.2 (H16-12): `floor(TI_p × e_k / Σ e)` each, the left-over minor units one each to the largest fractional parts, ties to the earlier tax, exact rationals; Σ = `TI_p`, never negative; one tax → all of `TI_p`). Each invoice tax row = (profile, tax code, payment class, rate, taxable base, tax) where the tax is (a) + (b) and the base is `N_p` (+ `SC_p`) for a simple tax, plus the earlier taxes for a compound one. Total before rounding, **both modes:** `G = N + SC + Σ tax rows` (inclusive: `N + Σ TI_p = Σ A_p`, so the menu amounts are paid as shown).
7. **Rounding adjustment, dues and refunds** ⚠️ Changed in v0.8 (§25), ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.1 (§26, H15-5, H15-6), ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.2 (H16-1, H16-4, H16-14; owner Q-2 provisional, §27). `G` = the exact total of rule 6 (tax on exact amounts; class per rule 5, `higher_rate`). Rounding rule = the **bill's frozen rule** (rule 2, H16-2). `rnd` rounds to `step_minor` by its mode on the magnitude (`nearest` half away from zero; step `NULL` = no rounding); `floor_step(x)` = the largest step multiple ≤ `x`. Notation (H16-14): `P_d`, `P_c` = Σ digital / cash net of the split's live payments (`in` − `out`); `D` remains the discount. **Rounding `R`:** `applies_to: 'all'`: `R = rnd(G) − G` on every split (Pakistan: every bill, card included); `applies_to: 'cash'`: `R = rnd(G) − G` while the split's **class is cash** (rule 5: a live cash `in`, or no live `in` at all — H16-4), else `R = 0`; step `NULL`: `R = 0`. `R` is an **untaxed rounding adjustment owned by the restaurant, in no payment class**: never in a tax base. Total `T = G + R`; **balance** `B = T − P_d − P_c`. **The four due fields** (exactly what `AddPayment` and the bill view return, and the only limits on an `in` / `out`): **`card_due_minor`** = `max(0, B⁺ᵈ)` and **`cash_due_minor`** = `max(0, B⁺ᶜ)`, where `B⁺ᵏ` is the balance recomputed **as if one more live `in` of class `k` were on the split** — rule 5's class with that payment (so `card_due` is at the digital rates while the split has no live cash `in`, and `cash_due` always at the cash rates) and `R` as that payment would make it (H16-1); under `applies_to: 'cash'` `cash_due` is rounded down to the step (H15-5); ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.3 (H17-1): **both payment dues are 0 while `B ≤ 0`** — a paid split is closed, an overpaid one is refunded, and only the refund dues show (H12-6's intent). **`card_refund_due_minor`** = `min(P_d, max(0, −B))` and **`cash_refund_due_minor`** = `min(P_c, max(0, −B))` (under `'cash'` rounded down to the step), at the **current** class (an `out` never changes it). Named (H16-1): Rs 5,710 net on `PK-PB`, unpaid → card due **5,996.00** (5,995.50 at 5 %, rounded), cash due **6,624.00** (6,623.60 at 16 %, rounded); after card 3,000 → cash due 3,624.00 (the split is then mixed → 16 % on the whole). An `in` is accepted only while `B > 0` (H17-1; else `422 PAYMENT_EXCEEDS_BALANCE { balance_minor: 0 }`); a digital `in` ≤ `card_due`; a cash `in` ≤ `cash_due` (excess tender is change) and, under `'cash'`, a step multiple (`422 PAYMENT_CASH_STEP`); a digital `out` ≤ `card_refund_due`; a cash `out` ≤ `cash_refund_due` (a type that paid nothing has 0 → `422 PAYMENT_EXCEEDS_BALANCE { balance_minor: 0 }`). **Zero cash total** (Q-2 (a), provisional, §27): under `'cash'` a split whose `rnd(G)` is 0 (e.g. a 0.02 remainder with a 0.05 coin) has `R = −G`, `T = 0` and closes at 0 with no payment (the rounding line is the whole amount; §6.12 zero-total close, H6-11); ⚠️ Changed in v1.0 (run-18 Q-1 (a), owner with the approval): such a split is **paid** — its balance is 0, so a card payment for it is refused (H17-1) and it closes at 0. **Refunds — "jitna liya utna wapas":** when `B < 0` (a reopen made the bill smaller) the bill is re-rounded (frozen rule) and the amount back is `−B`, per payment type up to what that type paid (the two refund dues; the cashier chooses the mix). A **full refund** (every line removed, `T = 0`) returns **exactly what each type paid**, rounding included, then `BillClosedEmpty` (§3.10). A refund never changes the split's payment class or tax (rule 5 uses the `in` payments). **Reopen after a rate or jurisdiction change** (Q-1 (a), provisional, §27): the credit copies the original's tax and snapshot (B5, H9-6); the new invoice is taxed at its issue date's rates; the difference is paid in or out like any reopen. **Invoice and fiscal:** `rounding_minor = R` prints as its own "Rounding adjustment" line, outside every tax row and every payment class; X / Z show "rounding" as a separate line (§6.26); the adapter maps it (§3.15, Appendix P, A14), never inside taxable value.
8. Invariants (property tests, §10.3): Σ over splits of `S` = Σ bill line bases; Σ printed tax = Σ invoice tax rows; `G = N + SC + Σ tax rows` in both price modes; one rounding per split; no negative line, net, tax or total; a one-tax profile gives v0.6.2's numbers exactly (Pakistan regression fixture, #11); inclusive with no discount, no SC and one class: `G = Σ menu amounts` (#11c). ⚠️ Changed in v0.8 (§25): `R` is never in a tax row or a tax base; tax rows are identical for the same lines and payment class whatever `R`; `T = G + R`; Σ payments − change = `T` at close; a full refund returns each type exactly what it paid.

### 3.6 Mixed classes (B6) ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.1 (§26)

**v1.0 supports only `higher_rate`** (owner 2026-11-18 14:40 PKT): a split paid only by digital modes is taxed at the digital rates; a split with any cash payment is taxed at the cash rates on the whole split (§3.5 rule 5); a refund never changes the class (only `in` payments count). The jurisdiction column `mixed_class_rule` stays, but `pro_rata` is refused by the `0014` CHECK (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.2 (P0.1b, R-6): was `0016`) and the snapshot parser (row A-2; ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.3 (H17-3)).

**Deferred to v1.1 — pro rata mixed-payment tax**, when an authority requires it (A6). Starting material: §3.6 of v0.6.2 – v0.8 (declared class amounts `d`, `DeclareClassShares`, frozen-once-paid, close check, line-change rules), the owner rulings §16, §21, §24, §25 rule 4, and the run 12 – 15 reviews with their property models (`docs/reviews/05_v0.7.4_targeted_check.md`, `05_v0.7.5_targeted_check.md`, `05_v0.7.6_targeted_check.md`, `05_v0.8_targeted_check.md`). It needs its own spec round and review before it is built.

### 3.7 Payments and change (B10, B11)

- `AddPayment` is accepted on an open split with `amount_minor > 0`; `direction: 'in'` only while the split's balance is > 0 (⚠️ Changed in v0.8.3 (H17-1): else `422 PAYMENT_EXCEEDS_BALANCE { balance_minor: 0 }`); `direction: 'out'` only when the split's balance is negative (a reopened bill whose new total is lower than what was paid) and never above that negative balance — money given back from the closer's money shift (cash out of the drawer, or a card / online reversal recorded with a reference).
- ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-2, H1-3): every `in` payment's `amount_minor` ≤ its due field (`card_due` / `cash_due`, §3.5 (7); ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.2 (H16-3); `422 PAYMENT_EXCEEDS_BALANCE`). Cash `in`: `tendered_minor ≥ amount_minor` optional (absent = exact); its change `tendered − amount` is fixed at `AddPayment` and belongs to **that payment's** `shift_id` (the drawer that received the tender). A payment carried by a reopen keeps its shift and its change.
- A mode with `reference_required` → `422 PAYMENT_REFERENCE_REQUIRED` without a 1–60 character reference; the reference is stored only in `payment_references` (hub-only), `PaymentAdded` carries `has_reference` ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-20).
- Expected cash of a shift = float + Σ cash `in` `amount_minor` − Σ cash `out` `amount_minor` + Σ settled-advance cash returned − Σ advances − Σ cash-outs + Σ cash-ins ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-2: tendered and change cancel out on the receiving shift) (G1-8; property test).

### 3.8 Splits (B2)

- `SetSplits` replaces the plan of the **open** splits: `mode: 'items'` (each split lists `{ order_item_id, units }`; **every live unit not in a closed split exactly once** (`units_missing` / `units_exceed`); a deal goes whole; an option child follows its parent), `'amount'` (shares in minor units (⚠️ v0.7.3 H10-8) of the open remainder's total at the default class, ≥ 1 each, Σ = that total; converted to net shares by `allocateDealMinor`), `'equal'` (`n` 2–20 over the open remainder). A split with payments keeps its number (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.10 (R-53): its number only — its share may change, as §6.5) or the command is refused (`409 BILL_HAS_PAYMENTS { split_nos }`, G0-4: no money moves silently). Closed splits are never touched; after the first close the mode of the open remainder may change, the closed splits keep theirs. ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-6)
- A later round (dine-in) adds lines: they **join the open remainder automatically** — the highest-numbered open split (item split: as assigned units; amount / equal: that split absorbs them whole; ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.10 (R-52): **equal** spreads them equally over the plan's n open splits — the plan holds `{ n, k }` only, as the money core does) — with no staff action and no block on close; staff may move them among open splits with `SetSplits` ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-6: was "unassigned until a `SetSplits`, close refused"). ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.6 (R-29, design brief Q-4): the bill view marks such lines with `lines[].added_after_close` (§6.1).
- **A unit leaving the bill** (`ItemVoided`, `ItemRemoved` folded by 05) comes off the **highest-numbered open split** that holds the line; units invoiced by a closed split can never leave (the line guard refuses, row Q-3) ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-6). ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.2 (P0.1b, R-16): 05's line guard takes **all** lines a call touches as one list (`[{ order_item_id, units_after, … }]`, row Q-3) and answers the whole list in **1 statement** (one read of `closed_split_units` for those ids), never one read per line.
- **Amount shares are re-based on every change** ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-22): the last open split's share = the open remainder's default-class total − Σ the other open shares; if that would fall below 1 minor unit the close is refused `422 BILL_SPLIT_INVALID { reason: 'shares_sum' }` until a `SetSplits`.
- The discount (B7, whole bill only, before the first close) and the service charge (B8) are bill-level and spread per §3.5 ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-4).
- **Money invariants** (property tests #10b, #10c) ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-6): Σ units over closed splits + open remainder = the bill's live line units; no unit is in two splits; the units invoiced by a closed split are unchanged until a reopen; Σ split totals (closed + open) = the bill as paid.
- The buyer-ID threshold reads the **whole bill** (B19); a split never changes it.

### 3.9 Follow 02a merges and splits (02a To 05, G0-4)

- Merge (`TablesMerged`, S2 into S): refused by 05's merge guard (row T-1) while either seating's bill has a payment, a discount, a closed split or **any invoice (`reopen_count ≥ 1`)** ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-11) (`409 BILL_HAS_PAYMENTS`). Otherwise the fold: S has a bill → S2's bill (if any) is marked `absorbed_into`; S has none → S2's bill is re-pointed to S. A removed service charge stays removed on the surviving bill (B8); the absorbed bill's own state is dropped.
- Seating split (`SeatingSplit`): refused while the source bill has payments, discount, a closed split **or `reopen_count ≥ 1`** (⚠️ Changed in v0.5.1 (H4-3): a reopened bill reads `bill_orders`, so a moved order would stay on it; same rule as merge, H2-11) (`409 BILL_HAS_PAYMENTS`); otherwise the moved orders leave the bill at the next read (the bill reads `seating_orders` live, B1); an item split plan that names a moved line drops that entry (fold); the remaining live units still sum to the open splits (a dropped unit simply leaves the bill).
- ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.19 (R-79): **statements** — 05's merge guard (row T-1, merge and seating split) and line guard (row Q-3) each cost **1** statement whenever they run, and they run only after 04's guard passes. The statement counts of 02a merge / seating split, 02b `RemoveItems`, `CompLine`, `SetLineWeight`, 04's void command and its `apply`, and 05's `RemoveComp` (§6.14) **exclude** the registered guards' statements (R-72 extended, row Q-7; G-72 carries the 02a / 02b / 04 follow-up). ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.24 (R-85 (3)): the same holds for 01a's `CloseBusinessDay`: its count is plus the registered **day-close guards' and appenders'** statements (04's guard, 05's guard and appender, §6.25); G-72 now also carries 01a.

### 3.10 Reopen (B14)

⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-10 + S-9, owner R2, 2026-11-17 23:58 PKT): **reopen = full edit for every order type**, decided inside the reopen transaction (direct path) or the answer transaction (`reopen_bill` approve), from the state read there (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.7 (R-40 / G-37): the decided mode is written into **`BillReopened.mode`**, and the billing fold sets `bills.reopen_mode` from it, never from a re-read of the seating / key state):

| Bill | Condition (read in the transaction) | Mode | 02a effect |
|---|---|---|---|
| takeaway / parking | **no other open bill holds the `bill_key`** ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-5) | **full edit** (no lock; additions = `SubmitOrder { adds_to_order_id }`, row Q-6; **not** for an earlier-day takeaway, see the next row) | none |
| takeaway / parking | the bill's orders are of an **earlier business day** (Q-3, developer call, owner delegated) | **corrections only**; an item to add is a new order today (its own bill) | none |
| takeaway / parking | another open bill holds the key (an addition after close opened it, #44e) | **corrections only** — **final** (Q-5, developer call, owner delegated) | none |
| dine-in | seating state ≠ `CLOSED` (not cleaned, so no new seating can be on its tables) **and** no other open bill on the seating | **full edit** | when the bill was CLOSED (⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-27)): `SeatingReopened { seating_id, bill_id, alert_id? }` → `bill_closed = 0`, state re-folded from its live orders (ORDERED / FOOD_ON_TABLE), `bill_requested_at` cleared; holder and assignee unchanged, so `requireLock` passes for the holder / `take-order-anywhere` as usual |
| dine-in | seating `CLOSED` (cleaned, maybe re-seated), or another open bill on the seating (S-9 (b)) | **corrections only** (`bills.reopen_mode = 'corrections'`; ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.7 (R-40 / G-37): set by the billing fold from `BillReopened.mode`, §6.13): void / comp / comp removal / remove of its own lines, discount, payments | none; a `SubmitOrder` naming that seating → 02a's `409 TABLE_SEATING_CHANGED` (T18; ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-14): no new code); a `RemoveItems` on a `CLOSED` seating skips the lock (row T-3) — ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.7 (R-39 / 02a v1.1.10): only the lock; the authorisation still runs (`requireLockHolder`: the seating's holder, or `take-order-anywhere` covering the floor, plus scope), else `403 PERMISSION_DENIED { reason: 'not_holder' }` |

**Another open bill** ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-9), ⚠️ Changed in v0.5.1 (H4-1) = a non-closed `bills` row on the seating / `bill_key` **other than this one**, **or** an order of it with `billed_at IS NULL`, not cancelled and **not in this bill's order set** (its `bill_orders`; for a bill reopened from PARTLY_PAID, the orders §6.13 reads in its step 1 and writes as `BillReopened.order_ids`) (a projected bill counts). The mode is decided with this set excluded, before any draft is built (§6.13 step order).

**Races** (single writer, every check inside `BEGIN IMMEDIATE`): `CleanTable` committed first → the reopen sees `CLOSED` → corrections only. Reopen (full) committed first → the seating is occupied again, so `CleanTable` is refused `409 TABLE_HAS_OPEN_ORDERS` (02a, unchanged); `OpenTable` on its table re-enters the reopened seating (holder, or `take-order-anywhere`), a stale `seating_id` → `TABLE_SEATING_CHANGED` ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-23: was "`TABLE_NOT_FREE`", which `OpenTable` never answers). A reopen request raised before a clean and approved after it → the mode is the one read at approve (corrections only). A `SubmitOrder` (S-9 (b)) committed before a full reopen → the seating has another open bill → corrections only.

**S-9 (b), a new order after close** (row T-3): after `BillClosed` and before `CleanTable`, `SubmitOrder` on that seating is accepted (`requireLock` no longer refuses NEEDS_CLEANING for `SubmitOrder` only; `OpenTable`, merge, handover and `RemoveItems` keep `TABLE_NEEDS_CLEANING`); 02a's `OrderPlaced` fold sets `bill_closed = 0` and re-folds the state (ORDERED / FOOD_ON_TABLE); 05 sees an unbilled order that is in no open bill's `bill_orders` → a new projected bill on the seating; its close writes `BillClosed` again (B14).

Allowed on a CLOSED or PARTLY_PAID bill whose orders are not cancelled. Effects, one transaction: `BillReopened` → per closed split, `InvoiceCredited { credit_no, ref_invoice_id }` (credit = the full original: same lines, tax rows, discount, service charge, rounding; payments not reversed) → 02b `OrdersUnbilled { order_ids, bill_id }` when the bill was CLOSED (row Q-2) → every split OPEN again with its payments (its closed-split units are no longer frozen); the split plan stays. Fiscal: each credit's outbox row is ordered behind its original (an original still pending → the credit waits, B20). After a full-edit reopen: lines are added and changed through 02b / 04 as usual (corrections only: no additions), discount and charges through 05, payments added / removed / paid out (§3.7); the next close of each split issues a new sale invoice. Reopen never reopens the business day; credits and new invoices land in today's Z (the day open now). ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-6, Q-3 (b), owner 2026-11-18 11:08–11:09 PKT): when every order of the reopened bill is cancelled (02b writes `OrderCancelled` when no live line is left) and every split's balance is 0 (money given back with `out` payments), the command that made it so appends **`BillClosedEmpty`** (payload §6.29; ⚠️ Changed in v0.6.2 (H8-4): for **any** bill with a `bills` row whose orders are all cancelled and every split balance 0 — reopened or never closed; a never-closed bill has nothing to credit) (⚠️ Changed in v0.6.1 (H7-3): **who appends it** — `AddPayment { out }` whose pay-back makes it true; 05's **order-cancelled hook** in 02b `changeDrafts` (row Q-7, ⚠️ Changed in v0.6.2 (H8-4): also `RemoveItems`; ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.17 (R-72): its statements count on top of 02b's and 04's cancel / void counts — 1 when the order has no bill, more when the bill empties; ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.19 (R-79): the same holds for registered guards — those counts are plus the registered hooks' **and guards'** statements, row Q-7) when the last line's removal or void — also a void approved later by 04's `apply`, then with that request's `alert_id` — cancels the last order while every split balance is already 0; and `CloseSplit` on such a bill, which writes `BillClosedEmpty` instead of a Rs 0 `InvoiceIssued`; the open-bills row shows **Close** for it. 02a folds it like `BillClosed` (NEEDS_CLEANING, row T-3 (7)); the bill's open 05 requests are refused at approve (bill CLOSED) and closed at day close; Z: not in bills count / average ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.23 (R-84): only when the bill has no sale invoice in that day; a bill closed, then reopened and emptied the same day has its sale invoice and still counts, §6.26) — state CLOSED, no sale invoice, no `OrdersBilled`, no fiscal row, no receipt; the credit is the only fiscal record.

### 3.11 Shifts (B23)

```
 (none) ──OpenShift──► OPEN ──CountShift (count + close, one transaction)──► CLOSED   (drawer free at once)
                         │                                     │  |variance| > threshold → variance_state 'pending' + shift_variance sign-off request
                         │                                     │      approve → 'approved'; reject → 'disputed' (X / Z); no recount, no reopen
                         └──CloseShift { skip_count } (skip-blind-count)──► CLOSED (count_skipped)        (v0.6, owner Q-2)
   HandoverCount (drawer shift, any time while OPEN): count + variance recorded, shift stays OPEN
```

- One open shift per person; a drawer device in at most one open shift (`409 DRAWER_IN_USE`). A shift's money (B11) and its petty cash (B24) are the only inputs to its expected cash (§3.7).
- **Blind count:** `CountShift { counted_minor }` stores the count first; only its response (and later reads) carry `expected_minor` and `variance_minor`. Every read before that returns the shift without them (server rule, tested). A shift is counted once: the count closes it (⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (Q-2, owner 2026-11-18 11:08–11:09 PKT)).
- Open petty-cash advances never block `CountShift` or `CloseShift` (G1-8, ⚠️ Changed in v0.6.2 (H8-9)); they are listed on the close screen and on the X / Z.
- Closing another person's shift: `close-shift-for-others` (blind count applies to the closer).
- A takeover on a drawer kiosk (README :183, Q10): the drawer shift stays open and keeps the drawer; the new person takes any mode; every payment lands in the drawer shift with `taken_by_staff_id` = the new person; the shift close and X / Z show the drawer's takings by `taken_by`.

### 3.12 Petty cash (B24)

`RecordPettyCash { kind: 'advance' | 'out' | 'in', amount_minor, category_id, paid_to?, advance_staff_id?, note? }` on the caller's money shift (cash). Advance: `advance_staff_id` required, open until `SettlePettyAdvance { entry_id, spent_minor, returned_minor, note? }` (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.18 (R-77): was `advance_id`; `entry_id` as §6.20 and the contract) with `spent + returned = amount` (`422`); settling may happen on another shift (the returned cash lands there). Approval: grant `requires_approval` → request every time; grant `allowed` and `amount_minor > petty_cash_limit_minor` → request; every `petty_cash` request is answered by holders of `close-shift-for-others` (§3.14, H1-1 / owner R1). Vendor khata stays in module 10.

### 3.13 Day close (B25)

`CloseBusinessDay` (01a) after its own checks asks the guard list: 04 (void requests open) then 05 → `409 DAY_CLOSE_BLOCKED` with `reason`:
- `'shift_open'`: `{ shifts: [{ shift_id, staff_name, opened_at, is_drawer }], count }` (≤ 40);
- ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.18 (R-76): `'variance_pending'`: `{ shifts: [{ shift_id, staff_name, closed_at, alert_id }], count }` (≤ 40): shifts with `variance_state = 'pending'` (any day; `alert_id` = `variance_alert_id`), checked after `'shift_open'` and before `'unbilled_orders'`;
- `'unbilled_orders'`: `{ orders: [{ order_id, identifier, total_minor, placed_at, open_minutes }], count }` (≤ 40): orders with `billed_at IS NULL`, `cancelled_at IS NULL`, not in `carried_orders` for this day. ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.25 (R-86): "this day" is the `business_date`, not the open session — `carried_orders` stays keyed by `(order_id, business_date)` (H6-10). When a date is closed, reopened and closed again (R-85 (1)), an order carried at the first close is still excused here at the second close, is listed again under the second Z's "carried over", and cannot be carried a second time that date (one row per order and date; a second `CarryOverOrders` adds no row, the first `reason_code` stays). No money effect; the order comes forward the next day as before (§6.25).
Then the appender (row A-4) returns, in order: `AlertClosed` drafts (`obsolete`, note `day_closed`) for every open 05 request (`discount`, `remove_service_charge`, `petty_cash`, `reopen_bill`, `remove_comp`, `close_without_buyer_id`; **not** `shift_variance`, a sign-off on a closed shift that carries over, ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (Q-2, owner 2026-11-18 11:08–11:09 PKT); ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.18 (R-76): none is open here — the guard refuses the close while one is pending, `'variance_pending'`) and 02b's open `comp` requests (01d To 05) — void requests never reach here (04 guard); then `ZReportIssued`; then 01a appends 03a's `stockEffects` and `BusinessDayClosed`.

### 3.14 Approvals (01d kinds registered by 05)

*⚠️ Changed in v0.6.1 (H7-5): every re-check below runs on **approve** only; a **reject** returns `[]` (for `shift_variance`: `ShiftVarianceSignedOff { outcome: 'disputed' }`) and always succeeds, closing the request.* *⚠️ Changed in v1.0.18 (R-76): for `shift_variance` this holds, and R-44's `business_date` (the open day) always exists, because a business day is always open when its request is answered — `CloseBusinessDay` is refused while any `shift_variance` request is pending (§6.25, `variance_pending`). The `shift_variance` `apply`'s `BUSINESS_DAY_NOT_OPEN` guard (task 21) stays as defence and is unreachable under this rule.*

| Kind | Raised by | Approver roles | `apply` (read-only, every draft carries `alert_id`) |
|---|---|---|---|
| `discount` | `ApplyDiscount` with grant `requires_approval` | the grant's `approver_role_ids` | `DiscountApplied` (re-checks the bill is OPEN and the amount still fits; else throws → answer refused) |
| `remove_service_charge` | `RemoveServiceCharge`, grant `requires_approval` | grant's | `ChargeRemoved` — ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-2): re-checks the bill is OPEN / PARTLY_PAID and the charge still applied, else throws |
| `reopen_bill` | `ReopenBill`, grant `requires_approval` | grant's | `BillReopened` + `InvoiceCredited`… + `OrdersUnbilled` (§3.10; numbers assigned in `apply` from the same read view, B13; ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.15 (R-66): credit ids from the values' `credit_invoice_ids`, minted at raise, §6.13; ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.17 (R-74): on approve, sale invoices that differ from the values' `invoice_ids` (a stale request) → throws `BILL_NOT_OPEN { bill_id, state }`, §6.13) |
| `remove_comp` | `RemoveComp`, grant `requires_approval` | grant's | 02b `LineCompRemoved` — ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-2): re-checks §6.14 (order not billed, `comped_units ≥ units`, line guard list incl. `LINE_IN_CLOSED_SPLIT`), else throws |
| `close_without_buyer_id` | `WaiveBuyerId`, grant `requires_approval` | grant's | `BuyerIdWaived` — ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-2): re-checks the bill is OPEN / PARTLY_PAID, else throws; ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.16 (R-68): it re-checks nothing else, so approving on a bill already waived writes a second `BuyerIdWaived` (§6.11) |
| `petty_cash` | `RecordPettyCash` (grant `requires_approval`, or over the limit) | **holders of `close-shift-for-others`** (01d right `{ mode: 'holders' }`, row D-1) ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-1, owner R1: was "grant's, or over-limit approvers") | the petty-cash event — ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-2): re-checks the shift is `open` (not CLOSED), else throws |
| `shift_variance` | `CountShift` with `|variance| > variance_threshold_minor` (the shift is already CLOSED) | **holders of `close-shift-for-others`** (⚠️ Changed in v0.3, H1-1, owner R1) | ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (Q-2, owner 2026-11-18 11:08–11:09 PKT): a **sign-off** — approve → `ShiftVarianceSignedOff { shift_id, outcome: 'approved', alert_id, approver_staff_id }`; reject → the same with `outcome: 'disputed'` (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.7 (R-44 / G-39): both carry **`business_date`** = the **open business day in the answer transaction** (`ctx.state` business day, 0 statements) — the day the sign-off is answered, which may be later than the shift's own day; the `shifts` fold copies it into `shift_signoffs.business_date`, never reading `billing_day` (§5, H8-1, #50c)); re-checks the shift's `variance_state` is still `pending`, else throws; never reopens the shift ⚠️ Changed in v0.6.1 (Q-4, owner 2026-11-18 11:21 PKT): `disputed` is a **record only** — shown on X / Z and the shift history with who and when; no automatic deduction, block or follow-up. ~~01d keeps the request open across day closes (request kinds never expire; only closed alerts are purged), so it can be answered on a later day.~~ ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.18 (R-76): a pending request **blocks the day close** (§6.25 `variance_pending`), so it is answered on the day it was raised and `business_date` (R-44) is that day; the answer rules above (H7-5) are unchanged. The requester is the counter (R-78, §6.17). |

⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-1, owner R1): 01d as built takes approver roles only from the requester's `requires_approval` grant (`alerts/targets.ts`); row D-1 adds the right **`{ mode: 'holders', permission: 'close-shift-for-others' }`**: any signed-in person who holds that permission `allowed` with a scope covering the requester may answer (Manager, Owner by default; never the requester), whatever the requester's own grant; routing (`answerers`) resolves those holders. `petty_cash` and `shift_variance` always use it.

*(v0.2's "over-limit approvers" via 01b `rolesGranting` is replaced by the 01d holders right; row B-3 dropped.)* Steps = `APPROVAL_TIER_STEPS`; `needs_pin: 'always'` (money; 01d `approval_receipt`); ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.9 (R-49): every 05 request kind is alert family **`urgent`** (the requester waits at the counter, as 04 `void_approval`); approver = `AnswerFacts.answerer_staff_id`, never `actor_id`. `details` carry ids, amounts and reason codes only (never a buyer ID), **in `AlertDetailsBase`'s existing keys** ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-6): `amount` (minor), `reason` (the reason code), `identifier`; `shift_variance` → `{ amount: variance_minor }`; any other key would be dropped by the schema. Withdraw = never asked (`onWithdraw` returns `[]`). Dedupe keys: `discount:<bill_id>`, `remove_sc:<bill_id>`, `reopen:<bill_id>`, `remove_comp:<order_item_id>`, `buyer_waiver:<bill_id>`, `petty:<command_id>`, `variance:<shift_id>`.

### 3.15 Fiscal port and outbox (B20–B22) ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-6: the core holds only the port contract; the PRA adapter is Appendix P)

```
 CloseSplit / ReopenBill tx ──► InvoiceIssued / InvoiceCredited ──fold──► fiscal_outbox row (state pending, ord = invoice seq)
                                                                                   │
 fiscal worker (after listen, one request in flight per hub) ◄─────────────────────┘
   adapter.submitSale / submitCredit(submission) ── ok {fiscal_number, qr_payload} ──► writer tx: FiscalNumberAssigned → row acked
                          ── retry (network, 5xx, timeout) ──► attempts+1, next_try_at = backoff (5 s, 30 s, 2 min, then 5 min)
                          ── rejected (the authority refused the record) ──► FiscalSubmitRejected (event), row 'rejected', alert; never auto-resent
                          ── duplicate (the authority already holds this idempotency key) ──► ok when the number is returned, else 'rejected'
```

**Port contract** (`apps/hub/src/billing/fiscal/port.ts`; the only thing the core knows about an authority):

```typescript
export interface FiscalAdapter {
  key: string;                                     // 'none' | 'PRA' | … (a jurisdiction's adapter_key)
  capabilities: {
    ordering: 'strict_per_pos_id' | 'none';        // strict: a pos_id's head row blocks its later rows
    credit: boolean;                               // can report a credit against an accepted original
  };
  settingsSchema: ZodType;                         // v0.7.2 (H9-4): validates this adapter's opaque settings blob (shared with the cloud PUT, row A-2); never a secret field (v0.7.3 H10-5)
  credentialNames: string[];                       // v0.7.3 (H10-5): the named secrets it needs per pos_id (PRA: ['token'])
  submitSale(sub: FiscalSubmission): Promise<FiscalAnswer>;
  submitCredit(sub: FiscalSubmission & { original: { invoice_ref: string; fiscal_number: string | null; issued_at: number } }): Promise<FiscalAnswer>;
  health(): Promise<'ok' | 'failing'>;
}
export type FiscalSubmission = {
  idempotency_key: string;                         // = invoice_ref (AC1-S000123 / AC1-C000007); resent unchanged after a crash
  pos_id: string | null; credentials: Record<string, string>;   // ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.2 (P0.1b, R-9 / G-7): null when the row has none, never ''   // v0.7.3 (H10-5): the adapter's credentialNames read from fiscal_credentials at send, never logged
  jurisdiction_id: string; currency_code: string; currency_exponent: number; prices_include_tax: boolean;
  seller: { branch_id: string; tax_registrations: { code: string; number: string }[] };   // v0.7.2 (H9-4, H9-5): from the invoice's snapshot
  config: { environment: 'sandbox' | 'production'; base_url: string | null; settings: unknown };
                                                   // v0.7.2 (H9-4): branch fiscal settings at send; `settings` = the branch's blob for this adapter, opaque to the core; never logged
  invoice: {                                       // neutral, from InvoiceIssued / InvoiceCredited (§6.29); amounts _minor
    invoice_ref: string; issued_at: number; order_type: string;
    lines: { order_item_id: string; item_id: string | null; tax_profile_id: string | null; fiscal_category: string | null;   // v0.7.2 (H9-4)
             name: string; units: number; unit_price_minor: number; base_minor: number; discount_minor: number;   // ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.3 (R-17): unit_price_minor = round_half_up(line base ÷ units), weighed line = whole total, deal_part = 0 (§6.12 step 6)
             taxes: { tax_code: string; payment_class: 'cash' | 'digital'; rate_bp: number; base_minor: number; tax_minor: number }[] }[];   // ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.3 (R-19): taxes[].base_minor per §6.12 step 6 (R-19); Σ `tax_minor` per tax over the lines + the service-charge tax = the invoice row (R-18)
    service_charge: { amount_minor: number; taxes: { tax_code: string; payment_class: 'cash' | 'digital'; rate_bp: number; base_minor: number; tax_minor: number }[] } | null;   // v0.7.3 (H10-8)
    subtotal_minor: number; discount_minor: number; tax_minor: number; rounding_minor: number; total_minor: number;   // ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.2 (P0.1b, R-12 / G-5): tax_minor = Σ the invoice tax rows incl. service-charge taxes
    taxes: { tax_profile_id: string; tax_code: string; payment_class: 'cash' | 'digital'; rate_bp: number; base_minor: number; tax_minor: number }[];
    payments: { authority_code: string | null; payment_class: 'cash' | 'digital'; direction: 'in' | 'out'; amount_minor: number }[];
    buyer: { name: string; tax_id: { type: string; value: string } } | null;   // from invoice_buyers; a credit reads its original's row
    buyer_id_refused: boolean;
  };
};
// ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.2 (P0.1b, R-9 / G-4): payments[].authority_code is null when the mode has no code for this adapter; the adapter answers `rejected` (`mode_unmapped`) on null
export type FiscalAnswer =
  | { outcome: 'ok'; fiscal_number: string; qr_payload: string | null }
  | { outcome: 'retry'; error_code: string }                       // short code only ('timeout', 'http_503', …)
  | { outcome: 'rejected'; error_code: string; http_status: number | null }
  | { outcome: 'duplicate'; fiscal_number: string | null };
```

⚠️ Changed in v0.7.2 (H9-4), ⚠️ Changed in v0.7.3 (H10-5, H10-6): an adapter's own **non-secret** configuration (endpoints beyond `base_url`, public codes the authority assigns to the seller) lives in the branch fiscal settings' **`adapter_settings: { [adapter_key]: object }`** (≤ 4 KB each), opaque to the core and validated by that adapter's `settingsSchema` at the billing-settings `PUT` (row A-2) and again at hub load (an invalid blob → rows stay `pending`, `last_error: 'adapter_settings_invalid'`, stuck alert; ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.9 (R-50): the same for `config_missing` — no provisioned branch config yet — and `invoice_missing` — the event at the row's `ord` is not its invoice). **No secrets there** (the blob reaches the cloud table, `config_audit`, every snapshot and the owner `GET`): a `settingsSchema` must not define secret fields; any key, token or password an adapter needs is a **named credential** (`credentialNames`) in the hub-only `fiscal_credentials`, entered on the hub admin page (B22, §6.28a). **Config per row:** the worker builds `config` for the **row's recorded adapter** (`fiscal_outbox.adapter_key`, which a credit copies from its original), never the branch's current one: `base_url = base_urls[row.adapter_key][environment] ?? null` (null = the adapter's defaults) and `settings = adapter_settings[row.adapter_key] ?? {}`. The adapter owns everything authority-specific: field names and invoice types, how the idempotency key, a credit's reference to its original, payment codes and the buyer ID are sent, how service charge and rounding are placed, the credential header, base URLs, timeouts and how the authority's errors map to `retry` / `rejected` / `duplicate`. The core never branches on an adapter key. The **`none`** adapter is never called (its rows are `not_required`); it exists so every branch has an adapter key.

- **Order** ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-17), ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-18), ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-6): when the adapter's `ordering` is `strict_per_pos_id`, for each `pos_id` the worker sends that `pos_id`'s oldest `pending` row by `ord`, and while it backs off only **that `pos_id`'s** later rows wait; with `none` every row is sent independently. In both, a credit waits for its original's answer and steps aside meanwhile; one `pos_id` with a missing credential never holds another's rows. A credit of a `rejected` original becomes `rejected` too (same stuck alert, same developer fix). One request in flight per hub.
- **At least once, same key:** every submission carries `idempotency_key = invoice_ref`; a crash between the answer and the `FiscalNumberAssigned` commit resends the same key (test #70). The outbox row is written by the projection fold in the close transaction, so a committed invoice always has its row; a rolled-back close has none.
- **Payment codes** ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-5): each payment's `authority_code` is the mode's code for **this adapter** (`authority_codes`, row A-1); the core guarantees one exists for every live payment of a fiscalized split (§6.9, §6.12); an adapter receiving none treats the row as `rejected` (`last_error: 'mode_unmapped'`), never a wait. ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.2 (P0.1b, R-9 / G-4): on the port that is `authority_code: string | null`; the adapter answers `rejected` (`mode_unmapped`) on null.
- **Not fiscalized** (adapter `none`, or order type with `fiscalize: false`, or an invoice issued while the branch's jurisdiction had adapter `none`): row state `not_required`; the receipt has no fiscal block.
- **Switch** of the branch's jurisdiction (e.g. `PK` → `PK-PB`, G1-16; ⚠️ Changed in v0.7.2 (H9-3): the pilot fallback is `PK`, not `none`) applies to invoices issued after the setting's `switched_at`; earlier invoices keep the jurisdiction they recorded. ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-8): **a credit always follows its original** — its fiscal state (`not_required` ↔ `not_required`; an `acked` / `pending` original → the credit is fiscalized), its jurisdiction, adapter and `pos_id`, and (⚠️ Changed in v0.7.2 (H9-6)) the jurisdiction snapshot recorded on the original invoice (`InvoiceIssued.fiscal`) — even when the branch has since switched or the reopening device maps to another `pos_id`.
- **Credit support** ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-1, J-6): a fiscalized original (state `pending` / `acked`) can be credited only when its adapter's `capabilities.credit` is true **and** its jurisdiction's `credit_support = 'credit_invoice'` (⚠️ Changed in v0.7.2 (H9-6): both read from the **original invoice's snapshot**, never today's snapshot); otherwise `ReopenBill` refuses `409 BILL_REOPEN_NOT_SUPPORTED { invoice_id }` (§6.13). Non-fiscalized invoices are always creditable (local credit series). Pakistan seed and PRA: supported (no change).
- **Stuck:** a `pending` row older than `stuck_after_ms`, a `rejected` row, or a row older than half the jurisdiction's `catch_up_window_ms` → one `fiscal_outbox_stuck` alert (dedupe `fiscal_stuck:<branch_id>`, managers, `closes_on: 'owner'`, **`expires_at_day_close: true`** ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-18: 01d refuses `false` without `'seen'`); closed `done` when no row is stuck; after a day-close expiry the tick re-raises it with the same dedupe on its next tick while a row is still stuck; ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.2 (P0.1b, R-12 / G-22): was "the worker" / "next pass", the stuck alert is raised by a 01d tick); details = `{ quantity: stuck count, minutes_late: oldest age in minutes }` (⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-6), existing keys).
- **Health:** `adapter.health()` is called by the worker at most every 60 s while rows wait; its result is in `fiscal-status` (§6.27). No HTTPS call ever runs on a command path.
- **Secrets:** the worker reads the `pos_id`'s credentials (every name in the row adapter's `credentialNames`, ⚠️ v0.7.3 H10-5) from the hub table `fiscal_credentials` (B22, §6.28a) at send time and passes them in the submission ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-3); a missing credential → the row stays `pending` with `last_error: 'token_missing'` and the stuck alert fires (go-live precondition, G1-16).
- **Adapters shipped:** `none` and **`PRA`** — the PRA adapter's mapping, values and open authority questions are **Appendix P** (STOP at build there, H6-4). Any other adapter implements this contract and adds a jurisdiction seed; nothing in the core changes.
- **Live sandbox runs are manual owner steps** (G1-12); CI and the normal gate use recorded fixtures only (no network, CLAUDE.md).

### 3.16 Printing (03c, B17, B18)

| Document | When | Printer | Drawer |
|---|---|---|---|
| Check bill | `PrintCheckBill`; 02a `BillRequested` (live-only fold, 03c pattern) | payload `printer_id`, else the bill route of the table's zone → floor → default (row P-1); takeaway / parking: the device's route → default | no |
| Receipt (one per invoice) | `CloseSplit`; reprint `PrintBillDocument` (§6.22). ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.31 (R-92): its **Change** line = the change due at that invoice's close (R-91): Σ (tendered − amount) of the invoice's cash `in` payments added after the bill's latest `BillReopened` before the invoice's `InvoiceIssued` (all of them on a never-reopened bill) — not `InvoiceIssued.change_minor` (the Σ); a reprint re-derives the same figure from stored events | the device's route → default | yes when the split had a cash `in` or `out` payment |
| Credit note | live-only fold of `InvoiceCredited` (direct `ReopenBill` and `reopen_bill` approve alike) | the event's device route (the answerer's on an approval) → default | no |
| Z / X | `ZReportIssued` (live-only fold); X and any reprint through `PrintReport` (§6.22, ⚠️ Changed in v0.6.2 (H8-9)) | `report` route → default | no |
| No-sale | `OpenCashDrawer` | the device's route | yes |

No printer routed / none active → the document is skipped (logged with ids), the command still succeeds (R8, 03c To 05); ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.10 (R-55): a QR payload the printer cannot encode (empty, or over 400 code points, 03c R-26) is left out of the Doc — the fiscal number line still prints, nothing errors, the slip prints; the screen shows the receipt (§8). ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.2 (P0.1b, R-11 / G-2): the receipt / reprint builder takes the invoice's fiscal state (`pending` / `acked` / `rejected`) and prints "FISCAL NUMBER PENDING", the fiscal number (+ QR), or "FISCAL NUMBER NOT ISSUED" (B21). ~~Every job id is kept~~ ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.18 (R-75): every **bill-document** job id (check bill, receipt, credit note, Z / X) is kept in `bill_print_jobs` so the bill view shows its status and "Print to" can move it (B18). The **no-sale** drawer kick (`OpenCashDrawer`, §6.21) is **not** kept: it has no bill or seating, so no bill view would show it, and `RetargetBillPrint` could move a drawer kick to another counter (against R-57).

---

## §4. Contracts

### 4.1 Consumes

⚠️ Changed in v1.0.7 (R-37 … R-39, R-43): the versions 05 now depends on are the header's "Depends on" line (current `module-05`: F v1.14, 00 v1.12.1, 01a v2.7.1, 01b v1.10.1, 01c v1.1.2, 01d v1.3.4, 02a v1.1.10 → v1.1.11 with this bump, 02b v1.0.18, 03a v1.2.6, 03c v1.0.14, 04 v1.0.7); the rows below say what 05 uses, not which version.

| From | What | Used for |
|---|---|---|

### 4.3 Requirements carried to other modules

| To | Requirement |
|---|---|
| 06 | Sync carries `bill`-stream events; cloud read models for shift and Z history and D22 tiles (sales by payment mode, discounts, voids) are built from synced events with `buildReport` / `computeBill` (K-9). `invoice_buyers` and `fiscal_outbox` are hub state and are **never synced** (B19). A hub restore from the cloud keeps the invoices (events) but loses buyer IDs, fiscal tokens and outbox worker state: the restore runbook re-derives the outbox from events (fold) and the owner re-enters the tokens on the hub admin page (B22). |
| 06 / 07 ⚠️ Changed in v0.7.2 (H9-1, owner Q-1) | **Currency:** one tenant = one currency (`tenants.currency_code`; every invoice also records it), so tenant-level views sum within one currency; ~~v0.7.1: group by currency across branches~~ (superseded). A **cross-tenant** owner view (an owner with tenants in two countries) is deferred (§12.4), not 05. |
| 07 | Sales by item / category / hour / staff, the live money view with failed-PIN and notice-seen lists (01d To 05 money section, README :226), tax report — from `InvoiceIssued` / `InvoiceCredited` / payment events (Q14). |
| 01e | A Windows-only authority path (PRA local service, KPRA RIMS) is decided with 01e / hardware when such an adapter is built (K-16). |
| Milestone C | Adapters SRB, KPRA, BRA, FBR-ICT (and any other country's) behind the port, each with its jurisdiction seed rows confirmed against a notification (research rule) ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-1). |
| 01a (text, later) ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-1), ⚠️ Changed in v0.7.2 (H9-8) | `lineTotalMinor` (S59) rounds a weighed line to 100 minor units ("whole rupee") because the owner chose it (Q6.13), not because of the exponent. It becomes jurisdiction data **`weighed_line_step_minor`** (`PK` / `PK-PB` 100, other seeds 1 = exact) when the first non-PK jurisdiction is seeded. Not changed now (§17 YAGNI). |
| 01a / 01c / 02b / 01d (client, later) ⚠️ Changed in v0.7.2 (H9-7) | Money formatters outside 05 print `Rs` with 2 decimals: `features/staff/orders/money.ts`, `features/owner/money.ts` (`shownRupees`), `features/owner/choice-groups/{choice.ts, ChoiceBranchRow.tsx}`, `features/owner/deals/{DealList.tsx, ChoicePicker.tsx, DealForm.tsx}`, `features/owner/size-groups/{SizeGroupForm.tsx, SizeGroupList.tsx}`, `features/owner/menu/{MenuView.tsx, ItemForm.tsx}`, `features/alerts/{ui.tsx, labels.ts}` (13 files) and `i18n/en.ts` (the `Rs` strings). Change: format with the snapshot's `currency_code` and `currencyExponent` (row F-6). Changed when the first non-PKR tenant is sold; 05's own client formats with the exponent from the start (§8). |
| Pilot checklist (plan) | (pilot branch, jurisdiction `PK-PB`, Appendix P; ⚠️ v0.7.2 (H9-3): fallback `PK`) G1-16 go-live preconditions (owner tasks): POS ID(s) registered with PRA (A1), production token entered on the hub admin page (B22, §6.28a), one sandbox run passed, the owner's / accountant's acceptance that only the POS floor reports to PRA (R20). Any missing → the branch starts on `PK`; the switch is a setting change (audited `config_audit`). M-2 note for 01c data entry: leave `is_default` off unless truly default. |

### 4.4 Shared types (`@pos/domain/src/billing/types.ts`, browser-safe)

```typescript
export const PAYMENT_CLASSES = ['cash', 'digital'] as const;
export const PAYMENT_MODE_KINDS = ['cash', 'card', 'wallet', 'online', 'other'] as const;   // kind 'cash' ⇒ class 'cash' (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.2 (P0.1b, R-4): was ⇔)
export const SPLIT_MODES = ['items', 'amount', 'equal'] as const;
export const MIXED_CLASS_RULES = ['higher_rate'] as const;   // ⚠️ v0.8.1 (§26): 'pro_rata' out of v1.0 (v1.1, A6)
export const ROUNDING_METHODS = ['nearest', 'down', 'up'] as const;
// ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-1, J-6): adapter keys and jurisdiction ids are data (strings), not a closed enum; shipped adapters: 'none', 'PRA'
export const ROUNDING_APPLIES_TO = ['all', 'cash'] as const;
export const CREDIT_SUPPORT = ['credit_invoice', 'none'] as const;
export const FISCAL_ORDERING = ['strict_per_pos_id', 'none'] as const;
// buyer tax ID types ('CNIC', 'NTN', 'VAT', …) are jurisdiction data, never an enum
export const INVOICE_SERIES = ['sale', 'credit'] as const;
export const FISCAL_ROW_STATES = ['pending', 'acked', 'rejected', 'not_required'] as const;
export const DISCOUNT_KINDS = ['amount', 'percent'] as const;
export const PETTY_KINDS = ['advance', 'out', 'in'] as const;
export const BILL_REOPEN_REASONS = ['wrong_item_billed', 'wrong_payment_mode', 'customer_returned_item', 'price_error', 'other'] as const;
export const CHARGE_REMOVE_REASONS = ['customer_request', 'service_complaint', 'staff_meal', 'other'] as const;
export const COMP_REMOVE_REASONS = ['comped_by_mistake', 'wrong_line', 'other'] as const;
export const CARRY_OVER_REASONS = ['table_still_seated', 'customer_will_pay_later', 'other'] as const;
// discount reasons are owner-edited (billing settings), e.g. regular_customer, staff, owner_guest, complaint, other
```

`note` (≤ 200) is required whenever a reason code is `other`.

`jurisdiction_profile_taxes (jurisdiction_id, fiscal_category, seq 1–4, tax_code, label, compound BOOLEAN, PRIMARY KEY (jurisdiction_id, fiscal_category, seq), UNIQUE (jurisdiction_id, fiscal_category, tax_code))`; `jurisdiction_tax_rates (jurisdiction_id, fiscal_category, tax_code, payment_class, integration_status ('integrated' \| 'not_integrated'), effective_from DATE, rate_bp 0–10 000, notification_ref, source_note, PRIMARY KEY (all key columns))`. **Seeded** only with confirmed rows: **`none`** (adapter `none`, currency `NULL`; rules: exclusive, step `NULL` = 1 minor unit = **no rounding** (⚠️ Changed in v0.7.2 (H9-3)), `nearest`, `all`, `higher_rate`, buyer `{ threshold_minor: null }`, no fiscal fields, catch-up `NULL`, `credit_invoice`, `weighed_line_step_minor` not used yet (H9-8); no profile rows — §3.4 gives one tax at `rate_bp`), ⚠️ Changed in v0.7.2 (H9-3) **`PK`** (Pakistan, no authority: adapter `none`, `PKR`; rules as `none` but step 100 — v0.6.2's adapter-`none` behaviour exactly; the pilot fallback) and **`PK-PB`** (Punjab; adapter `PRA`; `PKR`; rules from eIMS Rules 2019: exclusive, 100, `nearest`, `all`, `higher_rate`, buyer `{ 5 000 000, [CNIC `^[0-9]{13}$` strip `-`, NTN `^[A-Za-z0-9]{7,9}$`] }`, receipt `{ 'PRA invoice no.', tax_reg_labels: [{ 'NTN', 'NTN' }] (⚠️ v0.7.2 H9-5), show_qr: true, the PRA verification URL with `{fiscal_number}`, buyer_id_on_receipt: true }`, 604 800 000, `credit_invoice`; profile `restaurant_service` → `[{ 1, 'PST', 'Punjab sales tax on services', compound: false }]`; rates as v0.6.2: digital 500 bp, cash 1600 bp, `PRA.32-24/2026/1313`, 2026-02-19). Rows "ref not found" (research) are **not** seeded. (3) Per-branch **billing settings** document (table `branch_billing_settings`, one row, `version`): **`jurisdiction_id`** (default `PK` for a PKR tenant, else `none`, ⚠️ Changed in v0.7.2 (H9-3); its currency ≠ the **tenant's** `currency_code` → `422 CONFIG_REFERENCE_INVALID { field: 'jurisdiction_id', reason: 'currency_mismatch' }`, checked whenever the `PUT` sets or keeps a jurisdiction, ⚠️ Changed in v0.7.2 (owner Q-1)); owner overrides `prices_include_tax?`, `rounding_step_minor?` (1 … 10^(exponent + 4), ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.2 (H16-13)), `rounding_method?`, **`rounding_applies_to?`** (⚠️ Changed in v0.7.2 (H9-3)) (absent = the jurisdiction's; the v0.6.2 settings of the same names); payment modes `[{ mode_id, name, kind, payment_class, reference_required, is_active, sort_order, authority_codes: [{ adapter_key, code: string 1–20 }] }]` (⚠️ J-5: was `pra_payment_mode`; seeded `cash` → `{ PRA, '1' }`, `card` → `{ PRA, '2' }`, none otherwise until Appendix P A13; with the jurisdiction's adapter ≠ `none`, an active mode offered on a fiscalized order type needs a code for that adapter → `422 CONFIG_REFERENCE_INVALID { field: 'payment_modes.N.authority_codes' }`); `service_charge_bp` per order type (0–3 000, default 0); **`service_charge_tax_profile_id`** (J-2: was `service_charge_tax_class_id`); discount reasons `[{ code, label }]`; `max_discount_pct` (default 100); `variance_threshold_minor` (default 100 major units, Pakistan 10 000); `petty_cash_limit_minor` (default 5 000 major units, Pakistan 500 000); petty categories `[{ category_id, name, is_active }]`; `open_bill_flag_minutes` (default 240); receipt template (K-12: `header_lines` ≤ 6, `footer_lines` ≤ 6, ≤ 48 chars, show toggles `tax_reg_no`, `address`, `phone`, `cashier`, `order_no`, `bill_no`; **`tax_registrations: [{ code, number ≤ 30 }]`** (⚠️ Changed in v0.7.2 (H9-5), C6 keyed by `code`; codes from the jurisdiction's `tax_reg_labels`; Pakistan one `{ 'NTN', … }`; was `tax_registration_no` / `ntn`, also replaces the fiscal `taxpayer_ntn`), `address`, `phone`, `invoice_prefix` ≤ 6); fiscal `{ environment: 'sandbox' \| 'production', base_urls?: { [adapter_key]: { sandbox?: url, production?: url } } (⚠️ Changed in v0.7.3 (H10-6)), pos_ids: [{ pos_id, label }], default_pos_id, device_pos_ids: [{ device_id, pos_id }], integration_status, fiscalize_by_order_type: { dine_in, takeaway, parking, delivery } (delivery default false, Q17), stuck_after_ms (default 1 800 000), switched_at, **adapter_settings: { [adapter_key]: object }** (⚠️ Changed in v0.7.2 (H9-4): opaque per adapter, ≤ 4 KB, validated by the adapter's `settingsSchema`; ⚠️ Changed in v0.7.3 (H10-5): **never a secret**) }` (the authority is the jurisdiction's `adapter_key`; `switched_at` is set when `jurisdiction_id` changes); owner rate overrides `[{ tax_class_id, tax_code, payment_class, integration_status, rate_bp, effective_from, notification_ref }]`. **No secret** in it (B22). (4) Routes `GET` / `PUT /v1/admin/branches/:branch_id/billing` (`expected_version`, C2 branch guard, C4 check order, C7 route case, `config_audit` row, branch bump) and **`GET /v1/admin/jurisdictions?jurisdiction_id=`** (read-only; was `GET /v1/admin/fiscal-catalog?authority=`; same route count). (5) Snapshot V4 gains the optional key **`billing`** (no snapshot version bump, R29; 03b A-1 precedent): the settings above, **`jurisdiction`** = `{ jurisdiction_id, adapter_key, currency_code, currency_exponent (from `currencyExponent`, row F-6), rules: [every `effective_from` row, overrides applied] }` and **`tax_profiles`** = per tax class `{ tax_class_id, taxes: [{ seq, tax_code, label, compound, rates: [{ payment_class, integration_status, effective_from, rate_bp, ref }] }] }` (catalog + overrides resolved; all `effective_from` rows, so the hub picks by date; under `none` one tax from `rate_bp`); absent → the hub's **built-in default rules** (⚠️ Changed in v0.7.2 (H9-3), ⚠️ Changed in v0.7.3 (H10-4)): a constant in `apps/hub/src/billing/defaults.ts` equal to the **`PK` seed** (exclusive, step 100, `nearest`, `all`, `higher_rate`, no buyer threshold, one tax per tax class at `rate_bp`, adapter `none`) for a PKR tenant and to the **`none` seed** (same, but step `NULL` = no rounding) otherwise; one cash mode; no service charge; ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.2 (P0.1b, R-10 / R-14 / G-23): a minimal `discount_reasons` list (the §4.4 example codes `regular_customer`, `staff`, `owner_guest`, `complaint`, `other`) and a minimal `petty_categories` list (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.2 (P0.1b, R-15): the codes `supplies`, `utilities`, `staff_advance`, `transport`, `other`); `invoice_prefix` empty and the receipt `header_lines` / `footer_lines` empty; every other default per (3) (`max_discount_pct` 100, `open_bill_flag_minutes` 240, `stuck_after_ms` 1 800 000, the variance and petty limits, the receipt show toggles per K-12); ⚠️ Changed in v0.7.4 (H11-5): both the `0014` seed and `defaults.ts` take the `none` / `PK` rule values from **one exported constant** `@pos/events/src/admin/jurisdiction-seeds.ts`, and a cloud test asserts the seeded rows equal it. ## §5. Data model (hub SQLite) — `apps/hub/src/db/migrations/0023_billing.sql` (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.11 (R-56): plus `0024_billing_payments_shift.sql`, the `bill_payments_shift` index only; ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.24 (R-85 (1)): plus `0025_billing_z_reports_key.sql`, the `z_reports` key only)

Two projections, both **events-only** (they read only their own tables and the event; never 02b's or 02a's tables in a fold), with `reset`, registered after `orders`, `tables` and `audit`:

- **`billing`** — ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-15): also 02b `ItemVoided`, `ItemRemoved` (a leaving unit off the highest open split, §3.8) and `SeatingSplit` by `order_id`; `BillOpened`, `ChargeApplied`, `ChargeRemoved`, `CheckBillPrinted`, `BillSplitsSet`, `DiscountApplied`, `DiscountRemoved`, `PaymentAdded`, `PaymentRemoved`, `BuyerIdWaived`, `InvoiceIssued` (+ `closed_split_units`), ~~`SplitClassSharesDeclared`~~ (v0.8.1 §26), `BillReopened`, `InvoiceCredited`, `FiscalNumberAssigned`, `FiscalSubmitRejected`, `FiscalTokenSet`, `OrdersCarriedOver`, `ZReportIssued`, `BillClosedEmpty` (⚠️ Changed in v0.6.2 (H8-10)), 02a `TablesMerged` / `SeatingSplit` (bill re-pointing, §3.9), `BusinessDayOpened` (current day key). ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.7 (R-41 / G-38): also **`DiscountFrozen`** — the only source of `bills.discount_json.frozen_minor` (`json_set` on a non-null `discount_json`; a bill with no discount keeps `NULL`); `InvoiceIssued` no longer sets `frozen_minor`, it still fills `bill_discount_lines` from `discount_line_ids`; `BillReopened` sets `frozen_minor` back to `null` (H2-19), so the next first close's `DiscountFrozen` sets it again — and (text clarification) 02b **`OrdersBilled`** (`{ order_ids, bill_id }`, appended by the last `CloseSplit`, §6.12 step 6), the source of `bill_orders` (one `INSERT OR IGNORE … SELECT … FROM json_each(order_ids)`); `BillReopened.order_ids` adds to it (H3-2). ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.7 (R-40 / G-37): the `BillReopened` fold sets `reopen_mode` from `mode`.
- **`shifts`** — `ShiftOpened`, `ShiftCounted`, `ShiftCountSkipped`, `HandoverCounted`, `ShiftClosed`, `PettyCashAdvanced`, `PettyCashSettled`, `PettyCashOut`, `PettyCashIn`, `DrawerOpened`, and the money facts of `PaymentAdded` / `PaymentRemoved` (cash tendered, change) for the running expected cash. ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.7 (R-44 / G-39): **events-only stays** — `shifts` never reads a `billing` table. `expected_cash_minor` comes from `ShiftOpened` (float), `PaymentAdded` / `PaymentRemoved` (`shift_id`, `payment_class`, `direction`, `amount_minor`; a removal reverses its payment), `PettyCashAdvanced` / `PettyCashOut` / `PettyCashIn` (`shift_id`, amount) and `PettyCashSettled` (`shift_id` = the settling shift, `returned_minor`; the advance's amount from the fold's own `petty_cash` row); `shift_takings` from `PaymentAdded` / `PaymentRemoved` (`shift_id`, `mode_id`, `taken_by_staff_id`, `direction`, `amount_minor`, `change_minor`; a removal reverses the same `(shift_id, mode_id, taken_by_staff_id)` row); `InvoiceIssued` adds nothing the payments do not carry (a carried payment keeps its shift and change, §3.7); `shift_signoffs.business_date` from `ShiftVarianceSignedOff.business_date` (the open business day of the answer, §3.14). ⚠️ Changed in v0.6.2 (H8-1, H8-10): also `ShiftVarianceSignedOff` (sets `variance_state`, inserts a `shift_signoffs` row). ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.7 (R-42 / G-39): the `ShiftCounted` fold sets `variance_alert_id` = the event's `variance_alert_id` and `variance_state` = `'pending'` when it is non-null, else `NULL` — **from the event alone**; it never reads `variance_threshold_minor` (a later settings change never moves a rebuilt shift). `HandoverCounted` sets neither (no sign-off).

Hub state that is **not** a projection (plain statements, never events, never synced; 03c rule): `invoice_buyers` (command-written), `payment_references` (command-written, v0.3 H1-20), `fiscal_credentials` (command-written, v0.2 OQ-3), `bill_print_jobs` (written with each print), and the worker columns of `fiscal_outbox`.

```sql
-- projection billing ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE TABLE bills (
  bill_id            TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  scope              TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (scope IN ('seating','key')),   -- v0.3 R2: 'key' = takeaway / parking table_key
  seating_id         TEXT,                                  -- dine_in
  table_key          TEXT,                                  -- takeaway / parking: 02b orders.bill_key (v0.4 H2-4, H2-16; column name kept)
  order_type         TEXT NOT NULL,
  state              TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (state IN ('open','partly_paid','closed')),
  opened_at          INTEGER NOT NULL,
  closed_at          INTEGER,
  sc_bp              INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,            -- ChargeApplied (rule or manual); 0 after ChargeRemoved
  sc_removed         INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (sc_removed IN (0,1)),   -- sticky (B8)
  discount_json      TEXT,                                  -- { kind, value, reason_code, alert_id, frozen_minor|null } | NULL; whole bill only (v0.2, OQ-4); ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.7 (R-41 / G-38): frozen_minor set only by the DiscountFrozen fold, cleared by BillReopened
  split_mode         TEXT CHECK (split_mode IN ('items','amount','equal')),    -- NULL = one split
  buyer_waived       INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (buyer_waived IN (0,1)),
  check_printed_at   INTEGER,                               -- pulled status "check bill printed" (B17)
  reopen_count       INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  reopen_mode        TEXT CHECK (reopen_mode IN ('full','corrections')),   -- v0.3 R2 (§3.10); NULL = never reopened; ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.7 (R-40 / G-37): set by the billing fold from BillReopened.mode (§6.13, §6.29)
  absorbed_into      TEXT,                                  -- §3.9 merge
  prices_include_tax INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (prices_include_tax IN (0,1)),   -- v0.7.2 (H9-9): the mode in force at BillOpened, frozen for the bill
  rounding_json      TEXT NOT NULL,                         -- v0.8.2 (H16-2): { step_minor|null, mode, applies_to } frozen at BillOpened
  CHECK ((scope = 'seating') = (seating_id IS NOT NULL)),
  CHECK ((scope = 'key') = (table_key IS NOT NULL))
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX bills_live_seating ON bills (seating_id) WHERE seating_id IS NOT NULL AND absorbed_into IS NULL AND state <> 'closed' AND reopen_mode IS NOT 'corrections';  -- openBillOf(seating); one open bill per seating, a later one after close (v0.2, OQ-6)
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX bills_live_key     ON bills (table_key) WHERE table_key IS NOT NULL AND state <> 'closed' AND reopen_mode IS NOT 'corrections';  -- openBillOf(key) (v0.3 R2); ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.7 (R-40 / G-37): both bills_live_* predicates read reopen_mode as the fold wrote it from BillReopened.mode, in the reopen's own transaction: a 'corrections' reopen never re-enters either index (no clash with the newer bill on the seating / key, #37f), a 'full' one does; each BillReopened overwrites the mode
CREATE INDEX bills_open                ON bills (state, opened_at) WHERE state <> 'closed' AND absorbed_into IS NULL;  -- open-bills list, X flag
CREATE INDEX bills_seating             ON bills (seating_id);   -- ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.7 (task 6 review): every bill of a seating, corrections bills included (the bills_live_* indexes exclude them): §3.10 other-open-bill checks, §6.4a tile reader
CREATE INDEX bills_key                 ON bills (table_key);    -- ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.7 (task 6 review): every bill of a takeaway / parking key (02b bill_key), corrections bills included: §3.10 other-open-bill checks

CREATE TABLE bill_orders (              -- written at the bill's last close (B1); the reopen set (v0.2, OQ-6)
  bill_id TEXT NOT NULL, order_id TEXT NOT NULL, PRIMARY KEY (bill_id, order_id)
);
CREATE INDEX bill_orders_order ON bill_orders (order_id);                                  -- line guard (row Q-3)

CREATE TABLE bill_splits (
```
