```sql
  bill_id   TEXT NOT NULL, split_no INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (split_no BETWEEN 1 AND 20),
  plan_json TEXT,                                          -- amount: { share_minor }; equal: { n, k }; items: rows in bill_split_lines (v0.4, H2-15)
  state     TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (state IN ('open','closed')),
  invoice_id TEXT,                                         -- the current sale invoice when closed
  PRIMARY KEY (bill_id, split_no)
);

CREATE TABLE bill_discount_lines (      -- v0.4 (H2-8, owner Q-1): lines on the bill at the close that takes it from no closed split to one (v1.0.6, R-29: the first close, or the first after a reopen); only they carry the frozen discount
  bill_id TEXT NOT NULL, order_item_id TEXT NOT NULL, PRIMARY KEY (bill_id, order_item_id)
);                                        -- filled from that InvoiceIssued's discount_line_ids (v1.0.6, R-29: was "the first InvoiceIssued's"; one INSERT … SELECT json_each); cleared by BillReopened (H2-19); also read by §6.1 added_after_close

-- bill_split_shares removed in v0.8.1 (§26: pro_rata out of v1.0)

CREATE TABLE bill_split_lines (         -- v0.4 (H2-15): item-split plan entries, foldable from events alone
  bill_id TEXT NOT NULL, split_no INTEGER NOT NULL, order_item_id TEXT NOT NULL, order_id TEXT NOT NULL, units INTEGER NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (bill_id, split_no, order_item_id)
);
CREATE INDEX bill_split_lines_line  ON bill_split_lines (order_item_id);              -- ItemVoided / ItemRemoved fold
CREATE INDEX bill_split_lines_order ON bill_split_lines (order_id);                   -- SeatingSplit fold

CREATE TABLE bill_payments (
  payment_id        TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  bill_id           TEXT NOT NULL, split_no INTEGER NOT NULL,
  mode_id           TEXT NOT NULL,
  payment_class     TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (payment_class IN ('cash','digital')),
  direction         TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (direction IN ('in','out')),
  amount_minor      INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (amount_minor > 0),
  tendered_minor    INTEGER CHECK (tendered_minor IS NULL OR tendered_minor >= amount_minor),   -- change = tendered − amount, on this row's shift (v0.3, H1-2, H1-3)
  has_reference     INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (has_reference IN (0,1)),   -- v0.3 (H1-20): the text is in payment_references
  taken_by_staff_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  shift_id          TEXT NOT NULL,
  added_at          INTEGER NOT NULL,
  removed_at        INTEGER,
  invoice_id        TEXT                                   -- set by InvoiceIssued; cleared by BillReopened (carried to the next close)
);
CREATE INDEX bill_payments_split ON bill_payments (bill_id, split_no) WHERE removed_at IS NULL;   -- bill view, close
-- ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.11 (R-56): in hub migration 0024_billing_payments_shift.sql (0023 is merged): takings by shift (shift view, CountShift, X / Z)
CREATE INDEX bill_payments_shift ON bill_payments (shift_id, mode_id, taken_by_staff_id);

CREATE TABLE invoices (
  invoice_id      TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  series          TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (series IN ('sale','credit')),
  number          INTEGER NOT NULL,                        -- gap-free per series (B13)
  invoice_ref     TEXT NOT NULL,                           -- '<prefix>' + 'S' | 'C' + number = the fiscal idempotency key (v0.6, H6-1; v0.7 J-6)
  bill_id         TEXT NOT NULL, split_no INTEGER NOT NULL,
  ref_invoice_id  TEXT,                                    -- credit → original
  business_date   TEXT NOT NULL,                           -- the open day at issue (Z key)
  issued_at       INTEGER NOT NULL,
  order_type      TEXT NOT NULL,
  jurisdiction_id TEXT NOT NULL, adapter_key TEXT NOT NULL,   -- v0.7 (J-1): recorded at issue; a credit copies its original's
  currency_code   TEXT NOT NULL,                           -- v0.7 (J-1): the tenant's currency at issue (v0.7.2 owner Q-1: one tenant = one currency)
  prices_include_tax INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (prices_include_tax IN (0,1)),   -- v0.7 (J-3): the price mode used
  subtotal_minor  INTEGER NOT NULL, discount_minor INTEGER NOT NULL, service_charge_minor INTEGER NOT NULL,
  tax_minor       INTEGER NOT NULL, rounding_minor INTEGER NOT NULL, total_minor INTEGER NOT NULL,
  change_minor    INTEGER NOT NULL,
  payment_class   TEXT NOT NULL,                           -- the split's class, or 'mixed'
  has_buyer_id    INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (has_buyer_id IN (0,1)),
  buyer_id_refused INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (buyer_id_refused IN (0,1)),
  closed_by       TEXT NOT NULL,
  doc_json        TEXT NOT NULL,                           -- lines + tax rows (receipt reprint, Z); payments are joined by payment_id, regardless of removed_at, the event holds payment_ids only ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.2 (P0.1b, R-12 / G-6): was "as in the event")
  credited_by     TEXT,                                    -- the credit invoice_id once credited
  fiscal_number   TEXT, qr_payload TEXT                    -- FiscalNumberAssigned
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX invoices_series_number ON invoices (series, number);                  -- MAX+1, gap check
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX invoices_ref ON invoices (invoice_ref);                             -- v0.6 (H6-1): idempotency key unique across sale and credit
CREATE INDEX invoices_day   ON invoices (business_date, series);                         -- Z / X
CREATE INDEX invoices_bill  ON invoices (bill_id);                                       -- reopen, bill view

CREATE TABLE invoice_taxes (            -- v0.7 (J-2): one row per (profile, tax, payment class) — the rate snapshot of the invoice
  invoice_id TEXT NOT NULL, tax_profile_id TEXT NOT NULL, tax_code TEXT NOT NULL, payment_class TEXT NOT NULL,
  seq INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (seq BETWEEN 1 AND 4), compound INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (compound IN (0,1)), label TEXT NOT NULL,
  rate_bp INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (rate_bp BETWEEN 0 AND 10000), base_minor INTEGER NOT NULL, tax_minor INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (tax_minor >= 0), rate_ref TEXT,
  PRIMARY KEY (invoice_id, tax_profile_id, tax_code, payment_class)
);                                                                                       -- Z tax by (tax code, profile, class) (joined by invoices_day)

CREATE TABLE fiscal_outbox (            -- rows by the billing fold (live + rebuild, INSERT OR IGNORE); worker columns below
  invoice_id   TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  ord          INTEGER NOT NULL,                           -- the invoice event's seq (send order)
  ref_invoice_id TEXT,                                     -- credit waits for its original
  jurisdiction_id TEXT NOT NULL, adapter_key TEXT NOT NULL, pos_id TEXT,   -- v0.7 (J-1, J-6): was `authority`
  state        TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (state IN ('pending','acked','rejected','not_required')),
  queued_at    INTEGER NOT NULL,
  acked_at     INTEGER,
  -- worker-owned (plain statements; reset by a rebuild, which is harmless)
  attempts     INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, next_try_at INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  last_error   TEXT,                                       -- a short code only ('timeout', 'http_503', 'token_missing', …)
  in_flight_at INTEGER
);
CREATE INDEX fiscal_outbox_pending ON fiscal_outbox (ord) WHERE state = 'pending';       -- worker head, status, stuck check
CREATE INDEX fiscal_outbox_rejected ON fiscal_outbox (ord) WHERE state = 'rejected';     -- ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.7 (task 6 review): §3.15 stuck check (every tick) and §6.27 rejected_count; fiscal_outbox is never pruned, so without it both scan the table

CREATE TABLE closed_split_units (       -- v0.2 (OQ-6): units each closed split invoiced; deleted for the bill by BillReopened.
                                         -- v0.3 (H1-27): filled by one INSERT … SELECT … FROM json_each(InvoiceIssued.lines);
                                         -- amount / equal splits store the line's live units at that close (the whole line freezes)
  bill_id TEXT NOT NULL, split_no INTEGER NOT NULL, order_item_id TEXT NOT NULL, units INTEGER NOT NULL, share_minor INTEGER NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (bill_id, split_no, order_item_id)
);
CREATE INDEX closed_split_units_line ON closed_split_units (order_item_id);             -- 05 line guard (row Q-3), 1 statement

CREATE TABLE carried_orders (order_id TEXT NOT NULL, business_date TEXT NOT NULL, reason_code TEXT NOT NULL, PRIMARY KEY (order_id, business_date));  -- v0.6 (H6-10)  -- day-close guard
-- ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.24 (R-85 (1)): in hub migration 0025_billing_z_reports_key.sql (0023 is merged): z_no is the key, business_date is not unique —
-- a day closed, reopened on the same date (01a OpenBusinessDay) and closed again has two Zs. 0025 rebuilds the table
-- (SQLite cannot change a primary key): create z_reports_new, copy every row, drop z_reports, rename, create the index.
-- 0023's form, kept for history: z_reports (business_date TEXT PRIMARY KEY, z_no INTEGER NOT NULL UNIQUE, issued_at INTEGER NOT NULL, report_json TEXT NOT NULL)
CREATE TABLE z_reports (z_no INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, business_date TEXT NOT NULL, issued_at INTEGER NOT NULL, report_json TEXT NOT NULL);
CREATE INDEX z_reports_day ON z_reports (business_date);                                -- Z list by date (§6.26), PrintReport by date (§6.22)
CREATE TABLE billing_day (k INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK (k = 1), business_date TEXT, opened_at INTEGER);  -- current day key

-- projection shifts ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE TABLE shifts (
  shift_id        TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  staff_id        TEXT NOT NULL,
  drawer_device_id TEXT,                                   -- non-NULL = drawer shift (B11)
  business_date   TEXT NOT NULL,
  opened_at       INTEGER NOT NULL,
  float_minor     INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (float_minor >= 0),
  state           TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (state IN ('open','closed')),   -- v0.6 (owner Q-2): no counted state
  variance_state  TEXT CHECK (variance_state IN ('pending','approved','disputed')),   -- NULL = within threshold or skipped
  expected_cash_minor INTEGER NOT NULL,                    -- running (§3.7); never returned before counted_at (B23)
  counted_minor   INTEGER, counted_at INTEGER, variance_minor INTEGER,
  count_skipped   INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (count_skipped IN (0,1)),
  variance_alert_id TEXT,                                  -- ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.7 (R-42 / G-39): ShiftCounted.variance_alert_id (NULL within the threshold)
  closed_at       INTEGER, closed_by TEXT
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX shifts_open_staff  ON shifts (staff_id) WHERE state = 'open';                       -- SHIFT_ALREADY_OPEN
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX shifts_open_drawer ON shifts (drawer_device_id) WHERE state = 'open' AND drawer_device_id IS NOT NULL;  -- DRAWER_IN_USE
CREATE INDEX shifts_day ON shifts (business_date);
CREATE INDEX shifts_signoff_pending ON shifts (closed_at) WHERE variance_state = 'pending';   -- v0.6.2 (H8-1): "pending sign-offs" on X / Z
CREATE TABLE shift_signoffs (            -- v0.6.2 (H8-1): "variance sign-offs" of the report's day, any shift date
  shift_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, outcome TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (outcome IN ('approved','disputed')),
  approver_staff_id TEXT NOT NULL, signed_at INTEGER NOT NULL, business_date TEXT NOT NULL
);
CREATE INDEX shift_signoffs_day ON shift_signoffs (business_date);                                                         -- Z / X, day-close guard

CREATE TABLE shift_handovers (shift_id TEXT NOT NULL, at INTEGER NOT NULL, counted_minor INTEGER NOT NULL,
  expected_minor INTEGER NOT NULL, variance_minor INTEGER NOT NULL, by_staff_id TEXT NOT NULL, PRIMARY KEY (shift_id, at));
                                        -- ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.21 (R-81): `at` is strictly increasing per shift — the shifts fold writes
                                        -- max(recorded_at, last at + 1 ms) (§6.19); no envelope field is redefined

CREATE TABLE shift_takings (            -- per shift × mode × taker (X / Z / shift close breakdown, Q10)
  shift_id TEXT NOT NULL, mode_id TEXT NOT NULL, taken_by_staff_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  in_minor INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, out_minor INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, change_minor INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  PRIMARY KEY (shift_id, mode_id, taken_by_staff_id)
);

CREATE TABLE petty_cash (
  entry_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, shift_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  kind TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (kind IN ('advance','out','in')),
  amount_minor INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (amount_minor > 0),
  category_id TEXT, advance_staff_id TEXT, at INTEGER NOT NULL,   -- v0.5.1 (H4-4): paid_to / note columns dropped (texts in bill_texts)
  settled_at INTEGER, settled_shift_id TEXT, spent_minor INTEGER, returned_minor INTEGER
);
CREATE INDEX petty_cash_shift ON petty_cash (shift_id);                                  -- shift view, Z
CREATE INDEX petty_cash_open  ON petty_cash (at) WHERE kind = 'advance' AND settled_at IS NULL;   -- open advances list

-- hub state (not projections) ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE TABLE invoice_buyers (           -- B19: the only place a buyer tax ID lives; written by CloseSplit; never synced, logged or returned
  invoice_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, buyer_name TEXT NOT NULL,
  id_type TEXT NOT NULL,                   -- v0.7 (J-4): a type of the jurisdiction's accepted_types ('CNIC', 'NTN', …)
  id_value TEXT NOT NULL                   -- normalised (strip characters removed); never leaves the hub except to the adapter and that receipt
);
CREATE TABLE bill_texts (               -- v0.4 (H2-22), v0.5 (H3-5): every free text a 05 command takes; hub-only, never synced
  subject_id TEXT NOT NULL,              -- the id the command mints and its event carries (entry_id, payment_id, note_id)
  field TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (field IN ('note','paid_to')), text TEXT NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (subject_id, field)
);
CREATE TABLE payment_references (       -- v0.3 (H1-20): hub-only; written by AddPayment; never synced, logged or in an event
  payment_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, reference TEXT NOT NULL
);
CREATE TABLE fiscal_credentials (       -- v0.2 (OQ-3, B22): written only by SetFiscalToken; never synced, logged, returned or put in an event
  pos_id TEXT NOT NULL, name TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'token',   -- v0.7.3 (H10-5): an adapter's named secrets (credentialNames); PRA: 'token'
  token TEXT NOT NULL, set_at INTEGER NOT NULL, set_by_staff_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (pos_id, name)
);
CREATE TABLE bill_print_jobs (          -- B18: job ids per bill document (03c jobs are not events)
  job_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, bill_id TEXT, doc_kind TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (doc_kind IN ('check','receipt','credit','report','no_sale')),   -- ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.18 (R-75): 'no_sale' stays in the CHECK, unused (OpenCashDrawer writes no row); 0023 is merged, no migration
  ref_id TEXT, created_at INTEGER NOT NULL
);
CREATE INDEX bill_print_jobs_bill ON bill_print_jobs (bill_id, created_at DESC);        -- bill view print status
```

**Why these shapes (YAGNI):** `bills.sc_removed` + `ChargeApplied` / `ChargeRemoved` is the M-3 fix (a re-applied charge after removal is the observed failure); `invoices_series_number` makes a duplicate number impossible even under a bug; `fiscal_outbox` is the F §3.5 queue — without it a lost or doubled invoice is a legal fault (PRA s.48) and a close would have to wait for the internet (E5 AC1); `invoice_buyers` exists only because the buyer tax ID must reach the authority and the receipt but must not enter the immutable, synced event log (G1-9); `invoice_taxes` keyed by (profile, tax code, class) and the invoice's `jurisdiction_id` / `currency_code` / `prices_include_tax` exist because a money table cannot gain them after merge and a credit must copy exactly what the original used (v0.7, §1.13); `shift_takings` keeps the shift-close and Z breakdown by taker at one statement (Q10) instead of a scan of payments; `billing_day` lets the fold key invoices to the open day (⚠️ R-44: `shifts` never reads it; `shifts.business_date` comes from `ShiftOpened.business_date`) without reading the engine state. No soft delete, no version columns: every change is an event.

**Audit refold** ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-8): `0023_billing.sql` ends with `DELETE FROM projection_checkpoints WHERE projection = 'audit'` so `audit` refolds from seq 0 with 05's maps (00 v1.10 H-2 safe).

**Retention:** none of these rows is pruned in the pilot (money and fiscal records); `invoice_buyers` is kept as long as its invoice, hub-only, never synced ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-8). `fiscal_credentials` is in the hub database, so it is in the encrypted USB backup (00 `encryptFile`) and nowhere else. `bill_print_jobs` rows whose 03c job was pruned are dropped by the same 03c retention call (7 days).

---

## §6. Commands, events and queries

All commands: `POST /commands/<kebab-name>`, staff session (`sessionKind: 'device'`) unless stated; **not queueable** (G0-1: money, PIN-capable); scope checks last (C8); idempotent by `command_id` (D32; a retransmit returns the stored response); statement counts exclude 00's fixed envelope. Every money result carries the recomputed totals so the screen never computes its own (§8). `bill_ref` in payloads = `{ bill_id }` or `{ seating_id }` or `{ order_id }` (exactly one; an `order_id` resolves to its seating or its `table_key`, ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-10 + S-9, owner R2); a reference to a bill not yet created creates it, B1).

**Common bill-open step ("ensure bill"),** used by every bill command: resolve `bill_ref` → `openBillOf` (1) → none: read the seating / order (`readOrder` / `seatings`, 1), refuse a seating that is not open (`409 TABLE_NOT_OPEN`) or an order that is cancelled (`409 BILL_NOT_OPEN { state: 'cancelled' }`), a takeaway / parking order not yet sent cannot exist (B16) → append `BillOpened` (with `prices_include_tax` = the mode in force now, H9-9, and the rounding rule in force now, H16-2) + `ChargeApplied { source: 'rule', rate_bp }` (`rate_bp` = `service_charge_bp[order_type]`; 0 → no `ChargeApplied`).

### Batch 1 — the bill (STOP for owner review after this batch)

#### 6.1 `GET /queries/bill?seating_id|order_id|bill_id`

- **Permission:** `take-payment` (its scope: `own_tables` → the caller holds the table / placed the order; `my_floors` → the table's floor) **or** `take-order` (read-only view for the order taker's check bill; ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-20): a payment's `reference` text only to `take-payment` holders whose scope covers the bill, everyone else gets `has_reference`). `403 PERMISSION_DENIED { reason: 'outside_scope' }` last.
- **Result:** `{ bill_id | null, state, scope, order_type, identifier, orders: [order_id], lines: [{ order_item_id, order_id, name, options, units, base_minor, tax_profile_id, tags: ['comp' | 'pending_void' | 'weight_needed' | 'tax_unresolved'], added_after_close }], service_charge: { rate_bp, removed }, discount | null, splits: [{ split_no, state, plan, payments: [{ payment_id, mode_id, mode_name, direction, amount_minor, tendered_minor, change_minor, has_reference (⚠️ v1.0.10: always present), reference (from the hub-only `payment_references`, v0.3 H1-20), taken_by_name, taken_by_staff_id (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.29 (R-90)) }], totals: { cash: Totals, digital: Totals, as_paid: Totals }, balance_minor, invoice: { invoice_id (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.29 (R-90)), invoice_ref, fiscal_number | null, fiscal_state } | null }], totals_default: Totals, buyer: { required, threshold_minor, waived }, blocks: [{ reason, split_no, details }], open_requests: [{ kind, alert_id }], prints: [{ job_id, doc_kind, state, printer_name (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.6 (R-35): from `documentJobsStatus`, row P-2), last_error }], check_printed_at, reopen_count, currency: { code, exponent }, prices_include_tax, as_of_seq }` with `Totals = { subtotal_minor, discount_minor, service_charge_minor, taxes: [{ tax_profile_id, tax_code, label, payment_class, rate_bp, rate_ref (⚠️ v1.0.10: test #14), base_minor, tax_minor }], rounding_minor, total_minor }` (⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-1, J-2): one tax row per (profile, tax code, class); currency and price mode for the client's formatting). Never a buyer tax ID. `blocks` lists every §3.3 rule that would refuse a close now.
- **Fields added in v1.0.6** ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.6 (R-29, design brief Q-4, Q-5, Q-18); every other field as above:
  - **`lines[].added_after_close: boolean`** (design brief Q-4) — `true` exactly when the bill has **at least one closed split** (`bill_splits.state = 'closed'`) **and** the line's `order_item_id` is **not** in `bill_discount_lines` for the bill (§5): that table holds the lines on the bill at the close that took the bill from no closed split to one (the first close, or the first after a `BillReopened`, which clears it, H2-19; filled from that `InvoiceIssued.discount_line_ids`, B7), so a line not in it joined the open remainder after a split of the current cycle closed (§3.8). Else `false` (a projected bill, a bill with no closed split, and every line after a reopen until the next close). The flag is per line: 02b never adds units to an existing line (a round adds new lines), so "units that joined later" are always whole lines. It states a fact for every line, closed splits included; the client shows the "new" mark only on lines of open splits (§8). Read in `openBillOf`'s statement as one scalar subquery (`SELECT json_group_array(order_item_id) FROM bill_discount_lines WHERE bill_id = b.bill_id`); "has a closed split" comes from the splits statement already counted; no extra statement, no per-line read.
  - **`blocks: [{ reason, split_no: int | null, details }]`** (design brief Q-5; was `[{ reason, ids }]`) — one entry for each §3.3 rule that would refuse `CloseSplit` **now**, per open split for the split rules (`split_no` set) and once for the bill rules (`split_no: null`), ordered by §3.3 rule order, then `split_no`. `reason` is exactly one of the strings below; `details` is exactly the `details` object (§9) of the error `CloseSplit` of that split would answer for that rule. `[]` = no rule refuses (a close still sends `expected_total_minor`, and `buyer` when `buyer.required` and not waived). Computed in memory from the reads already counted (bill row, orders, `orderLines`, `pendingVoids`, splits + payments, `alerts.listOpen`, config):

    | §3.3 rule | `reason` | `split_no` | `details` (= the §9 error's) |
    |---|---|---|---|
    | 1 | `bill_not_open` (the bill is CLOSED or absorbed; `BILL_NOT_FOUND` is the query's own 404, and a closed split shows as `splits[].state: 'closed'`, never as a block) | `null` | `{ bill_id, state }` (`BILL_NOT_OPEN`) |
    | 2 | `void_pending` | `null` | `{ reason: 'void_pending', order_item_ids }` (`BILL_CLOSE_BLOCKED`, ≤ 40 ids) |
    | 3 | `weight_pending` | the split | `{ reason: 'weight_pending', order_item_ids }` (`BILL_CLOSE_BLOCKED`) |
    | 3a | `tax_unresolved` | the split | `{ reason: 'tax_unresolved', order_item_ids }` (`BILL_CLOSE_BLOCKED`; may hold `'service_charge'`, R-1) |
    | 4 | `discount_pending` | `null` | `{ alert_id }` (`DISCOUNT_PENDING`) |
    | 5 | — (removed in v0.2, OQ-6; never a block) | — | — |
    | 6 | `buyer_id_required` (while `buyer.required` and not waived; the close passes when its payload carries a valid `buyer`) | `null` | `{ threshold_minor, net_minor, accepted_types }` (`BUYER_ID_REQUIRED`) |
    | 7 | — (never a block: the view's totals are the recomputed ones, so `BILL_TOTAL_CHANGED` comes only from a stale screen) | — | — |
    | 8 | `balance_due` | the split | `{ split_no, balance_minor }` (`BILL_BALANCE_DUE`) |
    | 8a | `shares_sum` | the last open split | `{ reason: 'shares_sum' }` (`BILL_SPLIT_INVALID`) |
    | 9 | — (no check at close; change was fixed at `AddPayment`) | — | — |

  - **`reopen_count: int ≥ 0`** (design brief Q-18) — `bills.reopen_count` (§5; +1 per `BillReopened`, §3.10); `0` for a projected bill. Read in `openBillOf`'s statement. The client shows "Reopened" while `reopen_count ≥ 1` and the bill is not CLOSED (§8).
- **Fields added in v1.0.29** ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.29 (R-90, G-93, E2E E-3); every other field as above:
  - **`splits[].invoice.invoice_id: string`** — the split's current sale invoice id (`bill_splits.invoice_id`, the `invoices` row the view already joins for `invoice_ref`); present whenever `invoice` is not `null`. It is the `ref_id` of `PrintBillDocument { doc: 'receipt', ref_id }` (§6.22: `ref_id` of a receipt or credit note is an **invoice id**, never the `invoice_ref`; unknown → `404 INVOICE_NOT_FOUND { invoice_id }`, §9), so a closed bill opened later offers **Reprint receipt** for each closed split.
  - **`splits[].payments[].taken_by_staff_id: string`** — next to `taken_by_name`, from `bill_payments.taken_by_staff_id` (the column the payments join already selects for the name). An id, not personal data. The client decides "own payment" for `RemovePayment` (§6.10, §8 Remove payment) by comparing it with the session's staff id, never by name; the hub's §6.10 check stays the authority.
- **Statements:** ≤ 9, fixed (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.29 (R-90): unchanged by the two fields, both from the splits + payments statement already counted; ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.6 (R-29): unchanged by the three fields; `openBillOf` returns the `bills` row with `reopen_count` and the `bill_discount_lines` ids in its one statement): `openBillOf` (1), orders of the bill (`seating_orders` or `bill_orders`, 1), `orderLines` (1), `pendingVoids` (1), splits + payments (1 join), invoices of the bill (1), `alerts.listOpen` for the bill's 05 kinds (1), `bill_print_jobs` + `documentJobsStatus` (2). Config and directory in memory. Pure `computeBill`.

#### 6.2 `GET /queries/open-bills?floor_id?`

- **Permission:** `take-payment` (rows filtered by its scope).
- **Result:** `{ bills: [{ bill_id | null, seating_id | null, order_id | null, identifier, order_type, state, total_default_minor, paid_minor, check_printed_at, opened_at, open_minutes, flagged: open_minutes > open_bill_flag_minutes, partly_paid, has_open_request }], as_of_seq }` — ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-10, H2-25): every non-closed `bills` row (not absorbed) whatever its seating's state, plus every open seating with an order that is neither billed nor cancelled ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-21) and every `bill_key` (takeaway / parking) with an unbilled, uncancelled order, one row per key, whether or not a bill row exists yet (projected rows have `bill_id: null`); ordered by `check_printed_at` desc nulls last, then `opened_at`. Bounded by the branch's open tables and orders (≤ 300), returned whole.
- **Statements:** 3 (open seatings ⋈ seating_orders ⋈ orders ⋈ per-profile line sums in one aggregate; unbilled non-table orders by `bill_key` with per-profile sums (⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-2): was per class); bills ⋈ payments sums). ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-18): the per-profile sums feed the pure `computeBill` in memory (half-up per tax of each profile's list, capped discount spread), so the list total equals the bill view's except pending voids, which the list does not subtract (shown as "before pending voids"). ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-9): `check_printed_at` = max(`bills.check_printed_at`, 02a `seatings.bill_requested_at`).

#### 6.3 `PrintCheckBill` — `{ bill_ref, printer_id? }`

- **Permission** ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-7): registry `take-order` (every Cashier and Order taker holds it by default), scope covering the table / order.
- **Script:** Zod → ensure bill → bill not CLOSED (`409 BILL_NOT_OPEN`) → lines + pending voids (2) → `computeBill` at the default class → printer: payload `printer_id` (must be active, `404 PRINTER_NOT_FOUND`) else `billPrinterFor` (1) → `buildCheckBillDoc` (banner `PROVISIONAL - NOT A TAX INVOICE`, no number, no QR, "total if paid in cash"; plus one line per digital rate when it differs) → `printDocument` (2) + `bill_print_jobs` (1) → append `CheckBillPrinted { bill_id, total_default_minor, line_count, printer_id | null, job_id | null, source: 'manual' }`. No printer → event with nulls, result `{ printed: false }`. ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.6 (R-30, row T-4): for a dine-in bill whose seating is not `CLOSED`, the same append also carries 02a **`TableMarksChanged { seating_id }`** (stream `table`, the seating's primary table). That needs the seating's `state` and `primary_table_id`, which no statement above returns when the payload names `printer_id` (`billPrinterFor` is then skipped), so it is **+1 statement** for a dine-in bill (one read of 02a `seatings` by `seating_id`, read-only; when no `printer_id` is sent, the same read gives `billPrinterFor` its table's zone / floor, so the total is +1 either way), so every map re-reads the printer mark (§6.4a).
- **Result:** `{ bill_id, job_id | null, printer_name | null, totals_default }`. **Statements:** ≤ 10 (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.6 (R-30, row T-4): was ≤ 9; +1 seating read for a dine-in bill). **Audit:** `check_bill_printed`.

#### 6.4 Fold `BillRequested` → check bill (live only, B17)

05 registers a live-only reaction on 02a's `BillRequested` (03c's job-fold pattern; skipped on rebuild): ensure-bill drafts are **not** possible in a fold, so the reaction prints ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-9) **the `bills` row's bill when one exists** (its `sc_bp` / `sc_removed`, discount, splits; a removed service charge is never printed back, M-3), else the **projected** bill (same `computeBill`, reads `orderLines` + `pendingVoids` in the transaction after `orders` and `tables` folded) at `billPrinterFor({ zone_id, floor_id })` of the seating's primary table and records the job in `bill_print_jobs` (`bill_id` = the bill if it exists, else NULL with `ref_id = seating_id`). The audit row is 02a's `BillRequested` mapped by 05 as `bill_requested` (§6.30). No 01d notice. A replayed queued `RequestBill` prints at replay time. **Statements:** ≤ 6 per `BillRequested` ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.10 (R-54): **≤ 10**, as §6.3 (03c's printing alone is 5).

#### 6.4a Table-tile marks (02a `registerTileMarks`, `TableMarksChanged`) ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.6 (R-30 (b), row T-4, design brief Q-11)

- **Reader** (registered at startup, §6.30; code in task 7, `apps/hub/src/billing/tile-marks.ts`): `billTileMarks(db, seating_ids)` → per seating `{ reopened, check_printed_at }` in **1 statement** for the whole list (`seatings` ⋈ `json_each(seating_ids)` LEFT JOIN `bills` on `seating_id` with `absorbed_into IS NULL AND state <> 'closed'`, grouped by seating; 02a `seatings` read-only, §4.1):
  - `reopened` = some such bill has `reopen_count ≥ 1` (a reopened bill that is open again, full edit or corrections);
  - `check_printed_at` = the larger of those bills' `check_printed_at` and the seating's `bill_requested_at` (the §6.2 rule, H6-9: `RequestBill` prints the check bill through §6.4), `null` when both are null.
  A seating with neither mark may be left out of the map (02a gives `false` / `null`). Never a total, a name or a money figure.
- **`TableMarksChanged { seating_id }`** (02a's event, ids only) is appended by 05 **in the transaction of the event that moves a mark**, for a dine-in bill whose seating is not `CLOSED`:
  - `CheckBillPrinted` (§6.3, task 11);
  - `BillReopened` (§6.13, task 19; ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.7 (R-43 / G-40): written by the `reopen_bill` `apply`, it carries the request's `alert_id`, 02a v1.1.11), **except** when the same transaction appends `SeatingReopened` (a full-edit reopen of a CLOSED bill): `SeatingReopened` is already a table frame in the client map frame list (row T-3 (6)), so a second event would add nothing (a design decision under R-30: one table frame per transaction is enough). A PARTLY_PAID reopen and a corrections reopen on an open seating write no `SeatingReopened`, so they append `TableMarksChanged`.
  No other event needs it: the bill-request print is 02a's own `BillRequested` frame, and a re-close (`BillClosed` / `BillClosedEmpty`) is a table frame already; marks of a CLOSED seating are never shown (the reader is asked for open seatings only).
- **Tests:** #6b (§10.1); 02a #65, #66.

#### 6.5 `SetSplits` — `{ bill_ref, mode: 'items' | 'amount' | 'equal' | 'none', splits?: [{ split_no, lines?: [{ order_item_id, units }], share_minor? }], n? }`

- **Permission:** `take-payment` (scope as §6.1).
- **Script:** Zod (`splits` 2–20, keyed by `split_no`; `lines` keyed by `order_item_id` per split; `n` 2–20 for `equal`; ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.11 (integrator reading): `n` above the split numbers still free in 1–20 → `400 VALIDATION_FAILED` with an issue at path `n`) → ensure bill → state OPEN or PARTLY_PAID (closed bill → `409 BILL_NOT_OPEN`) → lines + closed splits (2) → validate per §3.8 over the **open remainder** ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-6): closed split numbers are kept and may not appear (`422 BILL_SPLIT_INVALID { reason: 'split_closed' }`); item plan: every live unit not in a closed split exactly once (`reason: 'units_missing' | 'units_exceed', order_item_id`), deal whole and option child with its parent (`'deal_whole' | 'option_with_parent'`); amount plan: Σ shares = the open remainder's default-class total (`'shares_sum', expected_minor`); a split with live payments must keep its number (`409 BILL_HAS_PAYMENTS { split_nos }`); `none` = the open remainder as one split (only when at most one open split has payments) → append `BillSplitsSet { bill_id, mode, splits }`.
- **Result:** the bill view's `splits` (recomputed). **Statements:** ≤ 5. **Audit:** `splits_set` (counts only).

#### 6.6 `ApplyDiscount` — `{ bill_ref, kind: 'amount' | 'percent', value: int, reason_code, note? }` (approvable) ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-4: no `split_no`)

- **Permission:** `apply-discount` (`branch`), `approvable: true`.
- **Script:** Zod (`amount` ≥ 1 minor unit; `percent` in basis points 1–10 000; `reason_code` ∈ the branch's discount reasons, `note` required for `other`) → ensure bill → state PARTLY_PAID → `409 BILL_PARTLY_PAID { bill_id }` (the discount must be set before the first split closes, OQ-4) → state not OPEN → `409 BILL_NOT_OPEN` ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-12: this order, so `BILL_PARTLY_PAID` is reachable) → no open `discount` request on the bill (`409 DISCOUNT_PENDING { alert_id }`) → lines (2) → amount ≤ the whole bill's `S` and ≤ `max_discount_pct` of it (`422 DISCOUNT_TOO_LARGE { max_minor }`) → grant `allowed` → append `DiscountApplied { bill_id, kind, value, amount_minor (at apply), reason_code, has_note, note_id (⚠️ Changed in v0.5 (H3-5): the note in `bill_texts`), requester_staff_id, approver_staff_id: null, alert_id: null, replaces }` → grant `requires_approval` → `raiseMany` kind `discount` (`details { amount, reason, identifier }`, ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-6)), result `{ pending: { alert_id } }`. The `discount` kind's `apply` re-checks in the same order (PARTLY_PAID, then not OPEN), else throws (the answer is refused).
- **Result:** `{ applied: Totals } | { pending }`. **Statements:** ≤ 6 (+ 01d's raise bound). **Audit:** `discount_applied` / `discount_requested`.

#### 6.7 `RemoveDiscount` — `{ bill_id, reason_code, note? }`

- **Permission:** `apply-discount` (`allowed` or `requires_approval` grant both may remove; removal raises the price, never lowers it). Script: state PARTLY_PAID → `409 BILL_PARTLY_PAID` ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-4) → state not OPEN → `409 BILL_NOT_OPEN` ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-12) → discount present ( no discount → `200 { changed: false }`) → `DiscountRemoved`. **Statements:** 2. **Audit:** `discount_removed`.

#### 6.8 `RemoveServiceCharge` — `{ bill_ref, reason_code, note? }` (approvable) and `ApplyServiceCharge` — `{ bill_ref, note? }`

- **Remove:** `remove-service-charge`, approvable. Ensure bill → OPEN / PARTLY_PAID → already removed (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.13 (R-61): or never applied — the bill's frozen rate is 0) → `200 { changed: false }` → `allowed` → `ChargeRemoved { bill_id, charge: 'service', reason_code, has_note, note_id, requester_staff_id, approver_staff_id, alert_id }`; `requires_approval` → kind `remove_service_charge` (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.12 (R-59): while one is open for the bill, `200 { bill_id, pending: { alert_id } }` with that request — no second request; ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.12 (R-58): its `apply` re-check, the charge no longer applied → `409 SERVICE_CHARGE_NOT_APPLIED { bill_id }`). **Never** re-applied by any read, print, split, merge or reopen (B8; tests 18, 19).
- **Apply (re-add):** `remove-service-charge` `allowed` (the same right decides both ways) → `ChargeApplied { source: 'manual', rate_bp: service_charge_bp[order_type] }`; already applied → `{ changed: false }` (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.13 (R-61): also when the rate is 0 — the bill's frozen rate, or today's rate for a re-add after a removal — no `ChargeApplied`). **Statements:** ≤ 3 each (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.12 (R-60): so the results carry **no** recomputed totals — the screen re-reads `GET /queries/bill`). **Audit:** `service_charge_removed` / `service_charge_applied`.

#### 6.9 `AddPayment` — `{ bill_ref, split_no, mode_id, direction: 'in' | 'out', amount_minor, tendered_minor?, reference? }`

- **Permission:** `take-payment` (scope as §6.1).
- **Script:** Zod (`amount_minor` 1 … 10¹² and ≤ `Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER` (⚠️ Changed in v0.8.2 (H16-13): scaled for any currency); `reference` 1–60, never echoed in an error; `tendered_minor` only on a cash `in`, else `400 VALIDATION_FAILED { path: 'payload.tendered_minor' }`, ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-28)) → ensure bill → split open (`409 BILL_NOT_OPEN`) → mode active in settings (`404 PAYMENT_MODE_NOT_FOUND`) → the branch's adapter ≠ `none`, the bill's order type is fiscalized and the mode has no `authority_codes` entry for **that adapter** → `422 PAYMENT_MODE_NOT_FISCAL { mode_id, adapter_key }` (⚠️ Changed in v0.6.1 (H7-4), ⚠️ Changed in v0.6.2 (H8-3): non-fiscalized order types take any mode; ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-5): per the branch's adapter, was "no `pra_payment_mode`") → reference rule (`422 PAYMENT_REFERENCE_REQUIRED`) → money shift (B11; state `open`; a counted shift is already closed, owner Q-2): the device's open drawer shift, else the caller's own open shift (an order taker's shift has no drawer, Q9) → none → `409 SHIFT_NOT_OPEN { reason: 'no_shift' }` → lines, pending voids, payments of the split (3) → recompute the split with this payment included (§3.5, §3.6) → an `in` on a split whose balance **before this payment** is ≤ 0 (§3.5 (7)) → `422 PAYMENT_EXCEEDS_BALANCE { balance_minor: 0 }` (⚠️ Changed in v0.8.3 (H17-1), ⚠️ Changed in v1.0 (H18-1)) → an `in` `amount_minor` above **its due field** → `422 PAYMENT_EXCEEDS_BALANCE { balance_minor: <that due> }` ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-2), ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.2 (H16-1, H16-3) (⚠️ Changed in v0.8 (§25): every `in` ≤ its due field (⚠️ Changed in v0.8.1 (§26), H15-6: digital ≤ `card_due`, cash ≤ `cash_due`); under `applies_to: 'cash'` a cash amount off the step → `422 PAYMENT_CASH_STEP { step_minor }`, §3.5 (7); v0.7.2–v0.7.5 per-mode dues removed); `out` only when the balance is negative and `amount ≤ −balance` (`422 PAYMENT_EXCEEDS_BALANCE`; ⚠️ Changed in v0.8 (§25): **every** `out` ≤ its type's refund due — per type, up to what that type paid on the split ("jitna liya utna wapas"); a type that paid nothing → `422 PAYMENT_EXCEEDS_BALANCE { balance_minor: 0 }`; under `applies_to: 'cash'` a cash `out` is a step multiple (`PAYMENT_CASH_STEP`), §3.5 (7)) → append `PaymentAdded { payment_id (ULID), bill_id, split_no, mode_id, payment_class, direction, amount_minor, tendered_minor, change_minor (= tendered − amount, cash only), has_reference, taken_by_staff_id: caller, shift_id }` + `payment_references` row when a reference was given (1) ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-20); ⚠️ Changed in v0.6.1 (H7-3): an `out` that leaves a reopened, all-cancelled bill with every split balance 0 also appends `BillClosedEmpty`.
- **Result:** the split's `totals.as_paid`, `balance_minor`, `{ card_due_minor, cash_due_minor, card_refund_due_minor, cash_refund_due_minor, rounding_minor }` (⚠️ Changed in v0.8 (§25): the four dues exactly as §3.5 (7) defines them (⚠️ Changed in v0.8.1 (§26), H15-5, H15-6); `rounding_minor` = `R`, the untaxed adjustment) (~~v0.7.2–v0.7.6 per-mode dues~~ superseded by §25), `change_due_minor` (if cash tendered above), so the screen shows the recomputed tax at once (M-7). **Statements:** ≤ 7; ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.17 (R-73): ~~**≤ 8 on the empty-close path only**~~ — the `out` that leaves an all-cancelled bill with every split balance 0 appends `BillClosedEmpty` through `emptyCloseDrafts` (H7-3, §3.10); ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.20 (R-80): that empty-close path takes **≤ 10** — **8** as the base, **+1** when a `reference` text is written (`payment_references`), **+1** when `closeMany` closes open 05 requests on the bill; every other path stays ≤ 7. **Audit:** `payment_added` (mode, amount, shift; no names).

#### ~~6.9a `DeclareClassShares`~~ ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.1 (§26)

Removed with `pro_rata` (out of v1.0; §3.6, §12.7). The command, its event `SplitClassSharesDeclared` and its errors return in v1.1 if an authority requires pro rata (A6).

#### 6.10 `RemovePayment` — `{ payment_id, reason_code: 'wrong_mode' | 'wrong_amount' | 'customer_changed' | 'other', note? }`

- **Permission:** `take-payment`; removing a payment taken by **someone else** also needs `close-shift-for-others` (`403 PERMISSION_DENIED { reason: 'not_own_payment' }`). Payment exists (`404 PAYMENT_NOT_FOUND`), not removed, its split open (`409 BILL_NOT_OPEN`) → its shift OPEN, else `409 SHIFT_NOT_OPEN { reason: 'payment_shift_closed' }` ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-9, owner R4): a payment on a CLOSED shift (counting closes it, owner Q-2; e.g. a reopened earlier bill) is never removed; money back is an `AddPayment { direction: 'out' }` on the actor's own money shift today → `PaymentRemoved { payment_id, bill_id, split_no, reason_code, has_note }` (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.7 (R-44 / G-39): + **`shift_id`, `mode_id`, `payment_class`, `direction`, `amount_minor`, `change_minor`, `taken_by_staff_id`**, copied from the removed payment's row as its `PaymentAdded` wrote them — ids and amounts only, still no reference text — so the `shifts` fold reverses the payment from the event alone, §5). **Statements:** 2 (unchanged: the payment read already returns these columns). **Audit:** `payment_removed`.

#### 6.11 `WaiveBuyerId` — `{ bill_id, note? }` (approvable)

- **Permission:** `close-without-buyer-id`, approvable. Bill OPEN / PARTLY_PAID; the whole bill's net above the threshold (else `200 { changed: false, reason: 'not_required' }`); already waived → `{ changed: false }` → `allowed` → `BuyerIdWaived { bill_id, has_note, note_id, requester_staff_id, approver_staff_id: null, alert_id: null }` (⚠️ Changed in v0.5.1 (H4-4)); `requires_approval` → kind `close_without_buyer_id`. Holds for every later split of the bill and survives a reopen (the customer is the same). ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.16 (R-68): the `close_without_buyer_id` `apply` re-checks only that the bill is OPEN / PARTLY_PAID (§3.14), not the "already waived" step; so approving a request on a bill that was waived directly after the raise writes a **second** `BuyerIdWaived`. The fold is idempotent (the bill stays waived once); the audit shows two `buyer_id_waived` rows. **Statements:** ≤ 6 (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.16 (R-67): was ≤ 4; the net-vs-threshold check needs the whole-bill reads — `openBillOf`, `SPLITS_OF_BILL`, orders, `orderLines`, `pendingVoids` — = 5, and the note write adds one = 6; the kind's `apply` uses 1, a reject 0; same pattern as R-54, R-60). **Audit:** `buyer_id_waived`.

#### 6.12 `CloseSplit` — `{ bill_ref, split_no, expected_total_minor, buyer?: { name: string 1–60, tax_id: { type: string, value: string 1–40 } } }` (⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-4): was `id_number`) (⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-11): `bill_ref` with ensure bill, so a zero-total bill closes with no payment)

- **Permission:** `take-payment` (scope as §6.1). Not approvable (the money-risky parts are approved before: discount, waiver).
- **Validation:** ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-4): `tax_id.type` ∈ the jurisdiction's `accepted_types` and `tax_id.value`, after removing that type's `strip` characters, matches its `pattern` (Pakistan seed: `CNIC` 13 digits, dashes allowed, stored without; `NTN` 7–9 alphanumerics) → else `400 VALIDATION_FAILED { path: 'payload.buyer.tax_id.value' }` (or `.type`), message without the value. The handler never logs the payload.
- **Script:**
  1. Zod → `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` (00) → bill + split + payments + invoices of the bill (2).
  2. Orders of the bill (1) → `orderLines` (1) → `pendingVoids` (1) → `alerts.listOpen({ kind: 'discount', subject: bill })` and 02b `comp` requests of the orders (2) → dine-in: the seating's state and other open bills (1, for the `BillClosed` rule) ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-28).
  3. §3.3 rules 1–9 in order (first failure throws; nothing written); ~~v0.6.2 `pro_rata` class check~~ (v0.8.1 §26: removed); ⚠️ Changed in v0.6.2 (H8-3): on a fiscalized split, every live payment's mode has a code for the branch's adapter (read at close; ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-5)) → else `422 PAYMENT_MODE_NOT_FISCAL { mode_id, adapter_key }` (⚠️ Changed in v0.8.2 (H16-8): one status; remove it, or pay back and re-take). ⚠️ Changed in v0.6.1 (H7-3): when every order of the bill is cancelled and every split's balance is 0, the command appends **`BillClosedEmpty`** (+ 02a fold, open-request closes as a last close) and **no** `InvoiceIssued`, outbox row or receipt; result `{ bill_state: 'closed', empty: true }`.
  4. `computeSplit` with the split's payments → totals, tax rows, rounding; change = Σ the split's cash payments' `change_minor` (each already on its own shift) ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-2, H1-3) — this Σ is `InvoiceIssued.change_minor`; ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.30 (R-91): the **result's** `change_minor` counts only the split's cash payments added **after the bill's latest `BillReopened`** (their `PaymentAdded` seq above the latest `BillReopened` seq on the bill stream; on a never-reopened bill every one, so it equals the invoice's), because the change of a payment carried across a reopen was given out at the earlier close; read in the post-fold read (step 7), no extra statement. ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.31 (R-92): the same figure is the receipt's Change line (step 7).
  5. Number: `SELECT COALESCE(MAX(number), 0) + 1 FROM invoices WHERE series = 'sale'` (1); `invoice_ref = invoice_prefix + 'S' + zero-padded number` (⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-1); a credit uses `'C'`); unique over both series (`invoices_ref`).
  6. Drafts: `InvoiceIssued { invoice_id, series: 'sale', number, invoice_ref, bill_id, split_no, business_date, issued_at, order_type, jurisdiction_id, currency_code, prices_include_tax, lines: [{ order_item_id, item_id, name, units, unit_price_minor, base_minor, discount_minor, tax_profile_id, fiscal_category (⚠️ v0.7.2 H9-4), taxes: [{ tax_code, payment_class, rate_bp, base_minor, tax_minor }] }] (⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-4), ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-2): per line, each (profile, tax) row's tax is spread over the profile's lines by line net with `apportionMinor`, Σ per tax = the invoice row; ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.3 (R-18 / G-8): **when the service charge shares a (profile, tax) row with taxed lines** (exclusive mode) **the charge's tax for that row is rounded on its own** — half up on its base, a compound tax's base per R-19 — **and the lines get the row minus the charge's part**, spread over the profile's lines by line net with `apportionMinor`, so Σ lines + charge = the row exactly; **in inclusive mode** the charge's part is §3.5 (6)(a) and the lines get the rest of the row, §3.5 (6)(b); **if the charge's profile has no lines** it takes the whole row; ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.3 (R-17 / R-7): **`lines[].unit_price_minor` = `round_half_up(02b line_total_minor ÷ the 02b line's units)`** (options included); the invoice line's own `units` and `base_minor` are the split's share and need not equal `units × unit_price_minor`; a weighed line (units = 1) = its whole weighed total; a `deal_part` = 0; read from 02b's `order_lines.unit_price_minor` (02b v1.0.16, R-7); ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.3 (R-19): **`base_minor` of each line tax and service-charge tax** (`lines[].taxes[]`, `service_charge.taxes[]`) — **exclusive:** the line net (base − discount share); **inclusive:** the line net − that line's taxes of the profile; **compound (any mode):** the base above **plus** that line's earlier taxes (seq order) of the profile, so inclusive compound = line net − the line's taxes of the profile + its earlier taxes; **the service charge's base is its amount** (never tax-inclusive, §3.5 (4)) in every mode; a compound tax adds the charge's earlier taxes of the profile; ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.4 (R-20): **in inclusive mode only** (exclusive keeps J-2 / R-18 unchanged) a line's taxes of one profile never sum above its net: the profile's rows are spread in seq order, each with `allocateCappedMinor(row's lines part, headrooms)`, the headroom (line net − that line's taxes of the profile so far) being both cap and weight, so the first row spreads by line net as in J-2 and a left-over unit moves to the next line; Σ per row stays the row and no inclusive base is negative (the total never exceeds Σ headrooms because `TI_p ≤ A_p`)), service_charge: { amount_minor, tax_profile_id, taxes: [{ tax_code, payment_class, rate_bp, base_minor, tax_minor }] } \| `null` (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.4 (R-21): `null` also when an applied charge comes to 0 (rate 0, net 0, or the rounded amount 0), as when removed or absent; ⚠️ v0.7.3 H10-8; ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.2 (P0.1b, R-12 / G-27): `null` when the split has no service charge), subtotal_minor, discount_minor, service_charge_minor, taxes: [{ tax_profile_id, tax_code, label, seq, compound, payment_class, rate_bp, base_minor, tax_minor, rate_ref }], rounding_minor, total_minor, payment_class, mixed_rule, payment_ids, change_minor, discount_line_ids (string[] on the bill's first close, else `null`, ⚠️ Changed in v0.5 (H3-8); ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.6 (R-29): "first close" = **the close that takes the bill from no closed split to one** — the bill's first close, or the first after a `BillReopened`, which clears `bill_discount_lines` (H2-19); as §6.1 `added_after_close` and test #4b), fiscal: { adapter_key, pos_id, required (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.8 (R-46): `true` only when the order type's `fiscalize` is on, the invoice is issued after the jurisdiction setting's `switched_at` **and** the branch adapter is not `none` — exactly the complement of §3.15 "Not fiscalized"; never `true` with adapter `none`, R-8 / G-1), capabilities, credit_support, rounding (H16-2), receipt: { fiscal_number_label, tax_reg_labels, show_qr, qr_url_template, buyer_id_on_receipt }, seller_registrations: [{ code, number }], payment_codes: [{ payment_id, authority_code \| null }] } (⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-6): was `authority`; ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.2 (P0.1b, R-12 / G-3): a jurisdiction whose `receipt_fiscal` is `null` gives `receipt` with `fiscal_number_label: null` and empty lists, mapped by the billing-settings snapshot builder (§11 P0.9); ⚠️ Changed in v0.7.2 (H9-6): the **jurisdiction snapshot** in force at issue, copied into `invoices.doc_json`; a credit copies its original's; credits, credit notes and reprints read it, never today's snapshot), has_buyer_id, buyer_id_refused, closed_by }`; the invoice's `lines` carry each line's invoiced units and share, folded into `closed_split_units` (B15) ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-6); the first close freezes the discount amount and lists `discount_line_ids` (the bill's lines at that moment, folded into `bill_discount_lines`, Q-1) (B7); ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.7 (R-41 / G-38): at that close (the one that takes the bill from no closed split to one, R-29), **right after `InvoiceIssued`**, the drafts carry **`DiscountFrozen { bill_id, frozen_minor, line_ids }`**: `frozen_minor` = the whole bill's discount `D_bill` that this close's `computeSplit` spread (§3.5 (2): the amount, or the percent of Σ bill bases, capped), 0 when the bill has no discount; `line_ids` = the same set as `InvoiceIssued.discount_line_ids` (kept unchanged; `bill_discount_lines` is still folded from it); its fold is the only writer of `bills.discount_json.frozen_minor` (§5); a later close, or the empty close (`BillClosedEmpty`, no `InvoiceIssued`), appends none; after a `BillReopened` (which clears the freeze) the next first close appends it again; no extra command statement (the amount comes from step 4, the set from step 2); when this is the **last open split**: 02b `OrdersBilled { order_ids, bill_id }` (stream `order`, first order) and, for dine-in when the seating is not `CLOSED` and holds no other open bill (⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-10 + S-9, owner R2): was "first full close only"), 02a `BillClosed { seating_id }` (stream `table`, primary table); `AlertClosed` drafts (`obsolete`, note `bill_closed`) for open `comp` **and `remove_comp`** (⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-2)) requests on the **units this split invoices** at every split close ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-23), and at the last close for the remaining `comp` requests on the bill's lines (02b To 05) and any open 05 request on the bill.
  7. Append (00) → projections fold (billing: invoice, outbox row; shifts: takings, change; 02b `billed_at`; 02a NEEDS_CLEANING) → `invoice_buyers` insert when `buyer` is present (1) → receipt: `billPrinterFor({ device_id })` (1), `buildReceiptDoc` (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.31 (R-92): its Change = the result's `change_minor` of step 4 (R-91), not `InvoiceIssued.change_minor`; fiscal block = "FISCAL NUMBER PENDING" when fiscalized (§3.15) (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.2 (P0.1b, R-8 / G-1): was "when `required`"), B21, its fields from the jurisdiction's `receipt_fiscal`; buyer name and the **full** buyer tax ID (with its type label) on this customer's receipt only, when the jurisdiction's `receipt_fiscal.buyer_id_on_receipt` is true (⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-5, Q-1, owner 2026-11-18 11:08–11:09 PKT)); ⚠️ Changed in v0.6.1 (H7-2, Q-2): the `Doc` carries the buyer block as a placeholder `{ fill: 'buyer_id', ref: invoice_id, last4 }` (⚠️ Changed in v0.6.2 (H8-7)), filled at send by 03c's worker through 05's filler (row P-5), so `print_jobs.payload` stores the last 4 only; drawer block when a cash payment exists) → `printDocument` (2) + `bill_print_jobs` (1).
  8. COMMIT → respond → WS (`InvoiceIssued` to the `bill`-stream audience of §6.30, ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.2 (H16-6); `BillClosed` per 02a).
- **Result:** `{ invoice_id, invoice_ref, total_minor, change_minor, bill_state, receipt: { job_id | null, printer_name | null }, fiscal_state: 'pending' | 'not_required' }`. ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.30 (R-91): `change_minor` = Σ (tendered − amount) of the split's live cash `in` payments added after the bill's latest `BillReopened` (all of them on a never-reopened bill; step 4) — the change due **now**; `InvoiceIssued.change_minor`, ~~the receipt,~~ the shift, the Z and every event keep the Σ over every cash payment of the split. ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.31 (R-92): the receipt's Change line prints this same figure (§3.16).
- **Statements:** ≤ 18, fixed (no per-line or per-payment statement; ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.30 (R-91): unchanged — the payment ids since the latest `BillReopened` are a scalar subquery of the post-fold read already counted (bill state, outbox row state, printer); measured 13 on a dine-in last split with a receipt). Not an engine command (no `EngineState` change) — but it appends events in a transaction with folds: COMMIT-failure test asserts no outbox row, no print job, no buyer row (§10 #36).
- **Audit:** `invoice_issued` (number, total, class, has_buyer_id only).

#### 6.13 `ReopenBill` — `{ bill_id, reason_code ∈ BILL_REOPEN_REASONS, note? }` (approvable)

- **Permission:** `reopen-bill` (`branch`), approvable.
- **Step order** ⚠️ Changed in v0.5.1 (H4-1): (1) read the bill, its invoices and **its order set** (`bill_orders`, or for PARTLY_PAID the bill's current orders); (2) read the seating / key state and the other bills and unbilled orders on it; (3) decide the mode (§3.10) with the set from (1) excluded; (4) build the drafts, `BillReopened { order_ids }` first, so its fold fills `bill_orders` before any later read in the same transaction. ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.7 (R-40 / G-37): `BillReopened` also carries `mode`, the result of (3); its fold sets `bills.reopen_mode` in the same step.
- **Script:** Zod → bill CLOSED or PARTLY_PAID (`409 BILL_NOT_OPEN { state }`) → an open `reopen_bill` request on the bill (`alerts.listOpen`, 1) → `409 BILL_REOPEN_PENDING { alert_id }` → invoices and the bill's order set (2) → ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-1, J-6): a sale invoice whose outbox row is `pending` / `acked` and whose **snapshot** (H9-6) has `capabilities.credit = false` or `credit_support = 'none'` → `409 BILL_REOPEN_NOT_SUPPORTED { invoice_id }` (never for the Pakistan seed or `none`; the `reopen_bill` `apply` re-checks it on approve) → the seating's state (dine-in), or the `bill_key`'s other bills (takeaway / parking), and the unbilled orders outside the set (1) ⚠️ Changed in v0.5.2 (H5-4) → mode per §3.10 ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-10 + S-9, owner R2) → `allowed` → drafts per §3.10 (+ 02a `SeatingReopened { seating_id, bill_id }` in full-edit mode; ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.6 (R-30, row T-4): + 02a `TableMarksChanged { seating_id }` for a dine-in bill on a not-`CLOSED` seating when no `SeatingReopened` is appended, §6.4a (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.7 (R-43 / G-40): `{ seating_id, alert_id }` when the `reopen_bill` `apply` writes it, so it passes 01d `checkAlertIds` like every other `apply` draft; no `alert_id` key on the direct path); its stream is the seating's primary table and its condition the seating's state, both from the script's seating read below — the `(1)` statement after the `BILL_REOPEN_NOT_SUPPORTED` check, which reads the seating row (`state` for the mode, §3.10, and `primary_table_id`, the stream `SeatingReopened` already needs) — so +0 statements; ≤ 8 unchanged): `BillReopened { bill_id, mode: 'full' | 'corrections' (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.7 (R-40 / G-37): the mode this script decided in step (3), §3.10; the billing fold sets `bills.reopen_mode` from it, §5, so the `bills_live_*` indexes and every later read in the same transaction see it; `apply` writes the mode it decides at answer time), order_ids (the bill's current order set; folded into `bill_orders` with `INSERT OR IGNORE`, ⚠️ Changed in v0.5 (H3-2)), reason_code, has_note, note_id, requester_staff_id, approver_staff_id: null, alert_id: null }`, one `InvoiceCredited { invoice_id (new), alert_id (null on the direct path, ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-1)), series: 'credit', number (MAX+1 of credit, 1), invoice_ref (`prefix + 'C' + number`, ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-1)), ref_invoice_id, ref_invoice_ref, bill_id, split_no, business_date (today), closed_by (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.7 (task 5 / integrator): **the reopen's requester**, `requester_staff_id` of this `BillReopened` — on the approval path the request's requester —, never the original's closer: the credit is issued by this reopen, and only the amounts, jurisdiction, currency, price mode, lines, tax rows and `fiscal` snapshot below are copied), …the original's amounts, its `jurisdiction_id`, `currency_code`, `prices_include_tax`, lines, tax rows and `fiscal` snapshot (H9-6) copied (never re-read from today's rates, B5), fiscal }` per current sale invoice, `OrdersUnbilled` when CLOSED → `requires_approval` → kind `reopen_bill` (values `{ bill_id, invoice_ids, credit_invoice_ids, reason_code, note_id, has_note }` (⚠️ Changed in v0.5.1 (H4-4); ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.9 (R-47): `reason_code` added, `apply` copies it to `BillReopened`) — the current sale invoices at raise; ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.15 (R-66, 01d AD-91: `apply` mints no ids): **`credit_invoice_ids`** = ULIDs minted at raise, as `note_id` is, one per `invoice_ids` entry in the same order (Zod: same length, distinct); on approve `apply` uses `credit_invoice_ids[i]` as the `InvoiceCredited.invoice_id` of the credit for `invoice_ids[i]`; the direct path mints its own ids, as before; on **approve** `apply` throws, so the answer is refused, when they differ at answer time (⚠️ Changed in v0.6.2 (H8-9); a reject always succeeds; ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.15 (R-66): this re-check is the only outcome of a mismatch — an invoice added, missing or replaced since the raise refuses the answer, so a credit id is never used for an invoice other than the `invoice_ids` entry at its index; ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.17 (R-74): such a **stale** request — its invoices changed between raise and approve — is refused by `apply` with **`409 BILL_NOT_OPEN { bill_id, state }`**, the existing §9 code; no new code), ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-17); every `apply` draft carries the request's `alert_id`, H2-1; `apply` builds the same drafts read-only, numbers from the answer transaction's read view). On both paths the credit note is printed by the live-only fold of `InvoiceCredited` (§3.16), never by the command, so it prints once.
- **Result:** `{ reopened: { mode: 'full' | 'corrections', credits: [{ invoice_ref, total_minor }] } } | { pending: { alert_id } }` ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-26). **Statements:** ≤ 8. **Audit:** `bill_reopened` (with the mode; ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.7 (R-40 / G-37): read from `BillReopened.mode`), `invoice_credited`.

#### 6.14 `RemoveComp` — `{ order_item_id, units?, reason_code ∈ COMP_REMOVE_REASONS, note? }` (approvable)

- **Permission:** `remove-comp` (`branch`), approvable.
- **Script:** `readOrder` (1) → order not billed (`409 ORDER_BILLED`), line comped (`409 LINE_NOT_COMPED { order_item_id }`), `units ≤ comped_units` (`400`) → line guard list (02b, 1: a void pending on it → `409 VOID_PENDING`; units in a closed split → `409 LINE_IN_CLOSED_SPLIT`) ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-6) → `planCompRemoval` (02b) → `allowed` → `LineCompRemoved { …, approver_staff_id: null, alert_id: null }` (02b event, 05 writes it); `requires_approval` → kind `remove_comp` (dedupe `remove_comp:<order_item_id>`).
- **Result:** `{ removed: { units, comp_minor } } | { pending }`. **Statements:** ≤ 4 (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.19 (R-79): ≤ 4 **besides the line-guard list** — the registered guards' statements are not counted, §3.9, row Q-7; the "1" above is the list's, not the command's; measured: direct path 3, or 4 with a note; request path 4, besides 01d's raise). **Audit:** `comp_removed`.

### Batch 2 — shifts, petty cash, drawer

#### 6.15 `OpenShift` — `{ float_minor, as_drawer: boolean }`

- **Permission:** `manage-own-shift`.
- **Script:** Zod (`float_minor` 0 … 10¹², ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.2 (H16-13)) → business day open (`409 BUSINESS_DAY_NOT_OPEN`, 01a's code) → caller has no open shift (`409 SHIFT_ALREADY_OPEN { shift_id }`) → `as_drawer`: the device is a `cashier_kiosk` or a tablet with a printer route (else `422 DRAWER_DEVICE_INVALID { device_id }`), and no open shift holds this device (`409 DRAWER_IN_USE { shift_id, staff_name }`) → `ShiftOpened { shift_id, staff_id, drawer_device_id | null, float_minor, business_date }` → drawer kick (`printDocument` with `{ drawer: true }` on the device route; ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.11 (R-57): **only the device's own** `bill_routes` device row with an active printer — no zone / floor / default fallback; with none, no kick and the shift still opens) when `float_minor > 0`.
- **Result:** `{ shift_id, opened_at }`. **Statements:** ≤ 5. **Audit:** `shift_opened`.

#### 6.16 `GET /queries/shift?shift_id?`

- **Permission** ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-7): registry `manage-own-shift`; another person's shift additionally needs `close-shift-for-others`, checked in the handler with `ctx.directory.resolve` (`403 PERMISSION_DENIED { permission: 'close-shift-for-others' }`).
- **Result:** `{ shift_id, staff_name, state, is_drawer, opened_at, float_minor, takings: [{ mode_id, mode_name, payment_class, payments_count (⚠️ v1.0.10 R-51), in_minor, out_minor, change_minor, by: [{ taken_by_name, payments_count, in_minor }] }], petty: [{ entry_id, kind, amount_minor, category_name, paid_to (read from the hub-only `bill_texts`, never from an event, ⚠️ Changed in v0.5 (H3-5)), advance_staff_name, settled }], open_advances_minor, handovers: [...], counted_minor | null, expected_minor | null, variance_minor | null }`. **Blind rule (B23):** `expected_minor` and `variance_minor` are `null` unless `counted_at` is set (a handover row shows the expected of its own count only); card / online takings **are** shown (they are checked against the terminal, story 7 AC5); the cash line shows only the count of cash payments, never their sum, before the count (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.10 (R-51): the count is each row's `payments_count` — live payments of that shift × mode, `in` and `out` — and each `by[]` entry's own `payments_count`; before the count a cash row's `in_minor`, `out_minor`, `change_minor` and every `by[].in_minor` are `null`). **Statements:** 4.

#### 6.17 `CountShift` — `{ shift_id, counted_minor, note? }` (⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (Q-2, owner 2026-11-18 11:08–11:09 PKT): counts **and closes**)

- **Permission** ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-7): registry `manage-own-shift`; another person's shift additionally needs `close-shift-for-others`, checked in the handler with `ctx.directory.resolve` (`403 PERMISSION_DENIED { permission: 'close-shift-for-others' }`).
- **Script:** shift OPEN (`409 SHIFT_NOT_OPEN { reason: 'not_open' }`) → `ShiftCounted { shift_id, counted_minor, expected_minor, variance_minor, by_staff_id, variance_alert_id: string | null }` (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.7 (R-42 / G-39): `variance_alert_id`) (expected from the running fold) **+ `ShiftClosed { shift_id, closed_by, has_note, note_id, alert_id: null, approver_staff_id: null }`** in the same transaction (the drawer is free at once) → `|variance| > variance_threshold_minor` → the fold sets `variance_state = 'pending'` and `raiseMany` kind `shift_variance` (`details { amount: variance_minor }`, approvers §3.14; dedupe `variance:<shift_id>`) ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.7 (R-42 / G-39): the **command** compares |variance| with `variance_threshold_minor` and raises the request in this transaction; `ShiftCounted.variance_alert_id` = that request's `alert_id`, `null` when |variance| ≤ threshold (no request); the `shifts` fold sets `variance_state = 'pending'` and `variance_alert_id` from the event alone (§5) and never reads the threshold; it is a sign-off only (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.7 (R-44 / G-39): answered by the `shift_variance` `apply`, §3.14, whose `ShiftVarianceSignedOff` carries `business_date` = the open business day in **that answer** transaction, ~~possibly days after this count~~ ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.18 (R-76): always the day of this count, since the day cannot close while the request is pending, §6.25) ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (Q-2, owner 2026-11-18 11:08–11:09 PKT). ⚠️ Changed in v0.6.1 (H7-6): the shift's open `petty_cash` requests are closed `obsolete` (note `shift_closed`) in the same transaction and listed in the result.
- **Result:** `{ shift_id, closed: true, expected_minor, variance_minor, sign_off: { alert_id } | null, closed_requests: string[] }` (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.18 (R-78): `closed_requests` = the `alert_id`s of the shift's `petty_cash` requests closed `obsolete` above, `[]` when none) — the first time the server reveals expected. ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.18 (R-78): the `shift_variance` request's **requester is the counter** (`by_staff_id`), so any **other** holder of the approving right (`close-shift-for-others`, holders mode, §3.14) answers it; a Manager counting his own shift needs another holder (Owner). **Statements:** ≤ 4 (+ 01d's raise bound). **Audit:** `shift_counted`, `shift_closed`.

#### 6.18 `CloseShift` — `{ shift_id, skip_count: true, note? }` (⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (Q-2, owner 2026-11-18 11:08–11:09 PKT): only the no-count close; a counted close is `CountShift`)

- **Permission** ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-7): registry `manage-own-shift`; another person's shift additionally `close-shift-for-others` (handler); `skip_count` → also `skip-blind-count` (`403 PERMISSION_DENIED { reason: 'skip_not_granted' }`).
- **Script:** shift exists (`404 SHIFT_NOT_FOUND`), OPEN (`409 SHIFT_NOT_OPEN { reason: 'not_open' }`) → `skip-blind-count` granted (`403 PERMISSION_DENIED { reason: 'skip_not_granted' }`) → `ShiftCountSkipped` + `ShiftClosed { shift_id, closed_by, has_note, note_id, alert_id: null, approver_staff_id: null }`. No variance, no request. ⚠️ Changed in v0.6.1 (H7-6): open `petty_cash` requests of the shift closed `obsolete` as in §6.17. Open advances are listed in the result, never blocking. (⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (Q-2, owner 2026-11-18 11:08–11:09 PKT): v0.6's "not counted → `SHIFT_NOT_COUNTED`" and v0.5.2's repeated-close `SHIFT_VARIANCE_PENDING` (H5-5) are gone: a counted shift is already closed.)
- **Result:** ~~`{ closed: { shift_id } } | { pending }`~~ ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.18 (R-78): `{ closed: { shift_id }, closed_requests: string[], open_advances: [{ entry_id, advance_staff_id, amount_minor }] }` — `closed_requests` as §6.17; `open_advances` = the shift's `petty_cash` advances with `settled_at IS NULL`, listed, never blocking; no `{ pending }` branch (a skip close raises no request, v0.6 Q-2). **Statements:** ≤ 5. **Audit:** `shift_closed` / `shift_count_skipped`.

#### 6.19 `HandoverCount` — `{ shift_id, counted_minor }`

- **Permission** ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-7): registry `manage-own-shift`; another person's drawer shift additionally `close-shift-for-others` (handler). Drawer shift OPEN → `HandoverCounted { shift_id, counted_minor, expected_minor, variance_minor, by_staff_id }` (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.7 (R-42 / G-39): no `variance_alert_id` — a handover raises no sign-off, §3.11, §3.14); the shift stays OPEN; the result reveals expected (it is a count). ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.21 (R-81): the `shifts` projection makes the handover's `at` strictly increasing from `recorded_at`: its fold writes **`shift_handovers.at` = `max(recorded_at, last_at + 1)`** in ms, `last_at` = the greatest `shift_handovers.at` of this shift (no row → `recorded_at`), in its single insert, so any number of handovers in one hub millisecond each get their own row (§3.11; §5 key `(shift_id, at)`, no migration) and a rebuild gives the same rows. No envelope field is redefined (`occurred_at` device clock, `recorded_at` hub, foundation §4.1); the command is unchanged. **Statements:** ≤ 2 (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.18 (R-78): was 2; the actual count is 1) (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.21 (R-81): unchanged; the fold stays 1 statement). **Audit:** `handover_counted`.

#### 6.20 `RecordPettyCash` — `{ kind, amount_minor, category_id, paid_to?, advance_staff_id?, note? }` (approvable) and `SettlePettyAdvance` — `{ entry_id, spent_minor, returned_minor, note? }`

- **Record:** `record-petty-cash`, approvable. Caller's money shift (B11; cash; state `open`) → else `409 SHIFT_NOT_OPEN` → category active (`422 CONFIG_REFERENCE_INVALID { field: 'category_id', reason: 'not_found' }`) → advance needs `advance_staff_id` in the directory (`422 … 'advance_staff_id'`) (⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-15): no check against the shift's expected cash — a refusal would let a cashier binary-search it; an overdraw shows as variance at the count) → grant `allowed` and `amount ≤ petty_cash_limit_minor` → `PettyCashAdvanced` / `PettyCashOut` / `PettyCashIn { entry_id, shift_id, amount_minor, category_id, has_paid_to, advance_staff_id, has_note, requester_staff_id, approver_staff_id: null, alert_id: null }` + drawer kick → else kind `petty_cash` (values carry the entry **without text** — `entry_id`, amounts, ids, `has_note` / `has_paid_to`; the texts go to `bill_texts` under `entry_id` at raise, ⚠️ Changed in v0.5 (H3-5); `apply` returns the event; the drawer kick on approval is the requester's next `OpenCashDrawer`, not automatic).
- **Settle:** `record-petty-cash` `allowed`. Advance open (`404 PETTY_CASH_NOT_FOUND`, `409 PETTY_ADVANCE_SETTLED`), `spent + returned = amount` (`422 PETTY_SETTLE_MISMATCH { amount_minor }`) → caller's money shift (the returned cash lands there) → `PettyCashSettled { entry_id, shift_id, spent_minor, returned_minor, has_note, note_id }` (⚠️ Changed in v0.5.1 (H4-4): its own `note_id` ULID, so it never collides with the advance's `(entry_id, 'note')` row).
- **Statements:** ≤ 4 each. **Audit:** `petty_cash` (kind, amount, category; `paid_to` and names never in the audit detail).

#### 6.21 `OpenCashDrawer` — `{ reason_code: 'change_for_customer' | 'check_cash' | 'other', note? }`

- **Permission:** `open-cash-drawer`. The device is a drawer of a shift in state `open` (`409 SHIFT_NOT_OPEN { reason: 'no_drawer_shift' }`) → `DrawerOpened { shift_id, device_id, reason_code, has_note, note_id }` → `printDocument` (`{ drawer: true }`) ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.18 (R-75): **no `bill_print_jobs` row** (§3.16). **Result:** ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.18 (R-75): `{ shift_id, printed }` — `printed` = a kick job was queued. **Statements:** ≤ 4 (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.18 (R-75): was 4; 4 with a note, 3 without, 1 with no own route or off-drawer). **Audit:** `drawer_opened`.

### Batch 3 — day close, reports, printing, fiscal

#### 6.22 `PrintBillDocument` — `{ doc: 'receipt' | 'credit_note', ref_id, printer_id? }` and `PrintReport` — `{ doc: 'x_report' | 'z_report', ref_id?, printer_id? }` ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-7: split)

- **Permission:** `PrintBillDocument` → `take-payment`; `PrintReport` → `view-reports` (one registry permission each, ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-7)). ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.25 (G-87): no plan task built `PrintReport`; task 27 built `PrintBillDocument` only. Plan row **27b** (`docs/plans/05-plan.md` §3) builds it, after 26b and 25; the rules here are unchanged.
- **`PrintReport` readings** ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.26 (R-87, owner 2026-11-20, task 27b):
  1. **Z by date.** A Z `ref_id` is the `business_date`; the latest `z_no` of that date prints, otherwise `404 Z_REPORT_NOT_FOUND { business_date }`.
  2. **Z with no `ref_id`.** Prints the latest Z (greatest `z_no`); with no Z at all, `404 Z_REPORT_NOT_FOUND { business_date: null }`.
  3. **X takes no `ref_id`.** `doc: 'x_report'` with a `ref_id` → `400 VALIDATION_FAILED`.
  4. **X needs an open day.** An X with no open business day or no branch config → `409 BUSINESS_DAY_NOT_OPEN`.
  5. **Printer order.** The payload's `printer_id` (`404 PRINTER_NOT_FOUND` when unknown or archived), then the `report` route, then `default`, then the result `printed: false` plus a log line with ids only.
  6. **Job row.** The job id is `printDocument`'s generated id. The `bill_print_jobs` row has `doc_kind 'report'`, `bill_id` NULL and `ref_id` = the business date.
  7. **Statements.** X print ≤ 13 (12 with `printer_id`): the X build 8 (§6.26), the route pick 2 (1 with `printer_id`), print plus the job row 3. Z print ≤ 9.
- **Script:** the invoice (`404 INVOICE_NOT_FOUND`) / the Z (`404 Z_REPORT_NOT_FOUND`; ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.24 (R-85 (1)): `ref_id` is the Z's `business_date`, and a date with two Zs prints **the latest `z_no` of that date**; the earlier Z stays readable through the Z list, §6.26; details stay `{ business_date }`) / the open day's X from the projections (≤ 6; ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.26 (R-87): the X build is 8, §6.26) → builder (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.31 (R-92): a receipt's Change line = the change due at that invoice's close, §3.16: the invoice's cash `in` payments whose `PaymentAdded` comes after the bill's latest `BillReopened` before the invoice's `InvoiceIssued` (seq below its outbox row's `ord`), all of them on a never-reopened bill; read with the receipt's payments in the same statement, so a reprint of the same invoice always prints the same figure and an earlier, credited invoice prints what it printed; ⚠️ Changed in v0.7.2 (H9-6): labels, QR template and registrations from the invoice's `fiscal` snapshot, never today's; a receipt reprint is marked `REPRINT`, shows the fiscal number + QR once `FiscalNumberAssigned` exists, else "FISCAL NUMBER PENDING", and for a `rejected` invoice "FISCAL NUMBER NOT ISSUED" (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.2 (P0.1b, R-11), §3.16); buyer from `invoice_buyers`, the full buyer tax ID on that invoice's reprint only (rebuilt from `invoice_buyers` through the same filler, never from the print queue, ⚠️ Changed in v0.6.1 (H7-2, Q-2); a credit note prints the last 4, Q-3), ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-5, Q-1, owner 2026-11-18 11:08–11:09 PKT)) → printer (payload, else route) → `printDocument` + `bill_print_jobs` → no event (a reprint changes no money; the audit of reprints is 03c's `document` job list). **Statements:** ≤ 9 (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.31 (R-92): unchanged, the Change flag rides the payments read; ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.26 (R-87): `PrintReport` X ≤ 13, 12 with `printer_id`; Z ≤ 9; reading 7 above).

#### 6.23 `RetargetBillPrint` — `{ job_id, printer_id }`

- **Permission:** `take-order` (⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-7)). `bill_print_jobs` row exists (`404 PRINT_JOB_NOT_FOUND`) → `retargetDocumentJob` (03c row P-2; `409 PRINT_JOB_NOT_PENDING` when printed / being sent / cancelled; `404 PRINTER_NOT_FOUND`). No event. **Statements:** ≤ 3 (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.16 (R-70): was 3; the happy path uses 2, a refusal 3). Result: the job's new status.

#### 6.23a `GET /queries/bill-printers` ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.5 (G-28, R-27)

- **Permission:** `take-order` (any grant), the same permission as `RetargetBillPrint` (§6.23; R-27 amended 2026-11-19), staff session (`session: 'required'`). A read cannot be approved, so an `allowed` **and** a `requires_approval` grant both read, as 04 §6.3 `void-preview` ("`void-item` (any grant)") does; `RetargetBillPrint` itself, a non-approvable command, still needs `allowed` (00 v1.12 §3.6 command rule: `requires_approval` on a non-approvable command → `403 PERMISSION_DENIED`). Printers are branch-wide, so every scope of `take-order` (`branch`, `my_floors`, `own_tables`) reads the whole list; there is no out-of-scope refusal. Not granted or denied → `403 PERMISSION_DENIED`. No new error code (foundation auth / permission errors only).
- **Input:** none.
- **Result:** `{ printers: [{ printer_id, name }] }` — the branch's **active** printers (03c `printers` with `archived_at IS NULL`), ordered by `name`. An empty branch answers `{ printers: [] }`. The list feeds the staff **Print to** picker (§8 Print status, B18); the chosen `printer_id` goes to `RetargetBillPrint` (§6.23). No address, connection or status field.
- **Statements:** 1 (`SELECT printer_id, name FROM printers WHERE archived_at IS NULL ORDER BY name`); no N+1.
- **Tests:** #60b (§10.5).

#### 6.24 `CarryOverOrders` — `{ order_ids: string[1..40], reason_code ∈ CARRY_OVER_REASONS, note? }`

- ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-9, owner R4): a carried bill **may** already have payments (an open split or a partly paid bill); the close is allowed and the X / Z show them as **carried forward** (and the next day's as **brought forward**, §6.26, ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 H2-3).
- **Permission:** `close-business-day`. Each order unbilled and not cancelled (`409 ORDER_BILLED` / `400` per index) → `OrdersCarriedOver { order_ids, business_date, reason_code, has_note, note_id }` (`hub` stream). They survive this day's close only. **Statements:** 2. **Audit:** `orders_carried_over`.

#### 6.25 Day close — 01a `CloseBusinessDay` with 05's guard and appender (rows A-3, A-4, A-5)
