- **05 guard** (after 04's; ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (Q-2, owner 2026-11-18 11:08–11:09 PKT): a pending variance sign-off never blocks — it is not an open shift and the appender does not close its request; the Z lists it as "pending sign-off"; ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.18 (R-76, owner 2026-11-20): ~~never blocks~~ reversed — a pending sign-off **blocks**, so a day is open whenever a variance is answered (§3.14 / H7-5, R-44); the Z's "pending sign-offs" list is therefore empty at every close, the X still shows it live): open shifts of any day (1, `shifts_open_staff`) → `409 DAY_CLOSE_BLOCKED { reason: 'shift_open', shifts, count }`; ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.18 (R-76): shifts with a pending sign-off, any day (1, `shifts_signoff_pending`) → `{ reason: 'variance_pending', shifts: [{ shift_id, staff_name, closed_at, alert_id }], count }` (≤ 40 listed, `count` via `COUNT(*) OVER ()`; the existing code and list, §3.13, no new code); unbilled, uncancelled, not carried orders (1) → `{ reason: 'unbilled_orders', orders, count }` (≤ 40 listed, `count` via `COUNT(*) OVER ()`). ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.25 (R-86, owner 2026-11-20, task 25 review): "not carried" reads `carried_orders` for the open day's `business_date`, keyed by `(order_id, business_date)` — by date, not by session. A date closed, reopened and closed again (R-85 (1)): an order carried at the first close is still excused at the second close, the second Z lists it again under "carried over" (it is carried for that date), and it cannot be carried a second time that date (the key keeps one row; a second `CarryOverOrders` adds none and the first `reason_code` stays). No money effect. The order comes forward the next day as before. No migration.
- **05 appender:** open 05 requests + 02b `comp` requests (`alerts.listOpen`, 1) → `AlertClosed { outcome: 'obsolete', note: 'day_closed' }` drafts (01d `close` helper); Z number = MAX + 1 (1) (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.24 (R-85 (1)): `MAX(z_no) + 1` over every Z, so a second close on the same date takes the next number); `buildReport` over the day (§6.26, ≤ 6) → `ZReportIssued { business_date, z_no, report }` (`hub` stream). ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.24 (R-85 (2)): when **no branch config** exists, the appender **skips the Z** — no `ZReportIssued`, no `z_reports` row, no print — logs a warning `{ code: 'z_skipped_no_config', business_date }` (ids only), and the close succeeds; the `AlertClosed` drafts are still appended. ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.23 (R-84 (2)): the appender passes `buildReport` an `opts.warn` that logs each warning (`reopen_credit_missing`, ids only: `reopen_id`, `credit_invoice_id`; no staff name, no amount); a warning never refuses or blocks the close, and adds no statement. The live-only fold prints it at the `report` route. ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.2 (H16-5): `ZReportIssued.report` carries **staff ids only** (`staff_id`, `taken_by_staff_id`, `approver_staff_id`, `advance_staff_id`); names are resolved at render (`buildReportDoc`, the X / Z queries) from `staff_names`, never stored in the event. ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.2 (P0.1b, R-12 / G-9): the `report` embeds `mode_name` (each takings row) and `category_name` (each petty row) so a Z prints without a lookup; staff stay ids only.
- **Bound:** 05 adds ≤ 10 statements to the close (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.18 (R-76): ≤ **11** — the `variance_pending` read, 1). ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.24 (R-85 (3)): 01a's `CloseBusinessDay` count is plus these registered guard and appender statements (R-72 / R-79, row Q-7, row A-4); the 01a and 04 tests that pin the close's statement count or its event list (now with `ZReportIssued`) are updated, each with a comment naming R-85. **Engine:** the command already has its COMMIT-failure test; 05 adds one asserting no `ZReportIssued`, no `z_reports` row and no print job after a failed COMMIT (§10 #63).

#### 6.26 `GET /queries/x-report` and `GET /queries/z-reports?business_date?|cursor&limit`

- **Permission:** `view-reports`.
- **Report content (B26, `buildReport`):** ⚠️ Changed in v0.7.2 (owner Q-1): the report states the tenant's `currency_code` (`ZReportIssued.report.currency_code`); sales: sale invoices count, gross (Σ subtotal), discounts (count, value, by reason, requester / approver / method from the audit fold), service charge, net, **tax by tax code, profile and payment class** with rate (⚠️ Changed in v0.8 (§25): on exact amounts; **"rounding" is its own line**, outside every tax row and outside payments by mode) (⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-2): one line per `invoice_taxes` key; a one-tax profile prints as v0.6.2's "tax by class" line), rounding, total; credits (count, value, by reason) and **net = sales − credits**; payments by mode (in, out, change); comps (value), voided comps (`comp_voided_minor`, 02b O21), comp removals; voids by disposition (wastage / staff meal / moved / null, count and value, 04 G-8, from `ItemVoided`); reopens (count, value, requester, approver); petty cash by category and kind, open advances (who, amount, age); per shift: staff, float, expected, counted, variance, skipped flag, **variance sign-off state** (`pending sign-off` / `approved` / `disputed`, ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (Q-2, owner 2026-11-18 11:08–11:09 PKT)); ⚠️ Changed in v0.6.2 (H8-1): two more lines — **"variance sign-offs"** = every `ShiftVarianceSignedOff` recorded in the report's day, any shift date (shift date, staff, amount, outcome, approver, time; `shift_signoffs_day`), and **"pending sign-offs"** = every shift of any date with `variance_state = 'pending'` at issue (date, staff, amount; `shifts_signoff_pending`); X shows both live; both outside takings (invariant unchanged); X bound +1 statement; takings by taker (Q10); **full refunds** (`BillClosedEmpty`: count and the credit value, already inside "credits", ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-6, Q-3, owner 2026-11-18 11:08–11:09 PKT)); fiscal: invoices pending / acked / rejected / not required; bills count, average bill, open bills (X only, with "open too long" flags, M-8); carried-over orders (Z of the day they are carried into lists them as "carried in"); **"brought forward"** and **"carried forward"** (payments on open bills) lines by mode and the shift's staff (`staff_id`) (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.22 (R-83 (5)): was "by mode and shift"; each row carries the staff of the payment's shift, as the report row's `staff_id`) ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-9, owner R4), ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-3). **X** = the same over the open day, ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.2 (P0.1b, R-12 / G-9): the Z `report` embeds `mode_name` per takings row and `category_name` per petty row (staff ids only, H16-5), the shifts' expected cash **hidden** for shifts not counted (B23) (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.22 (R-83 (8)): every uncounted shift, skipped ones included; the Z shows `expected_minor` for a skipped shift).
- **Content readings** ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.22 (R-83, owner 2026-11-20, task 26a; field names of `ReportDocInput` / `ZReportSchema`):
  1. **Sales from sale invoices only.** `sales.taxes[]`, `sales.rounding_minor` and `sales.total_minor` come from the day's **sale-series** invoices only; a credit invoice's taxes and rounding never enter them. Credits reduce the Z total separately: `credits.value_minor` = Σ the day's credit totals and `net_after_credits_minor` = `sales.total_minor` − `credits.value_minor` (Z = Σ sale − Σ credit).
  2. **One tax row per (key, rate, label).** A (`tax_code`, `tax_profile_id`, `payment_class`) key that has two `rate_bp` or two `label` values in one day prints one `sales.taxes[]` row per (key, `rate_bp`, `label`); rows are never merged across rates or labels.
  3. **Discounts per sale invoice.** `sales.discounts.count` and `value_minor` (and `by_reason`, `entries`) come from the sale invoices' `discount_minor`, counted per invoice (an invoice with `discount_minor` > 0 counts 1). `entries[].method` = `approval` when the discount had an approver (`approver_staff_id` not null), otherwise `direct`.
  4. **Voids in units.** `voids[].count` = Σ voided units per disposition; `value_minor` = Σ `refund_minor`, and a void without `refund_minor` adds 0.
  5. **Brought / carried forward by staff.** Each `brought_forward[]` / `carried_forward[]` row is (`mode_name`, `staff_id`), `staff_id` = the staff of the payment's shift.
  6. **Bills.** `bills.count` = the distinct bills of the day; `bills.average_minor` = `sales.total_minor` / `bills.count`, rounded half-up; a day with no bills has `bills.average_minor` = 0 (integrator, no division by zero). ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.23 (R-84 (1)): a bill is a bill of the day when it has **at least one sale invoice** in the day; a bill whose only record that day is `BillClosedEmpty` (no sale invoice) is not counted, but a bill closed, then reopened and emptied the same day keeps its sale invoice and counts.
  7. **Reopens, full refunds, advance age.** `reopens.value_minor` = Σ the totals of the credit invoices the day's reopens issued; `full_refunds.value_minor` = Σ the totals of each `BillClosedEmpty`'s `credit_invoice_ids`; `open_advances[].age_minutes` = the advance's age at `issued_at`, floored to whole minutes.
  8. **Expected cash.** The X hides `expected_minor` (null) for every shift with no count, skipped shifts included; the Z shows `expected_minor` for skipped shifts (`skipped: true`, `counted_minor` / `variance_minor` null).
- **Content readings** ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.23 (R-84, owner 2026-11-20, task 26a review; integrator readings 10, 11):
  9. **A reopen whose credit is missing.** A reopen entry (`reopen_id` = the `BillReopened` event id, with its `credit_invoice_ids`) any of whose credit invoices is not among the day's invoices is **skipped**: it adds nothing to `reopens.count`, `reopens.value_minor` or `reopens.entries`, and `buildReport` does not throw. For each missing id it calls the optional `opts.warn` with `{ code: 'reopen_credit_missing', reopen_id, credit_invoice_id }` (ids only); without `opts.warn` the entry is skipped silently. The day-close appender (§6.25) logs the warning, ids only; the X query may pass no `warn`. The rest of the report is unchanged.
  10. **Comp removals per event.** `comps.removals.count` counts comp-removal events (one per event, not per unit or line); `value_minor` = Σ their values (integrator reading).
  11. **Sales net.** `sales.net_minor` = `sales.gross_minor` − `sales.discounts.value_minor`; the service charge (`sales.service_charge_minor`) is its own line and not in net, per §3.1 **Net** (integrator reading).
- **Content readings** ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.24 (R-85, owner 2026-11-20, task 25):
  12. **Z by date.** A Z is keyed by `z_no` (§5). `GET /queries/z-reports?business_date=` returns **every** Z of that date, newest `z_no` first (a date closed twice has two); the cursor pages by `z_no`. `PrintReport` of a date prints the latest `z_no` of that date (§6.22).
  13. **Brought forward by `z_no`.** Brought forward = the carried forward of **the previous Z by `z_no`** (the greatest `z_no` below this Z's; for the X, the greatest `z_no`), never "the last earlier date"; no Z yet → nothing brought forward.
- **Z list row** ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.32 (R-93, G-96): `{ business_date, z_no, report, staff_names, print }`. `print: { job_id, state, printer_name } | null` (additive) is the Z's **latest** print job: the `bill_print_jobs` row with `doc_kind 'report'`, `bill_id` NULL, `ref_id` = the Z's `business_date`, `created_at` ≥ the Z's `issued_at` and below the `issued_at` of any later Z of the same date (R-85 (1)), whose 03c job is a Z (`kind_label` 'Z report', so an X printed in a second session of the same date is not counted), newest `created_at` first. That is the live fold's `<event_id>:z` (§6.25) and every `PrintReport` of that Z (§6.22, which prints a date's latest `z_no`). `state` = the 03c document-job state (03c §3.7: `waiting` / `printing` / `printed` / `failed` / `cancelled`); `expired` when the 03c job row is gone (retention deletes done jobs after 7 days, 03c §5: printed or cancelled, no longer known which); `printer_name` = the job's current printer's name, null when `expired` or the printer row is gone. `print: null` = **no Z job was written**: the close found no `report` / `default` route (§3.16, the print was skipped and logged) and the Z was not printed since. Ids, state and printer name only (never `last_error`). 05-owned and read-side: 01a's `CloseBusinessDay` result, every event and every migration are unchanged.
- **Statements:** X ≤ 8 fixed (invoices + taxes of the day, payments by mode, shifts + takings, petty, audit rows for discounts / reopens / voids of the day, outbox counts, open bills); Z list: 1 (cursor, F §6.3). ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.32 (R-93): the Z list's `print` is a subquery of the same statement — still **1**, at 1 and at N Zs.
- **Invariant (property test)** ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-3), ⚠️ Changed in v0.5 (H3-3): **brought forward** = live payments on **splits that are not closed** at the day's open (incl. a reopened bill's splits and a carried bill's open splits); **carried forward** = the same at the close. Payments of a closed split are in its invoice and never counted again. **Σ the day's takings (`in` − `out`, all modes) = Z net (Σ sale − Σ credit totals of the day) + carried forward − brought forward.** The Z prints both lines by mode. A payment never changes after its shift is counted.

#### 6.27 `GET /queries/fiscal-status`

- **Permission:** `take-payment` or `view-reports`.
- **Result:** `{ jurisdiction_id, adapter_key, environment, adapter_health: 'ok' | 'failing' | 'unknown', last_success_at, pending_count, oldest_pending_age_ms, rejected_count, catch_up_window_ms, stuck: boolean, token_missing: [{ pos_id, name }], tokens: [{ pos_id, name, set_at }] }` (⚠️ Changed in v0.7.4 (H11-4): per credential name; replaces `token_missing_pos_ids`) (⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-1): was `authority`) — never a token, never a buyer (⚠️ Changed in v0.2, OQ-3: `tokens` shows only "set on <date>"). **Statements:** 3.

#### 6.28 `RetryFiscalNow` — `{}`

- **Permission:** `close-business-day`. Sets `next_try_at = now` for `pending` rows (plain statement, not an event; 1) and wakes the worker; a `rejected` row is **not** resent here (it needs the fix the rejection names; a developer task). Result `{ pending_count }`.

#### 6.28a `SetFiscalToken` — `POST /admin/commands/set-fiscal-token`, `{ pos_id, name?, token }` ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-3, new), ⚠️ Changed in v0.7.3 (H10-5: `name`)

- ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.2 (P0.1b, R-13 / G-11): the branch fiscal settings that list the `pos_ids` cannot be saved without `default_pos_id` when the adapter is not `none` (row A-2).
- **Permission:** `manage-fiscal-settings` (allowed-only; **Owner only** by default, row B-1 / B-2), `sessionKind: 'admin'` (hub admin page `/admin/fiscal/`, row H-2). Not queueable.
- **Script:** Zod (`pos_id` 1–40 and listed in the branch fiscal settings' `pos_ids`, else `422 CONFIG_REFERENCE_INVALID { field: 'pos_id', reason: 'not_found' }`; `name` 1–40, default `'token'`, one of the `credentialNames` of the branch's adapter or of an adapter recorded on a pending row (else `422 CONFIG_REFERENCE_INVALID { field: 'name', reason: 'not_found' }`); `token` 1–512 printable, the Zod message never echoes it) → `INSERT … ON CONFLICT (pos_id, name) DO UPDATE` into `fiscal_credentials` (replace = a new token; there is no read-back) (1) → append `FiscalTokenSet { pos_id, name, set_at, set_by_staff_id }` (`hub` stream; **no token, no length, no hash**) → the worker picks the new token at its next send.
- **Result:** `{ pos_id, name, set_at }` — never the token. **Statements:** ≤ 2 (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.16 (R-71): was 2; a bad `pos_id` 0; a name of the branch adapter 1; a bad name 1; a name of an adapter on a pending row 2, one extra read). **Audit:** `fiscal_token_changed` (ids and time only; ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.16 (R-69): subject type `hub`, row V-3).
- **Rules:** no query, response, log line, event, sync payload or cloud row ever carries the token; `processed_commands` stores only the result above; the page field is write-only and never pre-filled; removing a token is not offered in the pilot (replace instead). ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.16 (R-71): because the token is hash-omitted (H-4), a retransmit with the same `command_id` and a different token replays the old answer, so clients mint a new `command_id` for every Save.

#### 6.28b `fiscal-credentials` — `GET /admin/queries/fiscal-credentials` ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.14 (R-63, new)

- **Permission:** `manage-fiscal-settings` (allowed-only), `sessionKind: 'admin'` (the `/admin/fiscal/` page, row H-2), checked before any read.
- **Result:** `{ credentials: [{ pos_id, label, name, set_at | null }] }` — one entry per (`pos_id` of the branch fiscal settings' `pos_ids`, `name` of every `credentialNames` entry of the branch's adapter and of any adapter recorded on a pending outbox row, H11-4), ordered by `pos_id`, `name`; `set_at` from `fiscal_credentials`, `null` when not set. **Never the token**: the read selects `pos_id, name, set_at` only. With no branch config → `{ credentials: [] }`.
- **Statements:** 2 (the pending rows' adapters; `fiscal_credentials` without its secret column). A leak test pins that no answer, log or table outside `fiscal_credentials` holds a token value.

| `BillOpened` | bill | `{ bill_id, scope, seating_id, bill_key, order_type, prices_include_tax, rounding: { step_minor, mode, applies_to } }` (⚠️ Changed in v0.8.2 (H16-2)) (⚠️ Changed in v0.7.2 (H9-9): the mode in force at open) (⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-16): `bill_key` non-null for scope `key`) | billing |
| `ChargeApplied` | bill | `{ bill_id, charge: 'service', rate_bp, source: 'rule' \| 'manual', has_note, note_id }` | billing |

| `CheckBillPrinted` | bill | `{ bill_id, total_default_minor, line_count, printer_id, job_id, source }` (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.7 (task 5 / integrator): `source: 'manual'`, the one value — only `PrintCheckBill` §6.3 appends it; the §6.4 `BillRequested` reaction is a fold and appends nothing) | billing |
| `BillSplitsSet` | bill | `{ bill_id, mode, splits }` (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.7 (task 5 / integrator): `splits[]` items are `{ split_no, lines?, share_minor? }` — `items`: `lines: [{ order_item_id, order_id, units }]` (`order_id` from the lines the command reads; the `bill_split_lines` row shape, §5); `amount`: `share_minor`; `equal`: one item `{ split_no }` per **open** split number the `n`-way plan gives (closed numbers never listed, §3.8); `none`: **exactly one** item, the open remainder) | billing |
| `DiscountFrozen` ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.7 (R-41 / G-38) | bill | **`{ bill_id, frozen_minor, line_ids }`** — `frozen_minor: int ≥ 0`, the whole bill's discount frozen at the close that takes the bill from no closed split to one (B7, §3.5 (2); 0 with no discount); `line_ids: string[]`, the set in `bill_discount_lines` (= that `InvoiceIssued.discount_line_ids`); appended by `CloseSplit` right after `InvoiceIssued` in the same transaction (§6.12 step 6); the source of `bills.discount_json.frozen_minor`; a `BillReopened` clears the freeze, so the next first close appends it again; no audit action of its own (the close audits `invoice_issued`) | billing |
| `DiscountApplied` / `DiscountRemoved` | bill | §6.6 / §6.7 (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.7 (task 5 / integrator): `DiscountApplied.replaces` is a `boolean`; **`DiscountRemoved { bill_id, reason_code, has_note, note_id }`**, nothing else — not approvable, so no `alert_id`, requester or approver) | billing |
| `PaymentAdded` / `PaymentRemoved` | bill | §6.9 / §6.10 (`has_reference` only, never the text, ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 H1-20) (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.7 (R-44 / G-39): **`PaymentRemoved { payment_id, bill_id, split_no, reason_code, has_note, shift_id, mode_id, payment_class: 'cash' \| 'digital', direction: 'in' \| 'out', amount_minor, change_minor, taken_by_staff_id }`** — the last seven copied from the removed payment (`change_minor` 0 when it had no tender); ids and amounts only, no reference text) | billing, shifts |
| ~~`SplitClassSharesDeclared`~~ ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.1 (§26) | — | removed with `pro_rata` | — |
| `BuyerIdWaived` | bill | `{ bill_id, has_note, note_id, requester_staff_id, approver_staff_id, alert_id }` (⚠️ Changed in v0.5.1 (H4-4)) | billing |
| `InvoiceIssued` | bill | §6.12 step 6 (no buyer tax ID, ever; ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-1, J-2): `jurisdiction_id`, `currency_code`, `prices_include_tax`, per-line `taxes[]`) (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.7 (task 5 / integrator): `payment_class` is `'cash' \| 'digital' \| 'mixed'`, as `invoices.payment_class` in §5 — "the split's class, or 'mixed'") | billing (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.9: not `shifts` — §5, the shifts fold takes nothing the payments do not carry) |
| `BillReopened` | bill | §6.13 (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.7 (R-40 / G-37): + **`mode: 'full' \| 'corrections'`**, the mode §6.13 decided; the billing fold sets `bills.reopen_mode` from it) — in full: **`{ bill_id, mode: 'full' \| 'corrections', order_ids: string[1..], reason_code ∈ BILL_REOPEN_REASONS, has_note, note_id, requester_staff_id, approver_staff_id: string \| null, alert_id: string \| null }`**; ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.7 (R-41 / G-38): its fold also clears the discount freeze (`frozen_minor` → `null`, `bill_discount_lines` deleted, H2-19) | billing |

| `FiscalNumberAssigned` | bill | `{ invoice_id, adapter_key, pos_id, fiscal_number, qr_payload }` (worker; ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-6): was `authority`) | billing |
| `FiscalSubmitRejected` | bill | `{ invoice_id, adapter_key, error_code (≤ 40), http_status }` (worker; no request body; ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-6)) | billing |
| `ShiftCounted` ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.7 (R-42 / G-39) | shift | **`{ shift_id, counted_minor, expected_minor, variance_minor, by_staff_id, variance_alert_id: string \| null }`** — `variance_minor = counted_minor − expected_minor`; `variance_alert_id` = the `shift_variance` request raised in the same transaction (§6.17), `null` when \|variance\| ≤ `variance_threshold_minor`; the fold sets `shifts.variance_state` (`'pending'` when non-null, else `NULL`) and `variance_alert_id` from the event alone and never reads the threshold. `HandoverCounted` keeps `{ shift_id, counted_minor, expected_minor, variance_minor, by_staff_id }` (no sign-off, §3.11) | shifts |
| `ShiftOpened`, `ShiftCounted`, `ShiftCountSkipped`, `HandoverCounted`, `ShiftClosed` | shift | §6.15–§6.19 (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.7 (task 5 / integrator): **`ShiftCountSkipped { shift_id }`**; the note and closer are on the `ShiftClosed` beside it) | shifts |
| `ShiftVarianceSignedOff` ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (Q-2, owner 2026-11-18 11:08–11:09 PKT) | shift | `{ shift_id, outcome: 'approved' \| 'disputed', alert_id, approver_staff_id }` (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.7 (R-44 / G-39): **`{ shift_id, outcome: 'approved' \| 'disputed', alert_id, approver_staff_id, business_date }`** — `business_date` = the **open business day in the answer transaction** (the `shift_variance` `apply`, approve or reject, §3.14), not the shift's own day; the fold copies it into `shift_signoffs.business_date`, as §5, H8-1 and test #50c require) | shifts |
| `PettyCashAdvanced`, `PettyCashSettled`, `PettyCashOut`, `PettyCashIn` | shift | §6.20 (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.7 (task 5 / integrator): `advance_staff_id` is set (non-null) on `PettyCashAdvanced` only and is `null` on `PettyCashOut` / `PettyCashIn`) | shifts |
| `DrawerOpened` | shift | §6.21 | shifts |
| `OrdersCarriedOver`, `ZReportIssued` | hub | §6.24, §6.25 (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.7 (task 5 / integrator): `ZReportIssued.report` is a `ReportDocInput` (`@pos/domain/src/billing/docs.ts`) with `kind: 'z'`, and its `business_date` and `z_no` equal the event's; staff ids only, `mode_name` / `category_name` embedded, §6.25) | billing |
| `BillClosedEmpty` ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-6, Q-3 (b)), v0.6.1 (H7-3), ⚠️ Changed in v0.6.2 (H8-5, H8-6, H8-8, H8-10) | bill | **`{ bill_id, seating_id: string \| null, credit_invoice_ids: string[], alert_id?: string }`** — the one payload (every other mention points here); `seating_id` set only under §6.12's `BillClosed` rule (dine-in, seating not CLOSED, no other open bill), else `null`; `alert_id` when the order-cancelled hook runs inside an approval `apply`. Fold: bill CLOSED and **every split `state = 'closed'`, `invoice_id` NULL**, so its payments leave brought / carried forward (their sum is 0). No sale invoice, no `OrdersBilled`, no fiscal row, no receipt. Z: not in bills count / average; **"full refunds"** = bills empty-closed in the day with the value of their credits of any day (each credit is counted in the day it was issued); the `out` payments in takings | billing |
| `FiscalTokenSet` ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-3) | hub | `{ pos_id, name, set_at, set_by_staff_id }` (no token; `name` v0.7.3 H10-5) | billing (audit only) |
| 02b `OrdersBilled`, `OrdersUnbilled`, `LineCompRemoved`; 02a `BillClosed`, `SeatingReopened` (⚠️ Changed in v0.3, R2); 02a `TableMarksChanged` (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.6 (R-30, row T-4): `{ seating_id }`, ids only, §6.4a; ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.7 (R-43 / G-40, 02a v1.1.11): `{ seating_id, alert_id?: string }`, `alert_id` only from the `reopen_bill` `apply`, never `null`) | order / table | owned by 02b / 02a (rows Q-2, Q-4, T-3, T-4), written by 05 | orders, tables (`TableMarksChanged`: no row change); ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.7 (text clarification, R-41 / G-38): **billing** folds `OrdersBilled` into `bill_orders` (§5) |

Every 05 event carries `has_note` / `has_paid_to` instead of free text (⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-22); the text in `bill_texts`). ⚠️ Changed in v0.5 (H3-5): **no payload and no alert `values` carry text**; `bill_texts` is keyed by `subject_id` = the id the command mints and the event carries (`entry_id` for petty cash, `payment_id` for a payment, `note_id` — a ULID minted by the command — for bill-, charge-, discount-, reopen-, drawer- and carry-over notes), written by the command on the direct path and at raise on the approval path; `apply` never reads text. `DiscountApplied`, `DiscountRemoved`, `BillReopened` carry `has_note`, `note_id` likewise. ⚠️ Changed in v0.5.1 (H4-4): **every approvable kind's `values` carry `note_id` and `has_note`** (`discount`, `remove_service_charge`, `reopen_bill`, `remove_comp`, `close_without_buyer_id`, `petty_cash`; `shift_variance` takes `CountShift`'s `note_id` (⚠️ Changed in v0.6.2 (H8-9))) and `apply` copies them into its event, so an approved note resolves in `bill_texts`; `BuyerIdWaived`, `LineCompRemoved`, `ShiftClosed` carry `has_note`, `note_id`; `PettyCashSettled` has its own `note_id`. Events written by approval `apply` carry the request's `alert_id` and `approver_staff_id` = `AnswerFacts.answerer_staff_id` (01d contract). Builders in `@pos/events/testing` for each (F §6.7) and one fixture per event.

#### 6.30 Registrations at startup (`apps/hub/src/billing/index.ts`)

**WS audiences** ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.2 (H16-6; `ws/audience.ts:22-25`: an event type with no audience reaches no socket): `registerEventAudience` for every `bill`-stream event → staff sockets holding `take-payment` (scope as §6.1) and cashier kiosks; every `shift`-stream event → the shift's own staff's sockets and holders of `close-shift-for-others` only, with `ShiftCounted` / `HandoverCounted` (which carry expected cash) to those only after the count; `ZReportIssued`, `OrdersCarriedOver` → holders of `view-reports` / `close-business-day`; `FiscalTokenSet` → admin sessions only. ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.25 (G-86, task 33 integrator reading): hub sockets today are `staff` and `station` only, so `FiscalTokenSet` reaches **no socket** — intended until an admin socket exists; its audience is still registered, so the every-event-has-an-audience check passes for it. ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.3 (H17-4): **no frame carries `expected_minor` or `variance_minor` before the shift's count (B23)**; `ShiftCounted` / `HandoverCounted` go only to the shift's own staff and `close-shift-for-others` holders, after the count; the inputs of expected cash (payments, petty cash, the float) reach the cashier's own socket by design.

**At module load, before any projection is built** ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-8: `registerAuditAction` throws after the `audit` projection seals): `registerAuditAction` for each event in row V-3 and `registerRequestAction` for the seven kinds (map: actor = requester for approved actions, approver from the event, `subject_type 'bill' | 'shift' | 'order' | 'day'` (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.16 (R-69): `| 'hub'` for `fiscal_token_changed`, row V-3), `amount_minor`, `reason`; detail ≤ 2 KB with ids and counts; **no buyer fields, no reference, no `paid_to`, no notes beyond the reason**; ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.15 (R-65): the `requested` rows are written by 04's built-in `AlertRaised` map, so a `money_request_raised` detail carries **no kind** (read it by joining `audit_requests` on `alert_id`); `money_request_answered` carries the kind in its detail); the eight alert kinds (§3.14, `fiscal_outbox_stuck`) at module top level (01d boot rule). ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.25 (G-86, task 33 integrator reading): the startup wiring check (every 05 event type has a registered audience and audit map, §11 task 33) has **two** audit exceptions — `DiscountFrozen` (an audience, no audit action of its own, §6.29, R-41) and `FiscalNumberAssigned` (row V-3 lists no action for it); every other 05 event type has both. **Then:** projections `billing`, `shifts` (after `orders`, `tables`, `audit`); live-only print folds (`BillRequested`, `InvoiceCredited`, `ZReportIssued`); `registerDayCloseGuard`, `registerDayCloseAppender` (rows A-3, A-4); `registerMergeGuard` (row T-1); `registerTileMarks(billTileMarks)` (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.6 (R-30, row T-4), §6.4a); `registerLineGuard` (row Q-3); `registerFiscalAdapter(noneAdapter)`, `registerFiscalAdapter(praAdapter)` (Appendix P); `startFiscalWorker` via 00 row H-3.

#### 6.31 Example — Table 7, Rs 5,710 of food (one profile with one tax, restaurant service), jurisdiction `PK-PB` (adapter `PRA`), prices exclusive, rounding step Rs 1, `applies_to: 'all'`

1. Waiter taps **Ask for bill** → 02a `BillRequested` → 05 prints the check bill at the first-floor bill printer: subtotal 5,710.00; tax 16 % (cash) 913.60 → total if paid in cash **6,624.00** (rounding +0.40); a line "card / wallet / QR: 5,996.00". Banner `PROVISIONAL - NOT A TAX INVOICE`. `bill_requested` audit row.
2. All by card: the cashier taps Card → the screen shows 5,710 + 285.50 = 5,995.50 → **5,996.00** (+0.50) → `AddPayment` card 5,996.00 → `CloseSplit { expected_total_minor: 599600 }` → `InvoiceIssued` `AC1-S000123` (class digital, tax row 500 bp), `OrdersBilled`, `BillClosed`; outbox row pending; receipt "FISCAL NUMBER PENDING" if the worker has not answered yet; no drawer kick.
3. Mixed instead, Rs 3,000 card + rest cash: `higher_rate` (the `PK-PB` seed) → the whole split at 16 % → 6,624.00, card 3,000 + cash 3,624. (⚠️ Changed in v0.8.1 (§26): `pro_rata` is out of v1.0, §3.6.)
4. The worker calls the branch adapter's `submitSale` with idempotency key `AC1-S000123` (the PRA mapping is Appendix P §P.2) → `FiscalNumberAssigned`; a reprint shows the fiscal number with the jurisdiction's label and the QR.

---

## §7. Cross-cutting (each item with the failure it prevents)

| Item | Rule | Prevents |
|---|---|---|
| Transactions | Every 05 command in one `BEGIN IMMEDIATE`; close / reopen / day-close checks inside it (F §6.3) | void approved, weight entered or a second kiosk closing between check and write (F §1 race table) |
| Idempotency | `command_id` (D32) for every command; the fiscal outbox keyed by `invoice_id`, idempotency key = `invoice_ref` (v0.7 J-6) | double invoice on a retransmit; double fiscal post after a crash |
| Gap-free numbers | `MAX + 1` under the single writer + unique index | a gap or duplicate an authority audit would flag |
| Fiscal worker | one in flight; back-off 5 s → 30 s → 2 min → every 5 min; HTTP timeout 10 s; never on a command path; started after listen (row H-3); paused while the hub has no route to the internet (`fetch` fails fast) | close waiting for the internet; a flood of retries on a dead link |
| Stuck alert | §3.15; one alert per branch | an invoice missing the jurisdiction's catch-up window unnoticed |
| Privacy | buyer tax ID (every type, J-4) only in `invoice_buyers`; the full ID prints only on that customer's receipt and its reprints (owner Q-1); no screen, check bill, X / Z shows it; no response returns it; logs carry ids and counts only (B30); `details` / audit / Z never carry names beyond the existing `staff_names` read model, never buyer data, never `paid_to` | a buyer tax ID leaks into the immutable, synced log or the cloud |
| Secrets | tokens only in the hub table `fiscal_credentials`, entered on the hub admin page (B22, §6.28a) ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-3); the adapter reads at send; error texts from the authority are stored as a short code | a token in the repo, a log, an event or the cloud |
| Blind count | `expected_*` fields null before `ShiftCounted`, enforced in the query and command result builders (one function); test per response | cashier sees expected before counting (D21) |
| Rate effective date | issue date = local calendar date of `issued_at` in the hub's time zone (01a S47 rule) for every rate row and every `jurisdiction_rules` row; the bill view shows the rate row's ref | wrong rate on the notification day |
| Performance | close ≤ 18 statements, bill view ≤ 9, X ≤ 8 (fixed, no N+1); no timing assert in the gate (03a AD-32); `test:perf` adds a 200-bill rush close row on the pilot box (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.27 (R-88): it asserts `CloseSplit` (with its payment) p99 ≤ 100 ms per close and all 200 closes ≤ 60 s in total; never in the normal gate; the owner runs it on the pilot hardware, on the real disk) | close slow at peak (200 bills / rush, R6) |
| Rate limits | none new (LAN, PIN lockout covers PIN answers, 01d) | — |
| Offline | close, payments, shifts, petty cash, day close work on the LAN without internet (G0-1); only the fiscal answer waits | billing stops when the internet drops (E5) |
| Folder | `apps/hub/src/billing/{commands,queries,projections,fiscal,print,kinds,lanes}/`, `packages/domain/src/billing/`, `packages/events/src/events/{bill,shift}/`, `apps/client/src/features/billing/` (F §6.9 `features/cashier` named `billing`); no new top-level folder | — |

---

## §8. Client contract (`apps/client`, staff entry; R5 permission-derived; ui-kit only)

- **Cashier screen** (kiosk / tablet with `take-payment`): open-bills list (§6.2) pulled on focus, on `event` frames of bill / order / table streams and on reconnect; "check bill printed" badge; "open too long" flag; partly-paid badge. No push, no sound for bill requests (Q13).
- **Bill screen:** lines with COMP / PENDING VOID / WEIGHT NEEDED tags; totals from the server only (§6.1) — the client never computes tax; **no tax buttons** (G1-15); the payment-mode pad shows each mode; choosing a mode shows the total at that class before taking it (M-7); ⚠️ Changed in v0.8 (§25): the pad shows the balance and the **"Rounding adjustment"** line separately from tax; the card and cash dues are shown side by side (§3.5 (7); under cash-only rounding the cash due is rounded down to the coin step, H15-5); a paid split shows no payment due and a negative balance shows only **"refund card <x> / cash <y>"** (⚠️ Changed in v0.8.3 (H17-1)) (per type up to what it paid; a type with 0 is hidden; a full refund shows exactly what each type paid); ~~a `pro_rata` declaration step~~ (v0.8.1 §26: removed); tendered amount entry with change due (M-6; never pre-filled with the total); split editor (items / amount / equal); discount sheet (reason buttons from settings, approval waiting card); service charge row with Remove / Add back; buyer sheet when `buyer.required` (name + a type picker from the jurisdiction's accepted types + the ID value, ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-4); "Customer refuses" → `WaiveBuyerId`); Close button disabled with the server's `blocks` text (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.6 (R-29, design brief Q-5): one copy key per pinned `reason`, filled from its `details`, §6.1); a **Reopened** chip while `reopen_count ≥ 1` and the bill is not CLOSED (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.6 (R-29, design brief Q-18)); `BILL_TOTAL_CHANGED` → refresh and show the new total, never auto-close. Online-only actions show "Needs the hub" offline (G0-1).
- **Remove payment** ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.28 (R-89, G-92, E2E E-3): on each payment row of an **open** split (never on a locked, closed split) a **Remove** action opens a sheet with the payment (mode, taker, time, amount), a reason picker (`wrong_mode`, `wrong_amount`, `customer_changed`, `other`, §6.10), a note (≤ 200, required for `other`, §4.4), **Keep it** and **Remove payment** → `RemovePayment { payment_id, reason_code, note? }` (§6.10). Shown to `take-payment`; on a payment taken by **someone else** only with `close-shift-for-others` as well (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.29 (R-90): "someone else" = the payment's `taken_by_staff_id` (§6.1) is not the session's staff id; never decided by `taken_by_name`). Not approvable: no waiting card. Refusals: `SHIFT_NOT_OPEN { reason: 'payment_shift_closed' }` → "This payment's shift is already counted — it can't be removed" with the hint to give the money back from today's shift (the pad's Give back, `AddPayment { direction: 'out' }`, §3.7); `PAYMENT_NOT_FOUND` / `BILL_NOT_OPEN` → refresh the bill; `PERMISSION_DENIED { reason: 'not_own_payment' }` → its `errors.<code>` text. Frames: 05 design handoff screen **05-D07** (`RemovePayment`, `RemovePaymentShiftClosed`, `docs/design/handoff/05/`).
- **Reopen** ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.28 (R-89, G-91, E2E E-2): a **Reopen** action on a CLOSED bill that has a sale invoice (a bill closed empty, §3.10, has none) and on a closed split's invoice of a PARTLY_PAID bill — `ReopenBill` takes the bill, so either one reopens the **whole bill** (every closed split's sale invoice gets a credit note) — and on the `LINE_IN_CLOSED_SPLIT` message ("Reopen the bill to change a paid split"). Shown only to holders of **`reopen-bill`** (`branch`; grant `allowed` or `requires_approval`, R5); under `requires_approval` the action carries "Needs approval". It opens a sheet with: the bill and its invoice refs; a reason picker from **`BILL_REOPEN_REASONS`** (§4.4: `wrong_item_billed`, `wrong_payment_mode`, `customer_returned_item`, `price_error`, `other`; EN / UR labels, never the raw code); a note (≤ 200, **required for `other`**, §4.4); a line saying what happens: "Each paid split gets a credit note. Payments stay. Close again to issue new invoices.", with the paid splits listed (invoice ref, "credit note prints by itself", §3.16). The sheet promises no mode: full edit or corrections only is decided by the hub inside the reopen, or at approve (§3.10, §6.13). **`allowed`** → **Reopen** sends `ReopenBill { bill_id, reason_code, note? }`; the `reopened` result shows the credit refs and totals and, when `mode` is `corrections`, the line "Corrections only: remove, void or comp; new items go on a new bill"; the bill shows its Reopened chip (Bill screen above). **`requires_approval`** → **Ask for approval** sends the same command; the `{ pending: { alert_id } }` result shows the waiting card (who it waits for, the reason, the time asked, **Withdraw**), the same way as the discount, remove-comp and waiver sheets; the bill stays closed until it is approved; Withdraw closes the request and changes nothing (§3.14, `onWithdraw` returns `[]`); an answer reaches the bill through its `event` frames. Refusals, shown in the sheet with their §9 codes: `BILL_REOPEN_NOT_SUPPORTED { invoice_id }` → "This bill can't be reopened: its tax invoice can't be credited here — ask the owner"; `BILL_REOPEN_PENDING { alert_id }` → "A reopen is already waiting for approval"; `BILL_NOT_OPEN { state }` → "Only a closed or partly paid bill can be reopened" (refresh the bill); `PERMISSION_DENIED` and `VALIDATION_FAILED` (`other` with no note) → their `errors.<code>` text. Frames: 05 design handoff screen **05-D05** (`Reopen`, `ReopenWaiting`, `ReopenCorrections`, `ReopenNotSupported`, `docs/design/handoff/05/`). The approver's answer sheet is 01d's (`AnswerReopen`), unchanged.
- **Print status:** each bill document shows waiting / printed / failed with the printer name; on failed or waiting > 10 s a **Print to** picker lists active printers (`RetargetBillPrint`, B18) from `GET /queries/bill-printers` (§6.23a), read when the picker opens (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.5 (G-28, R-27)).
- **Shift:** open with float; close shows card / online totals and asks for the counted cash **before** any expected figure; variance result after the count; "Variance sent for sign-off (pending); the drawer is free" (⚠️ Changed in v0.6.2 (H8-9)); open advances list; handover count (drawer shifts).
- **Petty cash:** advance / out / in sheet with categories; open advances with Settle.
- **Day close** (staff app, `close-business-day`): blockers list (voids, open shifts, unbilled orders with Carry over), then Close; Z shown ~~and printed~~. ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.32 (R-93, G-96): the screen never says the Z prints by itself. After the close it reads the Z of the closed date (`GET /queries/z-reports?business_date=<the close's business_date>`, the newest `z_no` first: row 0) and shows the print state from that row's `print` (§6.26): `null` → "Z not printed — print it from the Z screen" (with the Z screen's Print, `PrintReport`, §6.22); `waiting` / `printing` → "Z printing…" (printer name shown when known); `failed` → "Z printing… — printer problem on <printer>" (03c retries; Print to / the Z screen's Print stay open); `printed` → "Z printed on <printer>"; `cancelled` → as `null`. While the state is `waiting`, `printing` or `failed` the screen reads the same query again every 3 s, at most 10 times, then keeps the last state with a Refresh. `/admin/stock` keeps its Close day (row A-5).
- **Reports:** X (any time) and Z list (`view-reports`) (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.32 (R-93): each Z shows its print state from `print`, the same texts as Day close; `expired` → no print line; after Print on a Z the screen reads the Z list again to show the new job); fiscal status line (pending count, oldest age) on the cashier screen when the adapter is not `none`.
- **Errors:** every §9 code has an `errors.<code>` key (EN + UR, D12); `DAY_CLOSE_BLOCKED` reasons render as lists.
- **i18n:** amounts formatted only in the client (D47) with the bill's `currency.exponent` (⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-1, row F-6): never a hard-coded 2 decimals); Latin digits in Urdu (R20); no other locale work (§17).
- **Owner app** (01a row A-1 (6)): Billing settings page with the receipt preview (`renderText`) and the fiscal catalog; no secret field.
- **Hub admin `/admin/fiscal/`** ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-3): per `pos_id` "Token: set on <date>" / "not set", a write-only field + Save (`SetFiscalToken`), shown by `manage-fiscal-settings`; the field is cleared after Save and never filled from the server. ⚠️ Changed in v0.7.4 (H11-4): **one write-only field per (`pos_id`, `name`)** for every `credentialNames` entry of the branch's adapter and of any adapter on a pending row (PRA: one "Token" per `pos_id`, as before), each with "set on <date>" / "not set" and its own Save sending `SetFiscalToken { pos_id, name, token }`; same rules as the token (never pre-filled, cleared after Save).
- **Splits** ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-4, OQ-6): the discount sheet is on the bill only and is hidden once a split is closed; closed splits show as locked with their invoice; lines added later appear in the open remainder with a "new" mark (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.6 (R-29, design brief Q-4): shown on a line of an open split when `lines[].added_after_close` is true, §6.1); a void / comp on a locked line shows `LINE_IN_CLOSED_SPLIT` with "Reopen the bill to change a paid split".

## §9. Error catalogue additions (Foundation §6.2; appended to the status map and `@pos/domain/src/errors.ts` at build, no bump on its own, R34)

| Code | Status | `details` | Returned when |
|---|---|---|---|
| `BILL_NOT_FOUND` | 404 | `{ bill_id }` | unknown bill id |
| `SHIFT_NOT_FOUND` | 404 | `{ shift_id }` | unknown shift |
| `INVOICE_NOT_FOUND` | 404 | `{ invoice_id }` | reprint of an unknown invoice |
| `PAYMENT_NOT_FOUND` | 404 | `{ payment_id }` | remove of an unknown / already removed payment |
| `PAYMENT_MODE_NOT_FOUND` | 404 | `{ mode_id }` | mode unknown or inactive in the branch settings |
| `PETTY_CASH_NOT_FOUND` | 404 | `{ entry_id }` | settle of an unknown advance |
| `Z_REPORT_NOT_FOUND` | 404 | `{ business_date }` (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.26 (R-87): `business_date: null` when a Z print with no `ref_id` finds no Z at all, §6.22) | reprint of a Z that does not exist |
| `BILL_NOT_OPEN` | 409 | `{ bill_id, state }` | the bill / split is closed, absorbed, or its order cancelled |
| `BILL_CLOSE_BLOCKED` | 409 | `{ reason: 'void_pending' \| 'weight_pending' \| 'tax_unresolved', order_item_ids }` (≤ 40; ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.2 (P0.1b, R-1): `order_item_ids` may hold the literal `'service_charge'` for an unresolved service-charge tax profile) ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-6: `lines_unassigned` dropped), ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-2: `tax_unresolved`) | §3.3 rules 2, 3, 3a |
| `DISCOUNT_PENDING` | 409 | `{ alert_id }` | a discount request on the bill is open (close, second discount) |
| `SERVICE_CHARGE_NOT_APPLIED` ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.12 (R-58) | 409 | `{ bill_id }` | the `remove_service_charge` approval's re-check: the charge was removed meanwhile |
| `BUYER_ID_REQUIRED` | 409 | `{ threshold_minor, net_minor, accepted_types: string[] }` (⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-4)) | §3.3 rule 6 |
| `BILL_TOTAL_CHANGED` | 409 | `{ expected_minor, total_minor }` | §3.3 rule 7 |
| `BILL_BALANCE_DUE` | 409 | `{ split_no, balance_minor }` | §3.3 rule 8 |
| `BILL_PARTLY_PAID` | 409 | `{ bill_id }` | `ApplyDiscount` / `RemoveDiscount` on a bill with a closed split (B7) ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-4, OQ-6: was "any line edit on a partly paid bill") |
| `LINE_IN_CLOSED_SPLIT` ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-6) | 409 | `{ order_item_id, bill_id, split_no }` | a remove / comp / comp removal / weight / void touching units a closed split invoiced (B15, rows Q-3, V-1) |
| `BILL_HAS_PAYMENTS` | 409 | `{ bill_id, split_nos? }` | merge / seating split with payments; a re-plan that drops a paid split |
| `BILL_REOPEN_PENDING` | 409 | `{ alert_id }` | a reopen request on the bill is open |
| `BILL_REOPEN_NOT_SUPPORTED` ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-1, J-6) | 409 | `{ invoice_id }` | reopen of a bill with a fiscalized invoice whose adapter or jurisdiction cannot report a credit (§3.15) |
| `LINE_NOT_COMPED` | 409 | `{ order_item_id }` | `RemoveComp` on a line with no comped units |
| `SHIFT_NOT_OPEN` | 409 | `{ reason: 'no_shift' \| 'no_drawer_shift' \| 'not_open' \| 'payment_shift_closed' }` (⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (Q-2): `shift_counted` dropped, no counted state) (⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-26)) | money with no money shift; drawer action off a drawer; count of a closed shift |
| `SHIFT_ALREADY_OPEN` | 409 | `{ shift_id }` | second open shift for one person |
| ~~`SHIFT_VARIANCE_PENDING`~~ ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (Q-2: dropped, a counted shift is already closed; never appended, owner 2026-11-18 11:08–11:09 PKT) | — | — | — |
| ~~`SHIFT_NOT_COUNTED`~~ ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (Q-2: dropped; `CloseShift` is the skip path only, a missing `skip_count` is `400 VALIDATION_FAILED`, owner 2026-11-18 11:08–11:09 PKT) | — | — | — |
| `DRAWER_IN_USE` | 409 | `{ shift_id, staff_name }` | the device is already another open shift's drawer |
| `PETTY_ADVANCE_SETTLED` | 409 | `{ entry_id }` | settling a settled advance |
| ~~`PETTY_CASH_EXCEEDS_DRAWER`~~ ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-15: dropped, never appended) | — | — | a cash-out / advance above the shift's expected cash |
| ~~`BILL_PAYMENT_ORDER`~~ ⚠️ Changed in v0.6.2 (H8-2: dropped, payment order never matters, owner 2026-11-18 11:36 PKT) | — | — | — |
| ~~`BILL_CLASS_SHARE_MISMATCH`~~ ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.1 (§26) | — | — | removed with `pro_rata` (v1.1, A6) |
| ~~`BILL_CLASS_SHARE_INVALID`~~ ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.1 (§26) | — | — | removed with `pro_rata` (v1.1, A6) |
| `BILL_SPLIT_INVALID` | 422 | `{ reason: 'units_missing' \| 'units_exceed' \| 'deal_whole' \| 'option_with_parent' \| 'shares_sum' \| 'split_closed', order_item_id?, expected_minor? }` ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-6) | §3.8, §6.5 |
| `DISCOUNT_TOO_LARGE` | 422 | `{ max_minor }` | discount above the target or `max_discount_pct` |
| `PAYMENT_MODE_NOT_FISCAL` ⚠️ Changed in v0.6.1 (H7-4), ⚠️ Changed in v0.6.2 (H8-3) | 422 (⚠️ Changed in v0.8.2 (H16-8): at `AddPayment` and at `CloseSplit`) | `{ mode_id, adapter_key }` | a fiscalized order type's payment in a mode with no `authority_codes` entry for the branch's adapter while that adapter is not `none` (at add, or found at close; ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-5)) |
| `PAYMENT_EXCEEDS_BALANCE` | 422 | `{ balance_minor }` | ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.2 (H16-3): an `in` above its due field (`card_due` / `cash_due`); an `out` above its refund due (0 for a type that paid nothing); ⚠️ Changed in v1.0 (H18-4): any `in` while the split's balance is ≤ 0 (`balance_minor: 0`, H17-1) |
| `PAYMENT_REFERENCE_REQUIRED` | 422 | `{ mode_id }` | mode needs a reference |
| `PAYMENT_CASH_STEP` ⚠️ Changed in v0.7.3 (H10-2) | 422 | `{ step_minor }` | under `applies_to: 'cash'`, a cash `amount_minor` that is not a multiple of the rounding step |
| `DRAWER_DEVICE_INVALID` | 422 | `{ device_id }` | `as_drawer` on a device that cannot hold a drawer |
| `PETTY_SETTLE_MISMATCH` | 422 | `{ amount_minor }` | spent + returned ≠ the advance |

⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-14): v0.3's `TABLE_SEATING_CLOSED` is **dropped** — 02a's T18 `TABLE_SEATING_CHANGED` already refuses a submit naming a cleaned seating.

Reused (not new): `DAY_CLOSE_BLOCKED` (04) gains `reason: 'shift_open' \| 'unbilled_orders'` (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.18 (R-76): `\| 'variance_pending'`) (shapes §3.13); `ORDER_BILLED`, `VOID_PENDING`, `TABLE_NOT_OPEN`, `BUSINESS_DAY_NOT_OPEN`, `PRINTER_NOT_FOUND`, `PRINT_JOB_NOT_FOUND`, `PRINT_JOB_NOT_PENDING`, `PERMISSION_DENIED` (new reasons `not_own_payment`, `skip_not_granted`), `CONFIG_REFERENCE_INVALID`, `VALIDATION_FAILED`. G0-7's `REFUND_EXCEEDS_PAID` is not created (no refund command, Q8).

## §10. Tests (`apps/hub/test/billing/*.int.test.ts`, `packages/domain/src/billing/*.test.ts`, `apps/hub/test/billing/fiscal/*.test.ts`; client tests in `apps/client/src/features/billing/`)

Every command also has: happy path, one test per error code it returns, retransmit returns the identical response and writes nothing (D32), and every grant state of its permission (`allowed`, `requires_approval`, not granted, out of scope; 04 G1-7 pattern). **HIGH** tests (G0 §5, G1-13) are marked **H**.

### 10.1 Bill, splits, discount, charges

1. Projected bill (no 05 event yet) equals the bill after `BillOpened` for the same lines (same `computeBill`).
2. Dine-in bill follows `seating_orders` (only orders neither billed nor cancelled, ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 H1-21; a closed seating's billed orders never appear as a projected bill): a second round joins; a cancelled order leaves; an archived table's order stays billable (01a §6.5).
3. Takeaway bill = one `bill_key` (the root order and its additions, ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-25)); close allowed before any `ItemServed` (Q2, B16); afterwards `RemoveItems` / `VoidItems` → `ORDER_BILLED`.
4. Lines: COMP shown at value with deduction; PENDING VOID tagged and only `refund_minor` subtracted (a comped unit's `comp_voided_minor` not); return line base 0; pending weight 0 and listed in `blocks`.
4b. ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.6 (R-29, design brief Q-4) `lines[].added_after_close`: a projected bill and a bill with no closed split → every line `false`; item split A (split 1), B (split 2), split 1 closes, a round C joins split 2 → C `true`, A and B `false`; equal split of A, B into 2, split 1 closes, a round D → D `true`, A and B `false`; after the last split closes C / D stay `true`; `ReopenBill` → every line `false`; the re-close of one split → the lines on the bill at that close `false`, a later round `true`; the statement count stays ≤ 9 at 1 and 40 lines (#91).
4c. **H** ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.6 (R-29, design brief Q-5) `blocks`: for each §3.3 rule that can block (1, 2, 3, 3a, 4, 6, 8, 8a), a bill in that state → exactly one entry per open split it concerns (bill rules once, `split_no: null`) with the pinned `reason`, and `details` equal to the `details` that `CloseSplit` of that split then answers (same fixture, one call each); several rules at once → ordered by rule, then `split_no`; reasons for rules 5, 7, 9 never appear; a closed split gives no entry; no rule → `blocks: []` and the close (with `expected_total_minor`, and `buyer` when required) succeeds.
4d. ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.6 (R-29, design brief Q-18) `reopen_count`: `0` for a projected bill and a bill never reopened; `1` after one `ReopenBill` (direct and approved), `2` after a second; a re-close leaves it unchanged; a rebuild of `billing` gives the same value.
4e. ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.29 (R-90, G-93) the bill view's ids: a closed split's `invoice` carries `invoice_id` equal to its current sale invoice's id (and `PrintBillDocument { doc: 'receipt', ref_id: invoice_id }` prints that invoice); an open split's `invoice` stays `null`; every `payments[]` entry carries `taken_by_staff_id` equal to the `PaymentAdded` taker, next to `taken_by_name`; the §6.1 statement count is unchanged for a bill with 1 and with 40 lines / payments.
5b. ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-11): a fully comped (zero-total) bill closes with no payment via `bill_ref`.
5. **H** close refused for each §3.3 rule 1–9, in order, each with its code; nothing written (no event, outbox, job, buyer row).
6b. ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.6 (R-30, row T-4) tile marks: `billTileMarks` answers a list of 40 open seatings in 1 statement; a seating whose open bill was reopened (PARTLY_PAID, corrections on an open seating, full edit) → `reopened: true`, cleared when the bill closes again; `PrintCheckBill` → `check_printed_at` = its time; `RequestBill` alone → `check_printed_at` = `bill_requested_at`; an absorbed or closed bill gives no mark; `PrintCheckBill` and a dine-in `ReopenBill` without `SeatingReopened` each append exactly one `TableMarksChanged { seating_id }` in their transaction, a full-edit reopen of a CLOSED bill appends `SeatingReopened` and **no** `TableMarksChanged`, takeaway / parking append none; a rebuild of `billing` gives the same marks.
6c. ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.7 (R-43 / G-40) **H** a `reopen_bill` approve for a dine-in PARTLY_PAID bill on an open seating (no `SeatingReopened`) → the `apply` drafts include `TableMarksChanged { seating_id, alert_id }` with the request's `alert_id`, and 01d `checkAlertIds` accepts the answer (before 02a v1.1.11 it refused it); a direct `ReopenBill` and `PrintCheckBill` write `TableMarksChanged` with no `alert_id` key; the schema refuses `alert_id: null` (02a #68).
6. `BillClosed` reaches the staff sockets and cashier kiosks (05 registers it in `TABLE_EVENT_TYPES`, ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-7)); `OrdersUnbilled` reaches the map / table view; the tile turns NEEDS_CLEANING; takeaway writes no `BillClosed`.
7. Item split: units over 2 splits; a deal whole; an option child with its parent; `units_missing`, `units_exceed`, `deal_whole`, `option_with_parent` refusals ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-6).
8. Amount split: Σ shares must equal the default-class total (`shares_sum`); equal into 3 of Rs 1,000.00 → 333.33 / 333.33 / 333.34 net shares by `allocateDealMinor` (remainder to the last split, OQ-5) ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-13); a later round re-bases the last open share (H1-22).
9. Re-plan keeps a paid split's number; dropping it → `BILL_HAS_PAYMENTS`; after a close only the open remainder re-plans, naming a closed split → `BILL_SPLIT_INVALID { reason: 'split_closed' }` ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-6).
10. **H** property: for random bills (1–40 lines, 1–3 classes, discounts, service charge, item / amount / equal splits, random payment classes) Σ split `S` = Σ line bases; Σ printed tax = Σ invoice tax rows; one rounding per split; no negative figure.
10b. **H** ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-6) property over random bills with rounds added between split closes: Σ closed-split units + open-remainder units = the bill's live line units (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.14 (R-64): for **item** splits; an amount / equal close freezes each covered line whole — every such split records the line's live units, §5, H1-27 — so any `closed_split_units` row means the line is frozen); no unit in two splits; every closed split's invoiced units and totals unchanged by later rounds, voids and comps on other lines.
10c. **H** ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-6): after split 1 closes, a new round joins the highest-numbered open split with no staff action and no close block; when the last split closes the bill is CLOSED; a later order on that seating (where 02a allows one) opens a **new** bill with its own `BillOpened`.
10f. **H** ⚠️ Changed in v0.5 (H3-4): 3 × Rs 1,000, comp 1 before an item-split close (split 1 = 1 unit, share 666), then `VoidItems` 2 units → `LINE_IN_CLOSED_SPLIT` (residual base 334 on 0 open units). ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-2): line B 3 × Rs 1,000, 1 unit closed in split 1: comp 1 open unit → open part = 2,000 − 1,000 = 1,000 (not 1,333); comp 3 units → `LINE_IN_CLOSED_SPLIT`; a weight change on a line with closed units → refused; Σ closed shares + open base = line base after every step.

Σ `base_minor` per tax over the lines + the service charge = the invoice row's `base_minor`, in both modes and for the compound pair. ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.4 (R-20, R-21): inclusive with a profile of two taxes (13 % + 5 %) and 1-minor-unit lines (incl. an amount split [499 990, 10] of 10 × 50 000): no line's taxes of the profile above its net, no negative base, Σ = rows; an applied charge of 0 → `service_charge: null`.

Pakistan (`PK-PB`, `higher_rate`, `all` Rs 1): card-only 5 995.50 → total 5 996.00, `R` +0.50 untaxed, tax row 285.50 unchanged, card 5 996.00 closes; cash 6 623.60 → 6 624.00. (⚠️ Changed in v0.8.1 (§26): the `pro_rata` cases are removed.) Refunds ("jitna liya utna wapas"): **full refund** — `higher_rate` mixed bill paid card 3 000.00 + cash 3 624.00 (total 6 624.00 incl. rounding +0.40), every line removed → card out 3 000.00 + cash out 3 624.00, exactly what was taken, rounding included → `BillClosedEmpty`; partial — card bill 2 100.00, reopen −200.00 base → new exact 1 890.40, re-rounded 1 890.00 → card out 210.00, a cash out refused (cash paid nothing); mixed card 10.00 + cash 2.00 on 12.02 (`cash` step 5) → reopen to 7.01 → total 7.00 (cash involved) → refund 5.00, card ≤ 10.00, cash ≤ 2.00, both shown, cash a step multiple; no-change reopen → refund 0, same tax and total. `applies_to: 'cash'` (step 5): card-only 12.02 → `R` 0, total 12.02; card 10.00 + cash 2.00 → `R` −0.02, total 12.00; a cash amount off the step → `PAYMENT_CASH_STEP`; ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.1 (§26) (H15-5): exponent 0, step 5 `up`, cash total 12 050 (exact 12 049): card 33 first → shown cash due **12 015** (rounded down), cash 12 015 + card 2 closes; a cash refund due off the step is shown rounded down. ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.2 (H16-4, Q-2): `cash` step 5 — the check bill of an unpaid 13.99 split prints 14.00; a 0.02 split closes at 0 with no payment (rounding −0.02). ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.3 (H17-1): Rs 5,710 on `PK-PB` paid 5,996.00 by card → no cash due (before close, and on a no-change reopen); reopen −1.00 base owing Rs 2 → only "refund card 2.00", no cash due.
20c. **H** ⚠️ Changed in v0.8 (§25) **property** (the run-12 – run-14 model, rewritten for the rounding-adjustment model): ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.1 (§26): three setups — `higher_rate` × {`all` Rs 1, `cash` step 5, `none`} (`pro_rata` out of v1.0) — with class rates equal and different (Punjab 500 / 1600 bp); 30 000 random bills each, two seeds: (1) **sales** — random partial card / cash payments in random order, each within the pad's accepted range → every split reaches `B = 0` and closes; tax rows identical to the same bill with `R` forced to 0; `R` never in a tax row; every shown due is accepted as keyed and closes as the class it implies (H15-5, H15-6, H16-1); the rounding rule is the bill's frozen one (H16-2); (2) **refunds** — reopen with random line removals and additions (incl. under half a step and new rounds), then pay the shown refund / payment dues per type in random order → every split reaches `B = 0`; no `out` exceeds what its type paid; payment class and tax never change by a refund; **full refunds** return each type exactly what it paid; (3) **no-change reopens** — every closed bill reopened with no line change → 0 stuck, 0 changes of `G`, `R`, tax or total. Zero stuck splits is the pass condition; the generator is seeded and prints its seed on failure. ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.2: the no-change-reopen sweep excludes reopens across a rate or jurisdiction change (Q-1), which #14 covers. ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.3 (H17-1): while `B ≤ 0` both `in` dues are 0 and no `in` is accepted (every setup, sale and refund phases).

### 10.2 Payments, close, invoices

21. ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-2, H1-3): cash `amount` 430 000 tendered 500 000 → change 70 000 on **that payment's** shift; expected cash +430 000; two cash payments on two shifts each keep their own change. ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.2 (H16-3), ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.3 (H17-3): a cash amount above `cash_due` → `PAYMENT_EXCEEDS_BALANCE` (a cash `in` above the balance but ≤ `cash_due` is accepted).

Receipt `Doc`: number, lines, discount, service charge, one tax row per (tax label, rate, payment class) (⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-2)), rounding, total, payments, change, every seller tax registration, one line each with its label (⚠️ Changed in v0.7.2 (H9-5): two registrations → two lines) / header / footer per template, "FISCAL NUMBER PENDING" when fiscalized and not answered, drawer block only with cash. ⚠️ Changed in v0.8 (§25): the "Rounding adjustment" line is outside every tax row and no tax row changes with it; a card-only bill rounds too (Pakistan).

### 10.3 Reopen, remove comp, buyer ID

37. **H** reopen a closed 2-split bill → two `InvoiceCredited` (credit series 1, 2, each the full original), `OrdersUnbilled`, splits open with payments; re-close → new sale numbers; dine-in full edit writes `BillClosed` again (#37b), corrections writes none ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-25).
37b. **H** ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-10 + S-9, owner R2) dine-in full-edit reopen (seating NEEDS_CLEANING, no other bill): `SeatingReopened` (reaches the map and table-view sockets, ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-7); a PARTLY_PAID reopen writes none, H2-27), table ORDERED / FOOD_ON_TABLE, holder's `SubmitOrder` and `RemoveItems` pass the lock, re-close writes `BillClosed` → NEEDS_CLEANING again.
37c. **H** ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-10 + S-9, owner R2) dine-in reopen after `CleanTable` (seating CLOSED, table re-seated or free): mode `corrections`; void / comp / `RemoveItems` (no lock, `readOrder.seating_state`) work (⚠️ Changed in v1.0.7 (R-39): for the seating's holder or a `take-order-anywhere` covering the floor; anyone else → `403 PERMISSION_DENIED { reason: 'not_holder' }`, nothing written, 02a #67); `SubmitOrder` on that seating → `TABLE_SEATING_CHANGED` (⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-14)); re-close writes no `BillClosed`; the new seating on the table is unaffected.
37d. **H** ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-10 + S-9, owner R2) races: `CleanTable` then reopen → corrections; reopen then `CleanTable` → `TABLE_HAS_OPEN_ORDERS`; reopen then `OpenTable` → re-enters the reopened seating, a stale `seating_id` → `TABLE_SEATING_CHANGED` (⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-23)); reopen request raised before a clean, approved after → corrections.
37e. ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-10 + S-9, owner R2) S-9 (b): after `BillClosed`, a `SubmitOrder` on the uncleaned seating is accepted, the table re-occupies, a **new** bill opens with no staff action; its close writes `BillClosed` again; `OpenTable` / merge on NEEDS_CLEANING still refused.
37h. ⚠️ Changed in v0.5 (H3-2): reopen a PARTLY_PAID dine-in bill and a PARTLY_PAID takeaway bill → `mode: 'full'`, `bill_orders` holds every order, the bill keeps every line, no projected second bill appears.
37i. ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.7 (R-40 / G-37) **H** the billing fold sets `bills.reopen_mode` from `BillReopened.mode`: a fold of a `BillReopened` fixture with `mode: 'corrections'` → `reopen_mode = 'corrections'` and the bill is in neither `bills_live_seating` nor `bills_live_key` (a newer open bill on the same seating / key inserts, #37f); with `mode: 'full'` → `reopen_mode = 'full'` and the bill is in the index; a second `BillReopened` overwrites the mode; the fold reads no seating or key state (the mode comes only from the event); a rebuild from events gives the same `reopen_mode`; the `bill_reopened` audit row carries the event's mode; the `BillReopened` schema refuses a payload without `mode` or with another value.
37j. ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.15 (R-66) **H** a `ReopenBill` of a 2-split bill under `requires_approval` → the `reopen_bill` values hold `invoice_ids` and `credit_invoice_ids` of the same length, the ids distinct ULIDs; approve → each `InvoiceCredited.invoice_id` = `credit_invoice_ids[i]` for `ref_invoice_id` = `invoice_ids[i]`, and `apply` run twice on the same request (preRead, then the write) builds the same drafts; a sale invoice changed between raise and approve → the answer is refused and no credit is written; the direct path mints fresh ids.

### 10.5 Merges, splits of tables, printing

56. Merge with no payments: the absorbed bill folds into the survivor; a removed service charge on the survivor stays removed.

58. `BillRequested` → check bill at the zone's bill printer → floor → default; no 01d notice raised; a replayed queued `RequestBill` prints once.
59. Check bill `Doc`: banner, no invoice number / fiscal number / QR, default-class total, digital-rate line; `CheckBillPrinted` audit row with ids and counts.
60. **Print to:** a waiting / failed job → `RetargetBillPrint` moves it; a printed one → `PRINT_JOB_NOT_PENDING`; nothing prints twice.

76. Per-device `pos_id` mapping picks the device's `pos_id`; else the default (K-18).
77. `fiscal-status` and every log line of the worker contain no token, no buyer data (captured logger).
78. ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-8), ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-1): an original issued under `PK-PB` / `PRA` and credited after the branch switched back to `PK` → the credit is submitted through the original's adapter under the original's `pos_id`, with the original's jurisdiction, currency and tax rows; ⚠️ Changed in v0.7.2 (H9-6): the credit note and a reprint of the original show the original's "PRA invoice no." label and QR from the invoice snapshot after the switch. Switch `PK` → `PK-PB` by setting: earlier invoices stay `not_required`; later ones pending.
78b. ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-1, J-6): a fake adapter with `capabilities.credit = false` (or a test jurisdiction with `credit_support: 'none'`) → `ReopenBill` of a bill with a `pending` / `acked` invoice → `409 BILL_REOPEN_NOT_SUPPORTED`, nothing written; a `not_required` invoice on the same branch reopens; an approved `reopen_bill` re-checks it.

### 10.8 Platform rows and registrations

79. Row A-3 / T-1 / Q-3 lists: two guards registered, the first non-null wins; 04's results unchanged.
80. Row H-1: a `'both'` command answers on both routes with one `command_id` space.
81. Row H-2 / §6.28a (⚠️ Changed in v0.3, H1-19: `payload_hash` computed without `token`) ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-3): `SetFiscalToken` needs `manage-fiscal-settings` (Manager default refused, Owner allowed); the token is in `fiscal_credentials` only — not in the result, `processed_commands`, any event (`FiscalTokenSet` has no token), any log line (captured logger), `fiscal-status` (shows `set_at` only) or sync; replace overwrites; an unknown `pos_id` → `CONFIG_REFERENCE_INVALID`. ⚠️ Changed in v0.7.3 (H10-5): a second credential name for one `pos_id` is a second row; a `name` outside the adapters' `credentialNames` → `CONFIG_REFERENCE_INVALID`.
82. Row Q-1: `order_lines.tax_profile_id` folded from `ItemAdded.tax_class_id` (⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-2)); migration 0021 refolds `orders` (checkpoint deleted).
83. Row Q-2: `OrdersUnbilled` clears `billed_at`; edits accepted again.
83b. Row Q-5 (⚠️ Changed in v0.3, H1-11): a carried order keeps `day_closed_at = NULL` across the close and stays on open-orders; `OrdersUnbilled` clears `day_closed_at`.
84. Row Q-4: `LineCompRemoved` fold never below 0; cascades with `planCompRemoval`.
85. Row P-1: `billPrinterFor` order device → zone → floor → default; a route's printer cannot be archived.
86. Row P-3: QR block encodes `GS ( k` bytes (golden bytes); `renderText` prints `[QR] …`; row P-4 import from `@pos/domain` without `node:*` (lint).
87. Row V-3 (⚠️ Changed in v0.3, H1-8: request rows for the seven 05 kinds via `registerRequestAction`; registering after seal throws; the `0023` checkpoint delete makes rebuild = live with old `BillRequested` events): every 05 event in row V-3 maps to its audit action; rebuild of `audit` equals live; no buyer data in `detail_json`; ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.15 (R-65): a `money_request_raised` row has no `kind` in its detail and its kind is found by joining `audit_requests` on `alert_id`; a `money_request_answered` row's detail carries the kind.
88. Projections `billing` and `shifts`: rebuild from seq 0 equals live (every table, `fiscal_outbox` except worker columns).
88b. ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.7 (text clarification, R-41 / G-38) the `billing` fold of 02b `OrdersBilled { order_ids, bill_id }` fills `bill_orders` (one row per order, `INSERT OR IGNORE`, a repeat changes nothing); `OrdersBilled` is in the `billing` projection's `eventTypes`; rebuild equals live.
89. Kinds registered at module top level; a replayed 05 alert finds its kind at boot (01d rule).
90. ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-13): Manager (new tenant and backfill) holds neither `skip-blind-count` nor `manage-fiscal-settings`; a role with `close-shift-for-others` and no `manage-own-shift` → `422`. Row B-2: default grants for new tenants and the backfill by `default_key` (active roles only; approver rows; `directory_version` bump; idempotent re-run).
91. Every 05 query uses its stated fixed statement count for 1 and for 40 bills / lines (no N+1).
91b. ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.2 (H16-6): a `take-order`-only socket receives no `PaymentAdded`; a cashier's socket receives no other person's `ShiftCounted`; an event type of 05 without a registered audience fails a registration test.
91c. ⚠️ Changed in v1.0.7 (task 5 / integrator) event shapes pinned in §6.29: `BillSplitsSet.splits[]` — `items` lines `{ order_item_id, order_id, units }`, `amount` `share_minor`, `equal` the open split numbers only, `none` exactly one item (two refused); `DiscountApplied.replaces` boolean; `DiscountRemoved` refuses `alert_id` / requester / approver keys; `ShiftCountSkipped { shift_id }` only; `CheckBillPrinted.source` only `'manual'`; `InvoiceIssued.payment_class` accepts `'mixed'`; `InvoiceCredited` refuses `discount_line_ids`, needs `series: 'credit'`, `ref_invoice_id`, `ref_invoice_ref`, `alert_id`, and a credit built by `ReopenBill` has `closed_by` = the reopen's requester; `advance_staff_id` non-null on `PettyCashAdvanced` and `null` on `PettyCashOut` / `PettyCashIn`; `ZReportIssued.report.kind = 'z'` with `business_date` / `z_no` equal to the event's (a mismatch refused); `DiscountFrozen` and `ShiftCounted` as #16d, #50d; every 05 event has a builder and a fixture.

## §12. Definition of Done, checklist, questions, deferred

### 12.1 Definition of Done

1. Every §4.5 row applied with its marker, version and test; README R37 and row 05 updated.
2. Every §10 test exists with its number in its name and passes in the normal gate (`TMPDIR=/dev/shm …`); HIGH tasks reviewed in their own PR.
3. Every command: happy path, one test per code, retransmit, every grant state; every query: fixed statement count at 1 and 40.
4. No buyer ID, token, PIN, name or phone in any log line (captured-logger tests #43, #77).
5. Rebuild of `billing`, `shifts`, `audit` from seq 0 equals live.
6. Owner manual checks (§11 #46) done on the pilot hardware before go-live; go-live preconditions met or the branch on `PK` (⚠️ Changed in v0.8.2 (H16-9)).
